### [1] [Detecting and Explaining Fake News Short Videos with Multimodal Content and Real-World Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12678)
*Yifeng Luo,Yupeng Li,Ming Tang,Jianxiong Guo,Liang Lan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出NVKE-CEI，一个统一的假新闻视频检测与解释（FNVDE）系统，包含两个核心组件：NVKE（基于视觉与OCR文本相似度的时间变化来提取新闻视频关键帧）和CEI（结合内容信息与证据信息的假新闻事实核查框架，由基于内容和基于证据的两个LLM事实核查器组成，并通过轻量级法官模型融合输出）。实验证明该方法优于现有最优基线，并能生成高质量、有内容依据的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短视频平台已成为公众获取新闻的主要来源，但也导致了假新闻视频的广泛传播。现有FNVDE方法存在两个关键局限：一是常用的帧选择策略可能遗漏与真实性相关的线索，或缺乏理解新闻视频所需的足够时间上下文；二是先前方法忽视了多模态理解或证据检索。

Method: 提出NVKE-CEI统一系统：（1）NVKE关键帧提取方法，基于组合视觉相似度与OCR文本相似度的时间变化选择关键帧；（2）CEI框架，采用两个专用的基于LLM的事实核查器——基于内容的和基于证据的，其输出由轻量级法官模型进行融合。

Result: 大量实验表明NVKE-CEI优于现有最优（state-of-the-art）基线方法，同时能生成高质量的、基于内容解释（content-grounded explanations）的结果。

Conclusion: 通过融合改进的关键帧提取策略与内容-证据双通道LLM核查及轻量级融合机制，NVKE-CEI同时解决了帧选择和时间上下文不足、以及多模态理解与证据检索缺失的问题，为假新闻视频检测与可解释性提供了统一且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Short-video platforms have become a primary news source for the public, which has also enabled the widespread dissemination of fake news videos. We study the task of fake news video detection and explanation (FNVDE). Existing methods face two critical limitations. First, commonly used frame selection strategies may omit veracity-relevant cues or provide insufficient temporal context for understanding news videos. Second, prior methods neglect either multimodal understanding or evidence retrieval. To address these limitations, we propose NVKE-CEI, a unified system that integrates a news video keyframes extraction method (NVKE) and an FNVDE framework leveraging both content and evidence information (CEI). NVKE selects keyframes based on chronological changes in combined visual and OCR-text similarity. CEI employs two specialized LLM-based fact checkers (content-based and evidence-based) whose outputs are fused by a lightweight judge model. Extensive experiments show that NVKE-CEI outperforms state-of-the-art baselines while generating high-quality content-grounded explanations.

</details>


### [2] [Feature Recovery for Object Understanding After Irreversible Fire Damage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12078)
*Aditi Tiwari,Sofia Stoica,Savya Khosla,David Forsyth,Heng Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE基准，用于火灾后物体理解，包含21.4K真实图像基础的合成场景和物体级退化序列。现有模型在严重退化下性能大幅下降，作者提出特征恢复模块（FRM），通过将退化特征映射到原始对齐表示，在保持主机冻结的情况下显著提升检测和多种VLM任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 火灾后物体经历不可逆的物理变化，改变其几何、材料和外观。识别这些残余物对定位危险、重建灾前内容和损失清点至关重要。现有图像退化模型不适用于物理结构变化，缺乏针对性基准。

Method: 构建TRACE基准（21.4K场景、499个物体身份、189个类别，含5个任务）。提出FRM模块，一种即插即用的特征恢复模块，将退化编码器特征映射到原始对齐表示，保持主机模型冻结，仅用配对特征监督训练。

Result: FRM显著改善场景级检测、CLIP/SigLIP2特征恢复及四个物体级VLM任务。在更严重退化下增益更大，跨VLM主机和严重度，检索相对增益平均12.5%，材料恢复20.1%，描述生成13.2%，功能推理12.4%。对比基线，RF-DETR mAP相对下降71%，InternVL3.5检索R@1从93.85降至28.11。

Conclusion: FRM能有效恢复退化特征，提升火灾后物体理解能力，且即插即用、无需修改主机模型，为现实场景中的灾后分析提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Objects in post-fire environments often undergo irreversible physical transformations that change their geometry, material state, and visual appearance. Detecting and identifying these remnants is critical for locating hazards, reconstructing pre-incident contents, and inventorying losses. Unlike standard image corruptions, these degradations affect the physical structure of the object itself. To study this setting, we introduce TRACE, a transformation-aware benchmark for post-fire object understanding. TRACE contains 21.4K real-image-grounded synthetic scenes and paired object-level pristine-to-degraded progressions spanning 499 object identities across 189 categories. We define five tasks targeting localization and pre-degradation understanding: degraded-object detection, pristine-state recovery and retrieval, original material recovery, pristine description generation, and functional reasoning. Existing models degrade sharply with severity. From the least to the most severe level, RF-DETR mAP decreases by 71% relative, while InternVL3.5 retrieval R@1 falls from 93.85 to 28.11. To address this, we propose the Feature Recovery Module (FRM), a plug-and-play module that maps degraded encoder features to pristine-aligned representations while keeping the host frozen. Trained only with paired feature supervision, FRM improves scene-level detection, CLIP/SigLIP2 feature recovery, and all four object-level VLM tasks, with larger gains under more severe degradation. Across VLM hosts and severity levels, relative gains average 12.5% for retrieval, 20.1% for material recovery, 13.2% for description generation, and 12.4% for functional reasoning.

</details>


### [3] [Does Video Memory Use What It Retrieves? A Causal Audit of Memory Specificity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12090)
*Aditi Tiwari,Akshit Bhalla,Darshan Prasad,Heng Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"读时记忆替换"(read-time memory substitution)方法，通过替换模型消费的记忆值但保持其余计算不变，来区分记忆带来的收益究竟是来自检索到的具体内容（记忆特异性），还是来自通用的表示支持。实验表明：在冻结的视频世界模型上，不含评估特定内容的身份无关控制恢复了几乎全部收益（Ego-Exo4D和7-Scenes完全恢复，TUM约70%），支持"表示修复"假说；WorldMem呈现梯度依赖；SAM 2则表现出强烈的内容依赖。该方法能直接区分记忆收益依赖的是通用表示支撑、更宽泛上下文还是精确的情节内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频模型越来越多地使用记忆来在长序列中保留信息，其隐含假设是收益来自检索并利用正确的历史内容。但标准记忆消融实验只能验证"记忆是否有帮助"，无法验证"检索到的内容是否起决定作用"。因此作者希望设计一种能区分"记忆有益"与"记忆特异性"的测试方法。

Method: 提出"读时记忆替换"（read-time memory substitution）方法：在推理时将模型实际消费的记忆值替换为其他内容，同时保持计算流程其余部分不变，从而将"记忆带来的总体收益"从"对检索内容的依赖程度（记忆特异性）"中分离出来。通过在不同模型（冻结视频世界模型、WorldMem、SAM 2）和数据集（Ego-Exo4D、7-Scenes、TUM、DAVIS、MOSEv2）上使用身份无关控制、同轨迹错误记忆、跨轨迹/生物力学供体等多种替换方案进行剂量-反应实验。

Result: 冻结视频世界模型中，不含评估特定内容的身份无关控制恢复到几乎全部收益（Ego-Exo4D和7-Scenes为102%和完全恢复，TUM约70%）。在Ego-Exo4D剂量-反应实验中，当替换值偏离训练记忆表示时，恢复率从102%降至1%，支持"表示修复"作为最有力解释。WorldMem呈现梯度依赖：同轨迹错误记忆恢复94.1%的PSNR收益，而来自无关轨迹和生物力学的供体仅恢复43.7%。SAM 2表现强烈内容依赖：在DAVIS上，正确空间记忆换成有效错误记忆使均值和边界分数从0.926降至0.182；在MOSEv2重现时从0.459降至0.000。

Conclusion: 记忆收益可能依赖三种不同因素：通用表示支撑（representation repair）、更宽泛的上下文、或精确的情节内容。不同模型/任务体现不同的依赖类型。读时记忆替换提供了一种直接区分这些机制的方法，为理解长序列视频模型的记忆机制提供了更精确的诊断工具。

Abstract: Video models increasingly use memory to preserve information over long sequences, with the assumption that gains come from retrieving and using the correct past content. Standard memory ablations test whether memory helps, but not whether the retrieved content is responsible. We test this directly with read-time memory substitution, which replaces the consumed memory value while leaving the rest of the computation unchanged. This separates memory benefit from memory specificity, the extent to which the gain depends on retrieved content. Across frozen video world models, identity-free controls containing no evaluation-specific content recover essentially the full benefit on Ego-Exo4D and 7-Scenes and about 70% on TUM. In the Ego-Exo4D dose response, recovery falls from 102% to 1% as these values move away from observed training-memory representations, supporting representation repair as the best-supported explanation in this setting. WorldMem shows graded dependence. A wrong memory from the same trajectory recovers 94.1% of the PSNR benefit relative to zero content, while a donor from a disjoint trajectory and biome recovers 43.7%. SAM 2 shows strong content dependence. On DAVIS, replacing the correct spatial memory with a valid wrong memory reduces mean region and boundary score from 0.926 to 0.182. At MOSEv2 reappearance, it falls from 0.459 to 0.000. These results show that memory gains can depend on generic representation support, broader context, or exact episodic content. Read-time substitution provides a direct way to distinguish them.

</details>


### [4] [Beyond Argmax: A Mechanistic Study of Semantic Retention in Frozen Foundation-Model Composition for Generalized Few-Shot 3D Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12099)
*Silas Kwabla Gah,Ebenezer Owusu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文在冻结的异构基础模型组合做少样本三维分割的场景下，重新审视了经典分类器组合中“分数级融合”与“硬决策投票”的区分，并通过“同输入语义保留干预”（保留top-k语义候选项）证明：过早将异构来源坍缩到单一类别会形成可重复的信息瓶颈。保留完整分布比top-1融合在两大数据集上显著提升约3.5~6.4个点HM IoU，结论对稀疏源权重、融合算子、源替换诊断均稳健，且校准修正不能消除该优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机源于经典分类器组合中分数级融合与决策级投票的区分，但该区分在冻结基础模型推理时组合的背景下尚未被重新审视。核心问题是：当异构（dense RegionPLC与sparse SAM3）的预训练冻结模型在推理时组合时，若过早将多源信息坍缩为单一类别，会丢失多少有用的语义信息？作者希望对此给出受控的、量化的诊断。

Method: 方法为“同输入语义保留干预”（same-input semantic-retention intervention）。冻结所有组件——稠密RegionPLC与稀疏跨视角SAM3的证据、模型权重、掩码、几何、词汇表与融合规则均不变，仅通过匹配的top-k阶梯改变交互前保留的语义候选项数量（top-1至完整分布），以此隔离“语义坍缩”这一变量的效应。并在ScanNet200（156个保留场景）与ScanNet++（50场景）上评测HM IoU，辅以稀疏源权重扫描、替代算子（max、几何池化）、GroundingDINO–SAM2.1源替换诊断及校准诊断。

Result: ScanNet200上top-1为28.47 HM IoU，保留完整分布为34.87（+6.40，95% CI [+5.24,+7.64]）；ScanNet++上23.02 vs 26.50（+3.48，95% CI [+1.64,+5.93]）。结果稳健：完整分布HM在稀疏源权重0.3–0.7上稳定；max与几何池化等替代算子同样优于top-1；GroundingDINO–SAM2.1源替换中完整保留HM从14.77单调升至18.75。校准诊断显示两源误校准方向相反，但修正校准不能消除保留优势。跨数据集与源栈，大多数信息可由保留紧凑的可行备选集恢复。

Conclusion: 结论是：在异构冻结模型组合中，过早的语义坍缩（premature semantic collapse）是一个可重复的信息瓶颈；保留完整分布（或一组紧凑的可行候选项）可显著且稳健地提升少样本三维分割性能。本文的贡献是对此进行受控诊断，揭示该瓶颈在异构冻结模型组合中的普适性与可修复性。

Abstract: Classical classifier-combination work distinguishes score-level fusion from hard decision-level voting. We revisit this distinction where independently pretrained, frozen foundation models are composed at inference time for generalized few-shot 3D segmentation. We ask: how much useful semantic information is lost when heterogeneous sources are collapsed to a single class before they can interact?
  We answer with a same-input semantic-retention intervention. Dense RegionPLC and sparse cross-view SAM3 evidence, model weights, masks, geometry, vocabularies, and fusion rules are frozen; only the number of semantic alternatives retained before interaction is varied via a matched top-k ladder. On 156 held-out ScanNet200 scenes, top-1 reaches 28.47 harmonic-mean (HM) IoU while full distribution fusion reaches 34.87 HM (+6.40, 95% CI [+5.24,+7.64]). The pattern replicates on 50 ScanNet++ scenes: 23.02 vs. 26.50 HM (+3.48, 95% CI [+1.64,+5.93]).
  The conclusion is robust: full-distribution HM is stable across sparse-source weights 0.3--0.7; alternative operators (max, geometric pooling) also outperform top-1; and a GroundingDINO--SAM2.1 source-replacement diagnostic shows monotonic HM increase from 14.77 to 18.75 with full retention. Calibration diagnostics reveal opposite miscalibration of the two sources, yet correcting calibration does not eliminate the retention advantage.
  Across datasets and source stacks, most information is recovered by retaining a compact set of plausible alternatives. The contribution is a controlled diagnosis of premature semantic collapse as a repeatable information bottleneck in heterogeneous frozen-model composition.

</details>


### [5] [HSI-Road Relabeled: Surface-Aware Road-Scene Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12151)
*Imad Ali Shah,Imran Mehmood,Enda Ward,Martin Glavin,Edward Jones,Brian Deegan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍HSI-Road数据集，提出六类地表语义标签体系（背景、沥青、混凝土、泥土、水体、草地）及RGB-NIR配准流程，并用六种语义分割模型在四种输入配置下评测，结果表明原始分辨率RGB性能最优（但像素量是配准输入的12倍），而匹配分辨率下RGB-NIR通道堆叠优于NIR和配准RGB，说明空间分辨率重要且NIR提供互补信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HSI-Road数据集仅提供成对的RGB与25通道NIR图像及二值掩膜，缺少地表层面的类别标签，限制了其在路面/地表语义分割任务中的应用与多模态研究。本文旨在通过人工标注标签、构建RGB-NIR配准流程，并系统对比不同输入模态与分辨率下的分割性能，以量化空间分辨率影响并评估NIR的互补价值。

Method: 作者人工标注了六类语义标签（Background、Asphalt、Concrete、Dirt、Water、Grass），并搭建了RGB到NIR的配准管线及其相应标注。随后在四种输入配置下评估六种语义分割模型：原始分辨率RGB（RGB_ori）、配准的低分辨率RGB（RGB_reg）、NIR、以及通道堆叠的RGB_reg-NIR（RGBN_stk），采用逐类及平均IoU和F1指标量化空间分辨率缩减对RGB的影响，并评估NIR和RGBN_stk的表现。

Result: RGB_ori在整体性能上取得最高分数，但其像素数量是匹配分辨率输入的12倍；在匹配的192×384分辨率下，RGBN_stk对全部六种分割模型均优于NIR，且对五种模型优于RGB_reg，其中Water类别的增益最为一致。这些结果凸显了空间分辨率的重要性，同时表明NIR能为RGB提供互补信息。

Conclusion: 结论认为，空间分辨率对语义分割性能影响显著，配准导致的分辨率下降会带来性能损失；但在同分辨率条件下，NIR与RGB的通道堆叠融合能有效提升分割效果，尤其对Water类有利，证明NIR对RGB具有互补性，为多模态地表分割提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The HSI-Road dataset provides paired RGB and 25-channel NIR (600--960~nm) images with binary masks but no surface-level labels.~This paper introduces a manually labeled six-class taxonomy: Background, Asphalt, Concrete, Dirt, Water, and Grass, and an RGB-to-NIR registration pipeline with corresponding annotations. Six semantic-segmentation models (SSMs) are evaluated under four input configurations: original-resolution RGB (RGB$_{\text{ori}}$), registered low-resolution RGB (RGB$_{\text{reg}}$), NIR, and channel-stacked RGB$_{\text{reg}}$--NIR (RGBN$_{\text{stk}}$). The comparison quantifies the effect of spatial-resolution reduction on RGB, along with evaluation of NIR and RGBN$_{\text{stk}}$, with results reported using per-class and mean IoU and F1 scores. RGB$_{\text{ori}}$ achieves the highest overall performance but contains 12$\times$ more pixels than the matched-resolution inputs. At the matched 192$\times$384 resolution, RGBN$_{\text{stk}}$ outperforms NIR for all six SSMs and RGB$_{\text{reg}}$ for five of six, with the most consistent gains for the Water class. These results highlight the importance of spatial resolution while showing that NIR provides complementary information to RGB.

</details>


### [6] [Single-Query Person-Centric Bimanual Hand-Object Interaction Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12155)
*Jonghyun Kim,Junho Roh,Yubin Yoon,Hyotae Lee,Jongkuk Park,Taehwan Hwang,Jaechul Kim,Jungho Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种以人为中心（person-centric）的双手交互解析框架，将人的检测、姿态、双手状态与交互目标统一到一个query结构化输出中，并通过部分感知变形注意力与手到查询关系矩阵实现端到端推理，显著提升多人体场景下的双手交互解析性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手-物体交互方法大多是手中心（hand-centric）的，将每只手视为独立实例，在多人体场景中无法准确判断两只手是否属于同一个人，导致归属模糊，无法完整解析人物层面的双手交互。

Method: 提出person-centric公式：单个query预测一个人的结构化输出（人体框、人体姿态、手框与状态、交互目标）。引入部分感知变形注意力（part-aware deformable attention）在人体、手、姿态特定参考区域分配注意力，使一个query捕获完整人体结构；通过手到查询关系矩阵（hand-to-query relationship matrix）统一检测与交互推理，每只手从检测到的query集合加一个可学习的off token中选择交互目标，无需单独的对象回归即可恢复目标的框和类别。

Result: 构建了基于COCO的person-centric双手交互标注数据集，定义了评估手状态与完整手-物体元组的结构化指标；基于transformer检测器的实验表明该公式改进了人物级双手交互解析，并提供了联合检测、姿态估计与手推理的统一高效框架。

Conclusion: person-centric的视角配合统一的结构化输出与关系推理，能有效解决多人体场景中手的归属模糊问题，为双手交互解析提供了一个兼具检测、姿态估计与手推理的统一框架。

Abstract: Understanding person-level bi-manual interactions requires not only detecting hands, but also identifying which two hands belong to the same person and what each hand interacts with. Existing hand--object interaction methods are mostly hand-centric: they treat each hand as an independent instance, which can lead to ambiguous ownership in multi-person scenes.
  We propose a person-centric formulation in which a single query predicts a structured output for one person, including the human box, body pose, hand boxes and states, and interaction targets. We introduce part-aware deformable attention to allocate attention across human, hand, and pose-specific reference regions, enabling one query to capture the full person structure. We further unify detection and interaction reasoning with a hand-to-query relationship matrix, where each hand selects its interaction target from the detected query set plus a learnable off token, directly recovering the target's box and class without separate object regression.
  We build a COCO-based dataset with person-centric bi-manual interaction annotations and define structured metrics for evaluating hand states and complete hand--object tuples. Experiments with a transformer-based detector show that our formulation improves person-level bi-manual interaction parsing and provides an effective unified framework for joint detection, pose estimation, and hand reasoning.

</details>


### [7] [USPLIT-VQA: U-Shaped Split Learning for Visual Question Answering with Contribution-Aware Weighted Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12168)
*Md Khalid Syfullah,Alvi Ataur Khalil*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出USPLIT-VQA，一种U型分割学习框架，用于隐私保护的视觉问答，客户端保留浅层和分类头，服务端托管计算密集的中间层，并引入CAWA聚合机制抵御恶意客户端，在四个VQA数据集上验证了准确率、内存和通信开销的优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集中式训练需要汇聚所有数据，联邦学习要求每个客户端托管完整模型，两者均难以兼顾隐私和资源限制。视觉问答系统涉及用户敏感数据，需要一种在不暴露原始输入和标签的前提下进行协作训练的机制。

Method: 采用U形分割学习架构：客户端保留模型初始层和分类头，服务端托管计算量大的中间层，从而避免原始数据和标签离开客户端；同时设计贡献感知加权聚合（CAWA），基于梯度相似性评估客户端贡献，降低恶意更新的影响。

Result: 在VQA-RAD、SLAKE、PathVQA和VizWiz四个数据集上，使用两个骨干网络进行实验：相比联邦学习，Custom模型获得准确率提升，BiomedCLIP在固定分割下准确率下降；客户端内存减少最高5.8倍，通信减少最高10.8倍；在单个恶意客户端场景下CAWA将攻击者影响力降低98%以上，但在更高破坏水平下暴露局限；重建攻击实验显示图像反演质量降低。

Conclusion: USPLIT-VQA在隐私保护、资源效率和鲁棒性方面具有潜力，但存在模型适配和恶意客户端比例增大的局限性，需要进一步优化分割策略和聚合机制。

Abstract: Visual Question Answering (VQA) systems, jointly interpreting images and natural language queries, hold significant promise across many domains, yet the privacy-sensitive nature of user data creates a fundamental barrier. Centralized training requires access to all data, while federated learning requires each client to host the full model. We propose USPLIT-VQA, a U-shaped split learning framework for privacy-preserving VQA in which each client retains the initial layers and the classification head while the server hosts the computationally heavy intermediate layers, keeping raw inputs and labels on the client device. We further introduce Contribution-Aware Weighted Aggregation (CAWA), a gradientsimilarity-based client scoring mechanism designed to reduce the influence of malicious updates. Experiments on four VQA datasets (VQA-RAD, SLAKE, PathVQA, and VizWiz) with two backbones show accuracy gains over Federated Learning for the Custom model and reduced accuracy for BiomedCLIP under the evaluated fixed split, alongside client memory reductions of up to 5.8X and communication reductions of up to 10.8X. With one malicious client, CAWA reduces the attacker's influence by over 98%, while experiments at higher corruption levels identify its limitations. Reconstruction experiments further show lower inversion quality under the evaluated attacks.

</details>


### [8] [When Ground-Truth Fidelity Matters: An Orchestrated UAS Framework for Wheat Streak Mosaic Virus Detection Using Vision Transformers and Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12169)
*Dewi Endah Kharismawati,Sandeep Dhakal,Courtney E. McCusker,Jennifer R. Wilson,Erik W. Ohlson,Sami Khanal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出一个基于无人机(UAS)多光谱影像的自动化管道用于小麦条纹花叶病毒(WSMV)检测，集成正射镶嵌重建、地理空间对齐、植物提取与七通道输入的Vision Transformer分类器。尽管基于处理标签在6500多个测试块上达到89%准确率，但ELISA地面真值验证表明该高准确率主要由标签偏差驱动而非真实的疾病检测能力，在处理标签噪声大、感染率低的情况下，模型对行级症状严重度和植物级ELISA标签的性能显著下降，揭示UAS病害检测受标签保真度与数据规模的根本制约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WSMV是甜玉米等谷类作物的毁灭性病原体，其症状在空间上多变且细微，早期检测困难，在甜玉米种子生产中具有监管重要性（新西兰、智利等国的植物检疫法规要求种子批次无病毒）。视觉巡检因症状类似非生物胁迫而不可靠，ELISA虽准确但昂贵、劳动密集、难以规模化，因此需要一种可扩展且高效的自动化检测方案。

Method: 构建自动化检测管道，包括：(1) UAS多光谱影像的正射镶嵌重建；(2) 地理空间对齐；(3) 植物提取；(4) 使用Vision Transformer进行分类，输入为7个通道（5个光谱波段加NDVI和NDRE）。初始以基于处理组（接种vs mock接种）的标签进行训练与验证。

Result: 基于处理标签，模型在跨多个生长阶段的6500+测试块上达到89%准确率。然而，ELISA地面真值揭示了显著的标签噪声：接种图内仅有很小比例的采样植物实际被感染。处理标签无法可靠表征感染状态，高准确率主要由标签偏差（处理组间光谱差异）驱动而非疾病检测。当以行级症状严重度和植物级ELISA标签作为更高保真度但样本量更小的标签时，性能显著下降；深度学习和经典机器学习均表现出有限的泛化能力和弱可分离性（ELISA确认的mock接种与感染植物难以区分）。

Conclusion: 研究证实UAS-based病害检测的瓶颈并非传感器或模型架构，而是标签保真度与数据可用性。基于处理组的粗粒度标签会引入系统性偏差，掩盖算法的真实判别能力。未来工作必须强调生物学上有根据的、基于真实感染状态的标签，并构建与实际田间条件对齐的模型，方能实现可靠的自动化植物疫病检测。

Abstract: Wheat streak mosaic virus (WSMV) is a destructive pathogen of sweet corn and other cereal crops, causing yield losses and complicating early detection because symptoms are spatially variable and subtle. In sweet corn seed production, WSMV also has regulatory importance, as phytosanitary regulations from countries such as New Zealand and Chile require seed lots to be certified virus-free. Visual scouting is unreliable because symptoms can resemble abiotic stress, while enzyme-linked immunosorbent assay (ELISA) is accurate but expensive, labor-intensive, and difficult to scale. We present an automated pipeline for plant-level WSMV detection using unmanned aircraft systems (UAS) multispectral imagery. The framework integrates orthomosaic reconstruction, geospatial alignment, plant extraction, and classification using a Vision Transformer with seven-channel inputs (five spectral bands, NDVI, and NDRE). Using treatment-based labels, the model achieved 89% accuracy on over 6,500 test patches across multiple growth stages. However, ELISA-based ground truth revealed substantial label noise: only a small fraction of sampled plants in inoculated plots were infected. Treatment labels therefore did not reliably represent infection status, and the high accuracy was largely driven by label bias rather than disease detection. Performance decreased markedly against row-level symptom severity and plant-level ELISA labels. Under these higher-fidelity but smaller-sample conditions, both deep learning and classical machine learning showed limited generalization and weak separability between ELISA-confirmed mock-inoculated and infected plants. These results show that UAS-based disease detection is constrained by label fidelity and data availability, emphasizing biologically grounded labels and models aligned with real-world conditions.

</details>


### [9] [Physics as the label for measuring and correcting materials reasoning in multimodal models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12181)
*Hasan Kurban,Rasul Khanbayov,Mustafa Kurban*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MatPCR是一个无标注基准，利用程序化oracle（Bragg定律、比例尺、光谱峰、Materials Project物理检查等）自动验证材料推理链的物理一致性，并引入基于约束的自验证代理循环与开放验证器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态与语言模型在解释材料数据时存在幻觉和违反物理定律的问题，但评估仅将最终答案与稀缺的人工标签匹配，或验证最终提议/DFT执行，均无法衡量模型推理链的物理一致性。而材料数据本身携带物理规律，使得大量材料推理无需人工标注即可验证。

Method: 提出MatPCR基准，通过程序化oracle检查：Bragg定律(衍射几何)、比例尺、光谱峰、以及基于Materials Project的近稳态能量、计算带隙类别和净磁化。定义物理一致性率作为评估指标；提出约束接地自验证(Constraint-Grounded Self-Verification)代理循环。

Result: 该自验证循环的增益在自精炼和相同计算量的重新提示控制下依然保持；开放的验证器在分布内有用，但在全部六种保留约束类型上接近随机水平；并推导出oracle误差如何精确影响所报告物理一致性率的身份关系(identity)。

Conclusion: MatPCR提供了一种无需标注、利用材料固有物理规律验证推理链一致性的评估范式，并揭示代理验证循环能带来持续增益，且开放验证器存在分布外泛化限制。

Abstract: Vision-language and language models increasingly interpret materials data, yet benchmarks report that they hallucinate invalid properties and violate physical law. Evaluation matches final answers to scarce human labels, while discovery agents verify final proposals or density functional theory (DFT) execution. Neither measures the physical consistency of a model's reasoning chain. Materials data carries its own physics, making a large class of materials reasoning verifiable without annotation. We introduce MatPCR, a label-free benchmark whose programmatic oracles check diffraction geometry through Bragg's law, scale bars, spectral peaks, and Materials Project-grounded checks of near-hull stability, computed band-gap class, and net magnetization. We define the Physical-Consistency Rate over image and structure inputs; introduce Constraint-Grounded Self-Verification, an agentic loop whose gain survives self-refinement and equal-compute re-prompting controls; release an open verifier useful in distribution but near chance on all six held-out constraint types; and derive an exact identity for how oracle error displaces the reported rate.

</details>


### [10] [QuPAINT: Physics-Aware Multimodal Reasoning for Quantum Material Characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12202)
*Sankalp Pandey,Xuan-Bac Nguyen,Hoang-Quan Nguyen,Tim Faltermeier,Nicholas Borys,Hugh Churchill,Khoa Luu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出QuPAINT，一个物理感知的多模态框架，用于二维量子材料的转移式薄片表征。它通过合成数据框架Synthia生成保留层依赖光学行为的多样化合成显微图像，构建多模态指令数据集QMat-Instruct，并利用Physics-Informed Attention (PIA)注入基底相对光学先验来实现接地多模态推理。同时引入迄今最大的真实基准QF-Bench，覆盖多种显微与基底条件，验证其计数、视觉接地、推理质量、校准及对未知材料的迁移能力。QuPAINT-8B大幅超越现有方法，在一般性和单层薄片检测上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维量子材料的光学显微表征需要定位剥离薄片并根据微妙的光学对比度和干涉色判定层厚，以筛选合适器件用薄片。然而现有模型面临合成到真实的域偏移，以及不同材料、基底、实验室和成像条件之间的巨大差异，导致模型迁移能力不足，难以广泛适用。

Method: 提出三部分组成的框架：(1) Synthia，一个合成材料框架，可生成多样化合成显微图像同时保留层依赖光学行为；(2) QMat-Instruct，一个基于验证注释并约束到可观测光学线索、带图像特定推理轨迹的多模态指令数据集；(3) 通过Physics-Informed Attention (PIA)将基底相对光学先验注入视觉表征，支持接地多模态推理；并构建真实基准QF-Bench用于评估。

Result: QuPAINT-8B在计数、视觉接地、推理质量和置信度校准上大幅超越既有方法，在一般薄片检测和单层薄片检测上均建立了SOTA性能。进一步实验表明，图像接地监督能够改善严格的空间接地和置信度校准，同时保持对未见材料稳健的一般薄片检测能力。

Conclusion: 物理感知、图像接地监督的多模态方法能有效克服合成到真实的域偏移和跨材料/基底/成像条件的变化，实现对二维量子材料薄片的高精度可迁移表征，为材料科学与视觉语言模型的交叉应用提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Characterizing two-dimensional (2D) quantum materials by optical microscopy requires localizing exfoliated flakes and determining their layer thickness from subtle optical contrast and interference color to select suitable flakes for device fabrication. However, models face synthetic-to-real domain shifts and variation across materials, substrates, laboratories, and imaging conditions. We present QuPAINT, a physics-aware multimodal framework for transferable quantum flake characterization. The Synthetic Materials Framework (Synthia) generates diverse synthetic microscopy images while preserving layer-dependent optical behavior. Using these images, we construct QMat-Instruct, a multimodal instruction dataset with image-specific reasoning traces generated from verified annotations and constrained to observable optical cues. QuPAINT integrates these signals through Physics-Informed Attention (PIA), which injects substrate-relative optical priors into the visual representation to support grounded multimodal reasoning. For evaluation, we introduce QF-Bench, to our knowledge, the largest real-world benchmark for this problem, spanning diverse microscopy and substrate conditions. Using its verified annotations, we study counting, visual grounding, reasoning quality, confidence calibration, and transfer to an unseen material. QuPAINT-8B substantially outperforms prior methods and establishes state-of-the-art performance for both general and monolayer flake detection. Additional experiments show that image-grounded supervision improves strict spatial grounding and confidence calibration while preserving robust general flake detection on the unseen material.

</details>


### [11] [UniMo: Unifying Human and Animal Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12342)
*Zeyu Zhang,Zhiyuan Zhang,Siheng Wang,Yiran Wang,Danning Li,Ian Reid,Richard Hartley*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UniMo，一种基于统一点云表示的三维运动生成框架，通过将参数化骨架转换为非参数化表示以消除物种间拓扑差异，并引入动态采样突出活跃关节；同时构建了大规模运动-语言数据集UniML3D（涵盖人类与动物，规模为现有动物数据集的102倍以上）。该方法在UniML3D以及HumanML3D、KIT-ML、AnimalML3D等公开基准上均取得最优性能，验证了跨物种统一运动生成的可行性与有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本驱动的3D运动生成应用广泛，但现有方法主要针对标准人体结构，难以扩展到动物领域。核心痛点有二：其一，动物骨骼拓扑多样性极高，无法统一建模，导致需要为每个物种单独训练低效模型；其二，现有动物运动数据集规模小、标注质量差，严重制约模型性能。

Method: 针对上述痛点，作者提出UniMo框架：1) 采用统一的点云表示，将参数化骨架转换为非参数化表示，从根本上绕过拓扑差异问题；2) 设计动态采样策略，为活跃关节分配更多点以强化关键物理信息。同时构建UniML3D大规模数据集，包含145,907条运动序列与433,388条文本标注，覆盖人类与动物类别。

Result: UniMo在自建的UniML3D基准以及HumanML3D、KIT-ML、AnimalML3D三个公开基准上均达到了当前最优（SOTA）水平，验证了统一人类-动物运动生成方案的可行性与有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作证明，通过非参数化点云表示结合大规模高质量数据，可以突破骨骼拓扑差异与数据稀缺的双重瓶颈，实现跨物种的统一运动生成，为机器人、AR/VR、游戏及内容创作等领域提供普适性的运动生成基础。

Abstract: The conditional generation of 3D motion has emerged as a key research topic due to its wide applicability across robotics, AR/VR, gaming, and content creation. However, extending recent advances in text-driven human motion generation to the animal domain remains challenging due to two core limitations. First, animals exhibit highly diverse skeletal topologies, unlike the standard human structure, making unified modeling across species difficult and leading to inefficient per-species models. Second, existing animal motion datasets suffer from limited scale and annotation quality, constraining model performance. To address these challenges, we propose UniMo, a unified point cloud-based motion generation framework that bypasses topological discrepancies by converting parametric skeletons into unparametric representations, further enhanced by dynamic sampling that allocates more points to active joints. Additionally, we present UniML3D, a large-scale motion-language dataset spanning both human and animal categories, containing 145,907 motion sequences and 433,388 captions-over 102x larger than existing animal datasets. Our method achieves state-of-the-art results on UniML3D and three public benchmarks including HumanML3D, KIT-ML, and AnimalML3D, demonstrating the feasibility and effectiveness of unified human-animal motion generation. Website: https://steve-zeyu-zhang.github.io/UniMo.

</details>


### [12] [VS-Splat: Voxel-Selective feed-forward Gaussian Splatting for end-to-end 3D object reconstruction from sparse-views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12343)
*Yunsu Jeong,Hyuk Heo,Youngsang Kwak,Jaehwa Kwak,Il Yong Chun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VS-Splat，一种新的端到端前馈高斯泼溅框架，通过可学习的体素选择方法仅在与物体相关的体素内预测高斯图元，无需3D结构监督，在三个基准数据集上的稀疏视角渲染实验中优于多种最先进方法，并可作为现有稠密化方法的主干提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈高斯泼溅模型在整个3D空间中均匀预测高斯图元，导致大量图元位于非物体区域，阻碍了对物体细节的精细表示，因此需要一种仅聚焦于物体区域的高斯图元预测方法。

Method: 提出可学习的体素选择方法，仅通过2D渲染监督识别物体中心体素，无需3D结构监督，并在此选中体素内预测高斯图元。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的稀疏视角渲染实验中，VS-Splat优于多种最先进方法；作为现有稠密化方法的主干时也表现有效，可选扩展还能提升对不准确相机姿态估计的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: VS-Splat通过体素选择性高斯预测有效提升了稀疏视角3D重建的精度与细节表示，同时具备作为通用主干的可扩展性和对相机噪声的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Feed-forward Gaussian splatting models have demonstrated remarkable effectiveness in reconstructing three-dimensional (3D) objects from a few two-dimensional (2D) images, even if they are unseen. As existing methods typically predict Gaussian primitives uniformly across the 3D space, most primitives are placed in non-object regions. This may hinder the representation of fine object details. This paper proposes a Voxel-Selective Gaussian Splatting model (VS-Splat), a new end-to-endfeed-forward Gaussian splatting framework that predicts many primitives only within selected voxels that are likely to belong to an object, without 3D structural supervision. To achieve this, we propose a new learnable voxel selection approach that identifies object-centric voxels only with 2D rendering supervision.Our sparse-view rendering experiments with three benchmark datasets show that proposed VS-Splat outperforms several state-of-the-art methods. We further demonstrate its effectiveness as a backbone for an existing densification method and show that anoptional extension improves its robustness to inaccurate camera pose estimates.

</details>


### [13] [EgoMaize: A First-Person Maize Instance Segmentation Benchmark under Severe Field Occlusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12350)
*Jiayi Li,Zihan Zhang,Erhankang Yan,Yitian Chen,Yuze Li,Chengzhang Ding,Jianxin Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EgoMaize是一个用于第一人称（近距离）玉米实例分割的基准数据集。该任务要求从近距离田间图像中预测一致的植株掩膜以及植株所属的茎/雄穗线索。由于玉米植株存在严重的同类遮挡，现有可见标签会破坏同一物理植株的连通性，而全模态标签则难以验证。EgoMaize采用基于证据封闭的标注流程处理遮挡区域，并将不可靠区域标记为忽略而非背景。基准结果显示，预训练查询分组、边界细化和高分辨率裁剪细化各有帮助，但没有一种架构能同时解决细结构恢复、同类实例归属和遮挡推理的耦合挑战。数据集与代码已公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向移动式玉米表型分析，近距离第一人称田间图像至关重要，因为许多植株层面的性状依赖于冠层内部结构，而这些结构难以从俯视角度观察。然而，出苗后玉米田构成困难的实例分割场景：茎、叶、雄穗及邻近植株细长、重复且严重遮挡。现有标签方案在同类严重重叠下无法提供可靠的监督信号。

Method: 引入EgoMaize紧凑基准，其核心创新是证据封闭（evidence-closed）标注工作流：对遮挡的玉米区域进行可靠的标注，将不可靠的玉米区域归入忽略（ignore）而非背景（background），从而避免可见标签导致的物理植株碎片化以及全模态标签的不可验证问题。

Result: 基准结果显示：预训练查询分组、边界细化和高分辨率裁剪细化分别在任务的不同方面有所助益；但没有任何一种架构能同时解决细结构恢复、同类实例归属和遮挡推理这三者耦合的挑战。遮挡级别分析进一步表明，植株可见性越有限，性能下降越明显。

Conclusion: EgoMaize揭示了近距离玉米实例分割中"细结构恢复-同类实例归属-遮挡推理"三者耦合的难题，证明现有实例分割架构尚无法解决该挑战，为移动玉米表型分析提供了公开基准与代码，可推动该方向研究。

Abstract: Close-range first-person field images are important for mobile maize phenotyping because many plant-level traits depend on in-canopy structures that are difficult to ob serve from overhead views. However, post-seedling maize fields create a difficult in stance segmentation setting: stems, leaves, tassels, and neighboring plants are elon gated, repetitive, and strongly occluded. We introduce EgoMaize, a compact benchmark for first-person maize instance segmentation, where the task is to predict ownership consistent plant masks and plant-owned stem/tassel cues from close-range field images with severe same-class overlap. Existing visible-only labels can fragment one physi cal plant into disconnected supervision, while full-amodal labels may require unverifi able completion behind neighboring plants or field objects. EgoMaize therefore uses an evidence-closed annotation workflow for occluded maize regions and assigns unreli able maize regions to ignore rather than background. Baseline results show that pre trained query-based grouping, boundary refinement, and high-resolution crop refine ment help different aspects of the task, but no architecture solves the coupled chal lenges of fine structure recovery, same-class instance ownership, and occlusion reason ing; occlusion-level analysis further shows that performance decreases as plant visi bility becomes more limited. The dataset and code are publicly available at https: //github.com/JaaaaaaaD/EgoMaize.

</details>


### [14] [Context-Aware Causal Gaze Forecasting for Human-Vehicle Interaction During In-Cabin Tracking Dropouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12374)
*Shabnam Shabani,Ghazal Farhani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出因果注视恢复（Causal Gaze Recovery）任务与因果上下文门控预测器（CCGF），用于在驾驶监控中因大幅头部转动（如肩膀检查、后视镜扫视）导致眼动追踪丢失时，仅利用丢失前的历史数据（注视、头部姿态、场景特征）在线预测丢失期间的注视，无需使用未来的目标追踪器数据。在10.5小时自然驾驶、2,047个丢失事件上，CCGF平均每位驾驶者中位误差为175.7px（10.5°），相比仅用历史的因果预测降低33%误差；实时场景更新比冻结场景输入误差更低，表明丢失期间获取的场景观测具有预测价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车载仪表盘注视追踪器在驾驶员大幅头部转动（肩膀检查、后视镜扫视、十字路口扫描）时常丢失对眼睛的视线，而这些恰恰是驾驶视觉注意力信息最关键的时机。离线间隙填充方法可同时利用间隙两侧的观测，但在线驾驶监控系统无法依赖尚未发生的未来数据。因此需要一种严格因果的注视恢复方法，在目标追踪器在t时刻及之后不可用时仍能预测其注视。

Method: 提出Causal Context-Gated Forecaster（CCGF）：编码丢失前60帧的注视与头部姿态历史，并与DINOv3场景特征结合；通过一个可学习的可靠性门控（reliability gate）在丢失进展过程中动态调节历史与场景表示的贡献。评估了两种场景条件：Live（场景表示在追踪丢失期间持续更新）与Frozen（丢失期间使用丢失前最后的场景表示）。以头戴式Neon追踪器的同步注视坐标作为监督与评估目标，但从不作为模型输入。

Result: 在10.5小时自然驾驶、10名驾驶者的2,047个合格的GazeSense head_lost事件上，head_lost占GazeSense记录时间的8.5%。采用留一驾驶员交叉验证，CCGF在Live场景更新下达到每位驾驶者中位误差175.7px（10.5°），相比仅用历史的因果预测降低33%；Frozen场景输入下误差增至210.8px（12.9°），表明丢失期间获取的场景观测提供了有用的预测信息。

Conclusion: CCGF验证了在线因果环境下的注视恢复可行性，证明结合丢失前注视/头部姿态历史与实时场景特征能够显著降低跟踪丢失期间的注视预测误差，且丢失期间持续更新的场景信息比冻结场景更有价值。作者将公开数据集、评估协议与因果基线，以推动该方向研究。

Abstract: Dashboard-mounted gaze trackers often lose sight of the driver's eyes during large head rotations, including shoulder checks, mirror glances, and intersection scanning. These maneuvers occur when information about the driver's visual attention is most useful. Offline gap-filling methods may reconstruct a missing interval using observations from both sides, but an online driver-monitoring system cannot rely on measurements that have not yet occurred. We therefore formulate causal gaze recovery: forecasting unavailable gaze at time t without target-tracker gaze at t or later. We introduce the Causal Context-Gated Forecaster (CCGF), which encodes a 60-frame pre-dropout history of gaze and head pose and combines it with DINOv3 scene features. A learned reliability gate controls the contribution of the history and scene representations as the dropout progresses. We evaluate two scene conditions: Live, in which the scene representation continues to update during tracker loss, and Frozen, in which the final pre-dropout representation is used throughout the missing interval. We evaluate CCGF on 2,047 eligible, naturally occurring GazeSense head\_lost events drawn from 10.5 h of naturalistic driving by ten drivers. Across all recordings, head\_lost accounts for 8.5 percent of GazeSense recording time. Synchronized gaze coordinates from a head-mounted Neon tracker provide supervision and evaluation targets but are never used as model inputs. Under leave-one-driver-out evaluation, CCGF achieves a mean per-driver median error of 175.7 px (10.5 deg) with Live scene updates, a 33 percent reduction relative to history-only causal forecasting. With Frozen scene input, the error increases to 210.8 px (12.9 deg), indicating that scene observations acquired during the dropout provide useful predictive information. We will release the dataset, evaluation protocol, and causal baselines.

</details>


### [15] [UFO: Chain-of-Evaluation for Omni-Condition Alignment in Multi-Modal Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12397)
*Danning Zhang,Yijing Lin,Shuhan Zhuang,Mengqi Huang,Shaojin Wu,Shancheng Fang,Zhendong Mao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UFO，首个用于多模态图像生成（特别是主体驱动定制）的全条件对齐同步评估的统一框架。UFO引入"原子化评估链"（Atomized Chain-of-Evaluation）范式，将全条件对齐分解为细粒度、解耦的原子评估单元（AEU），按模态相关性分类，并采用通用或专用功能调用对不同AEU类型进行精确验证。实验表明UFO与人类评估偏好相关性最高，平均提升15.25%；同时推出UFO-Bench基准，用于在文本与视觉条件多样交互下全面评估现有定制化模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态图像生成尤其是主体驱动定制化日益受到关注，但现有评估方法严重滞后。现有基于嵌入或基于多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的方法将各模态条件的对齐孤立地分别评估，这与多模态图像生成需同时满足多条件对齐的目标相矛盾，导致评估结果与人类判断一致性差。因此需要一种能实现全条件对齐同步评估的统一框架。

Method: 提出UFO（Unified Framework for Omni-condition alignment simultaneous evaluation）。其核心创新是"原子化评估链"（Atomized Chain-of-Evaluation）范式：首先将全条件对齐分解为一系列细粒度、解耦的原子评估单元（Atomic Evaluation Units, AEU）的序列链；然后将这些AEU分类为不同的模态相关性类别；最后针对不同AEU类型采用通用或专用的功能调用（functional calls）进行精确验证，从而实现多模态条件下同步、精确的一致性评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，UFO与人类评估偏好的相关性最高，平均提升达15.25%。此外还构建了UFO-Bench专用基准，用以在文本与视觉条件的多样相互交互下，全面评估现有定制化模型的性能。

Conclusion: UFO首次实现多模态图像生成的全条件对齐同步评估，克服了传统孤立评估与多条件对齐目标相悖的问题，显著提升与人类判断的一致性。UFO-Bench为定制化模型在复杂多模态交互下的全面评估提供了标准测试平台，为生成模型评估领域提供了新范式。

Abstract: Multi-modal image generation, particularly subject-driven customization, has garnered growing attention in recent years. Despite the rapid advancement of generative models, their evaluation remains largely lagging. Existing methods, whether embedding-based or Multi-modal Large Language Model (MLLM)-based, evaluate alignment with each modal condition in isolation, which contradicts the simultaneous condition alignment objective of multi-modal image generation, leading to poor consistency with human judgments. To address this challenge, we propose UFO, the first unified framework for omni-condition alignment simultaneous evaluation. Specifically, UFO introduces a novel Atomized Chain-of-Evaluation paradigm, \emph{i.e.}, it first decomposes omni-condition alignment into a sequential chain of fine-grained, disentangled Atomic Evaluation Units (AEUs), categorizes them into distinct modality-relevance classes, and then employs general or dedicated functional calls for accurate verification of different AEU types. Experimental results demonstrate that UFO achieves the highest correlation with human evaluation preferences, delivering an average improvement of 15.25\%. Furthermore, we present UFO-Bench, a dedicated benchmark designed to holistically evaluate the performance of existing customization models under the diverse mutual interactions of textual and visual conditions.

</details>


### [16] [GSO-Net: Visual State Machines for Hazardous Freight Transfer Compliance at Petrochemical Logistics Nodes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12408)
*Yu Xie,Bangshu Xiong,Zhibo Rao,Rui Gan,Chongxuan Liu,Zechu Ouyang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GSO-Net，这是首个面向石化危险品装卸场景的视觉标准作业程序（SOP）理解的大规模基准数据集，包含从64个真实高速公路石化物流节点采集的超过5万帧图像，并构建了9个宏观步骤与15个微观操作状态的分层体系，用于在稀疏轮询等现实部署约束下评估模型对转移状态的细粒度感知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 石化物流节点的危化品作业对智能交通系统而言是安全关键环节，但现有视觉基准很少关注符合现实部署约束（如摄像机稀疏轮询导致的观测不完整）下的程序合规性问题。由于大型基础设施网络中的摄像机通常是稀疏轮询工作，转移状态必须从不完整观测和局部证据中推断，因此亟需一个专门针对该场景的视觉SOP理解基准。

Method: GSO-Net构建了一个SOP衍生的层级体系，将9个宏观程序步骤与15个微观操作状态联系起来，数据来自64个真实高速公路石化物流节点的50,000余帧独立采样图像。基准定义了两个任务：核心任务为微观状态与宏观步骤的联合检测，诊断参考任务为帧级步骤分类。实验中采用轻量级、基于Transformer、开放词表和整体模型等多种模型进行评测。

Result: 实验显示现有模型在对象感知与转移阶段理解之间存在明显差距，尤其在接触级状态锚定、瞬时步骤识别和阶段一致性方面表现薄弱；在稀疏轮询、微小关键目标和长尾操作证据等条件下性能尤为不足。

Conclusion: GSO-Net为危化品运输中的细粒度状态感知和基于视觉的安全监控提供了一个实用基准，揭示了当前感知模型在真实部署约束下理解程序合规性的短板，数据集已公开供社区使用。

Abstract: Hazardous-freight operations at petrochemical logistics nodes are safety-critical for intelligent transportation systems, yet existing vision benchmarks rarely address procedural compliance under realistic deployment constraints. In large infrastructure networks, cameras often operate under sparse round-robin polling, so transfer status must be inferred from incomplete observations and localized evidence. We present GSO-Net, a large-scale benchmark for visual understanding of standard operating procedures (SOPs) in petrochemical unloading scenarios. To our knowledge, GSO-Net is the first public benchmark dataset dedicated to visual SOP understanding in petrochemical hazardous-freight transfer scenarios. It contains over 50,000 independently sampled frames from 64 real expressway petrochemical logistics nodes and adopts an SOP-derived hierarchy linking 9 macroscopic procedural steps with 15 microscopic operational states. Two tasks are defined: joint detection of microscopic states and macroscopic steps as the core benchmark, and frame-level step classification as a diagnostic reference. Experiments with lightweight, transformer-based, open-vocabulary, and holistic models reveal a clear gap between object perception and transfer-stage understanding. Current models remain weak on contact-level state grounding, transient step recognition, and stage consistency, especially under sparse polling, tiny critical targets, and long-tailed operational evidence. GSO-Net provides a practical benchmark for fine-grained state perception and vision-based safety monitoring in hazardous freight transportation. The dataset is publicly available at https://github.com/yuxieHarrison/GSO-Net

</details>


### [17] [OphBiWSSD: Scaling Temporal Action Localization in Ophthalmic Surgeries with Bidirectional Weight-tied State Space Duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12409)
*Yang Liu,Qionghong Ma,Joongwon Chae,Lihui Luo,Yibing Shen,Yulin Zhuo,Yingting Zhu,Jiashu Chang,Xiaoyun Zhong,Dongmei Yu,Peter E. Lobie,Peiwu Qin,Chengming Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OphBiWSSD 提出了一种基于双向状态空间对偶（Bidirectional State Space Duality）的手术时间动作定位框架，通过权重共享的选择性扫描机制同时整合前向与后向的手术上下文，以线性复杂度实现非因果时间线索的全局综合，在 OphNet 基准上取得领先性能（phase 和 operation 的 mAP 分别为 44.42% 和 43.08%，较基线提升 6.80% 和 6.66%），兼顾局部边界精度与长程上下文建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 眼科手术中的高频手术动作需要高保真的时间建模，但基于注意力架构的模型在处理长程过程依赖时计算开销过高（二次方内存复杂度）。现有方法常需激进的时间降采样，这会损害细粒度动作边界与器械-组织交互的检测精度。因此，需要一个既能捕捉长程非因果依赖、又保持线性计算复杂度的方法来突破这一可扩展性瓶颈。

Method: 提出 OphBiWSSD 框架，核心思想是采用双向状态空间对偶（Bidirectional State Space Duality）。具体地，利用权重绑定的选择性扫描机制，同时纳入手术过程的前序与后继上下文，从而以线性复杂度全局综合非因果时间线索。该架构能自然捕捉眼科工作流中的双向依赖，在保持线性内存开销的同时弥合局部边界精度与长程过程上下文之间的差距，而无需承受传统 Transformer 的二次方内存负担。

Result: 在 OphNet 基准上进行的大量实验表明，OphBiWSSD 实现了当前最优的时间定位性能：phase 的平均精度（mAP）为 44.42%，operation 为 43.08%，分别超越基线 6.80% 和 6.66%。经验验证表明该方法能保证精确的时间定位，并提供了一条计算上可行的路径。

Conclusion: OphBiWSSD 通过双向状态空间对偶与权重绑定选择性扫描机制，在保持线性复杂度的同时实现了高精度的手术时间动作定位，兼顾了局部边界精度与全局长程上下文，为临床环境中部署手术智能系统提供了计算上可行的解决方案。代码已公开。

Abstract: High-frequency surgical maneuvers in ophthalmology necessitate high-fidelity temporal modeling, yet characterizing long-range procedural dependencies remains computationally prohibitive for attention-based architectures. Existing models often require aggressive temporal downsampling, which compromises the detection of fine-grained action boundaries and instrument-tissue interactions. To address these scalability constraints, we present OphBiWSSD, a framework that reformulates surgical temporal action localization leveraging Bidirectional State Space Duality. By employing a weight-tied selective scan mechanism that incorporates both preceding and succeeding surgical contexts, our approach facilitates the global synthesis of non-causal temporal cues with linear complexity. This streamlined architecture is well-suited to capture the bidirectional dependencies present in ophthalmic workflows, effectively bridging the gap between local boundary precision and long-range procedural context without incurring the quadratic memory overhead of traditional Transformers. Extensive experiments on the OphNet benchmark demonstrate that OphBiWSSD achieves state-of-the-art temporal localization performance, with mean Average Precisions of 44.42% on phases and 43.08% on operations, surpassing the baselines by 6.80% and 6.66%, respectively. Empirical validation indicates that our approach ensures precise temporal localization and offers a computationally viable pathway for deploying surgical intelligence systems in clinical environments. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/yo3nglau/OphBiWSSD.

</details>


### [18] [A Multimodal Explainable Deep Learning Framework for Alzheimer's Disease Diagnosis using 3D Magnetic Resonance Imaging and Clinical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12410)
*Yusuf Brima,Marcellin Atemkeng,Lakshmana Rao Namamula,Antoine Vacavant*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一个可解释的多模态深度学习框架（3D CNN编码T1加权MRI + 前馈网络处理临床与人口学数据），在ADNI与OASIS-3两个队列上比较不同融合策略对阿尔茨海默病（AD）诊断性能与可解释性的影响。结果发现无论性能还是解释均具有任务、模态、融合方式和队列依赖性：仅表格数据的模型在ADNI表现最佳，仅视觉模型在OASIS-3表现最佳，无融合策略能稳定超越单一模态；MMSE是主导的表格特征且在两个队列中一致，但CAM型解释随模型配置和队列变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 痴呆症是日益增长的全球健康负担，AD占大多数病例，及时准确诊断至关重要且日益依赖整合互补的临床与影像信息。虽然多模态深度学习可结合这些模态进行AD诊断，但其解释在不同模态、融合策略和队列之间的行为仍不清楚，因此有必要系统研究可解释性在队列迁移下的表现。

Method: 构建可解释多模态框架：以3D CNN编码器处理T1加权MRI，以前馈网络处理已协调的临床与人口学数据；在三分类及两两分类诊断任务上比较多种模型配置（含跨注意力融合等）。使用ADNI内部6,479条记录和OASIS-3独立1,703条记录，采用SHAP与Integrated Gradients分析表格特征，并利用CAM类方法解释影像特征。

Result: ADNI上仅表格数据模型取得最高三分类AUC-ROC（0.879）及最佳CN vs MCI区分（0.903），跨注意力融合在MCI vs AD上最佳（0.861），CN vs AD整体区分度高。OASIS-3上仅视觉模型表现最佳（三分类AUC-ROC 0.910），CN vs MCI仍困难，且无融合策略在所有任务和队列中一致超越单一模态。SHAP与Integrated Gradients在两个队列中均将MMSE识别为主导表格特征，全局特征排序在ADNI（ρ=0.94）与OASIS-3（ρ=0.96）强烈一致；但CAM解释随模型配置和队列变化。

Conclusion: 多模态性能与解释是任务、模态、融合方式和队列依赖的：主导认知信号（MMSE）跨队列保持，但特征贡献和CAM解释并不保持稳定。这强调应将可解释性置于队列迁移下评估，而非视为稳定、内在的属性。

Abstract: Dementia is a major and growing global health burden, with Alzheimer's disease (AD) accounting for most cases. Timely and accurate diagnosis is central to managing this burden and increasingly depends on integrating complementary clinical and imaging information. Multimodal deep learning can combine these modalities for AD diagnosis, but how its explanations behave across modalities, fusion strategies, and cohorts remains unclear. We developed an explainable multimodal framework pairing a 3D CNN encoder for T1-weighted MRI with a feedforward network for harmonized clinical and demographic data, comparing varied model setups on three-way and pairwise diagnostic tasks using 6,479 internal records from the ADNI and 1,703 independent records from the OASIS-3. On ADNI, the tabular-only model achieved the highest three-class AUC-ROC of 0.879 and best discriminated cognitively normal (CN) versus mild cognitive impairment (MCI; 0.903), while cross-attention performed best for MCI versus AD (0.861); CN versus AD was highly discriminative overall. On OASIS-3, the vision-only model performed best (three-class AUC-ROC 0.910); CN versus MCI remained difficult, and no fusion strategy consistently outperformed single modalities across tasks and cohorts. SHAP and Integrated Gradients identified the MMSE as the dominant tabular feature in both cohorts, with global feature rankings agreeing strongly in ADNI ($ρ=0.94$) and OASIS-3 ($ρ=0.96$); CAM-based explanations, however, changed with model configuration and cohort. These findings show that multimodal performance and explanations are task, modality, fusion, and cohort-dependent: a dominant cognitive signal persisted across cohorts, but feature contributions and CAM explanations did not, underscoring the need to evaluate explainability under cohort shift rather than as a stable, intrinsic property.

</details>


### [19] [DERA: Detached Edge-Residual Adaptation for Prohibited item Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12411)
*Yonathan Michael,Mohamad Alansari,Mohammed Bennamoun,Dwarikanath Mahapatra,Andreas Henschel,Naoufel Werghi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DERA是一个用于X光图像违禁品检测的"分离边缘残差自适应"框架。它通过融合分层视觉特征与并行像素差分边缘金字塔，从训练时实例掩膜的轮廓中学习物体特定的边界先验。该分离先验用于门控边缘敏感特征，并通过残差头注入早期视觉阶段。该设计在自适应开始时保留基础检测器，将边界监督与语义特征学习隔离，最终自适应阶段仅需14.7K可训练参数。在PIDray、CLCXray和STCray上分别比基线提升3.1、1.6和2.4个AP点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: X光图像中的违禁品检测由于物体叠加、纹理弱和材料杂乱而极具挑战性，这些因素会掩盖语义外观和物体边界，导致现有检测器性能受限。

Method: DERA框架：1) 将分层视觉特征与并行像素差分边缘金字塔结合；2) 从训练时实例掩膜轮廓学习物体特定边界先验；3) 分离先验门控边缘敏感特征，通过残差头注入早期视觉阶段；4) 分级设计在自适应开始时保留基础检测器，将边界监督与语义特征学习隔离。

Result: 在PIDray、CLCXray和STCray三个数据集上，DERA分别将基线提升3.1、1.6和2.4个AP点，且最终自适应阶段仅需14.7K可训练参数。

Conclusion: 通过分离边界先验和残差注入机制，DERA在保持基础检测器性能的同时高效解决了X光图像中边界模糊和物体叠加导致的检测困难问题，在多个数据集上验证了有效性。

Abstract: Prohibited-item detection in X-ray imagery remains challenging due to object superposition, weak texture, and material clutter which obscure both semantic appearance and object boundaries. We propose \textbf{DERA}, a \textbf{D}etached \textbf{E}dge-\textbf{R}esidual \textbf{A}daptation framework for prohibited item detection under X-ray imagery. DERA combines hierarchical visual features with a parallel pixel-difference edge pyramid and learns an object-specific boundary prior from training-time contours of the instance masks. The detached prior gates edge-sensitive features, which are injected into the early visual stages through residual heads. This staged design preserves the foundation detector at the start of adaptation, isolates boundary supervision from semantic feature learning, and restricts the final adaptation stage to only \(14.7\)K trainable parameters. Evaluated on PIDray, CLCXray, and STCray, DERA improves the baseline by \textbf{3.1}, \textbf{1.6}, and \textbf{2.4} AP points, respectively.

</details>


### [20] [Spectral Consistency-Guided Multiview Point Cloud Registration for Low-Overlap Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12417)
*Tianyu Li,Yanghong Lin,Shudong Zhou,Kui Yang,Jingru Zhang,Li Fang,Wei Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GMPCR，一种基于谱一致性引导的非学习多视角点云配准方法，通过稀疏位姿图与自适应同步在低重叠场景下实现高效鲁棒配准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低重叠场景下多视角点云配准存在可靠对应点稀缺、错误成对变换影响全局位姿估计的问题，且全配对配准计算开销大。

Method: 构建精炼的二阶兼容性结构，利用主导谱响应评估对应点可靠性与扫描对置信度，过滤不可靠对应点并选择信息量高的扫描对；对保留扫描对采用最大团假设生成相对变换；通过自适应历史感知同步方案精化位姿图，并根据全局旋转残差调整历史影响，支持一致性改善时恢复边权重。

Result: 在3DMatch和3DLoMatch上分别达到97.2%和89.6%的配准召回率，ScanNet与ETH上表现具竞争力。

Conclusion: GMPCR在配准精度、低重叠鲁棒性和计算效率之间取得了良好平衡，代码已开源。

Abstract: Multiview point cloud registration is particularly challenging in low-overlap scenes, where reliable correspondences are limited and incorrect pairwise transformations can affect global pose estimation. In addition, registering all scan pairs is computationally expensive because many pairs provide weak geometric information. To address these problems, we propose GMPCR, a non-learning-based spectral consistency-guided framework for efficient and robust multiview point cloud registration. GMPCR builds a refined second-order compatibility structure from initial correspondences and uses its dominant spectral response to evaluate both correspondence reliability and scan-pair confidence. This allows unreliable correspondences to be filtered and informative scan pairs to be selected before relative transformation estimation, leading to a sparse pose graph and reduced pairwise registration cost. For each retained scan pair, maximal-clique-based hypothesis generation is used to estimate reliable relative transformations. The resulting pose graph is further refined by an adaptive history-aware synchronization scheme, in which the effect of residual history is adjusted according to changes in the global rotation residual. A recovery mechanism also allows down-weighted edges to regain confidence when their global consistency improves. Experiments on 3DMatch, 3DLoMatch, ScanNet, and ETH demonstrate the effectiveness of GMPCR. It achieves registration recalls of 97.2% and 89.6% on 3DMatch and 3DLoMatch, respectively, while maintaining competitive performance on ScanNet and ETH. The results show that GMPCR provides a favorable balance among registration accuracy, robustness to low overlap, and computational efficiency. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/swccj/gmpcr.

</details>


### [21] [An End-to-End Automated Pipeline for Controllable Crack Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12431)
*Conghui Li,Muxin Pu,Chern Hong Lim,Weiyao Lin,Xin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种端到端的可控裂缝数据合成流水线，通过程序化Bézier曲线骨架与GAN共同生成多样裂缝掩膜，并基于双ControlNet扩散架构实现外观与几何解耦引导，支持背景无关合成及上下文感知修复，在CRACK500和CrackTree200数据集上验证了相较现有增强基线的稳定性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习方法在自动化裂缝检测中受限于缺陷数据的稀缺性与弱可控性；现有生成式增强方法往往把裂缝合成当作通用图像生成任务，缺乏对裂缝形态学特征、边缘保真度和场景上下文（如背景纹理、光照条件）的精细控制。

Method: 方法包含两个核心环节：（1）程序化采样Bézier曲线骨架，利用生成对抗网络（GAN）将其转换为逼真的裂缝掩膜，免去人工掩膜设计流程，实现大规模可控形态生成；（2）构建双ControlNet扩散框架，将外观引导与几何引导解耦，其中基于边缘提取的分支施加严格的边界一致性约束；框架支持两类工作模式——无背景条件下的纯裂缝合成，以及上下文感知的裂缝区域修复（inpainting）。

Result: 在CRACK500和CrackTree200两个公开裂缝数据集上进行实验，该合成流水线相比现有数据增强方法在检测性能上取得一致提升，表明生成的裂缝数据具有良好的形态多样性和边界保真度，能有效支撑下游检测模型的训练。

Conclusion: 该工作将裂缝几何与检测场景背景形式化为可复用的计算约束，构建了一个可扩展的工程信息学工作流，为自动化裂缝检测中的数据生成问题提供了系统化的工程解决方案。

Abstract: Automated crack inspection increasingly relies on deep learning, yet its reliability is limited by scarce and weakly controllable defect data. Existing generative augmentation methods often treat crack synthesis as a generic image-generation task, offering insufficient control over morphology, boundary fidelity, and scene context. This paper proposes an end-to-end automated pipeline for controllable crack data synthesis that formalizes crack geometry and inspection context into reusable computational constraints. First, procedurally sampled Bézier-curve skeletons are translated into realistic crack masks using a GAN, enabling scalable generation of diverse crack morphologies without manual mask design. Second, a dual-ControlNet diffusion framework disentangles appearance guidance from geometric guidance, with an edge-based branch enforcing strict boundary consistency. The framework supports both background-free synthesis and context-aware inpainting. Experiments on CRACK500 and CrackTree200 show consistent gains over existing augmentation baselines, demonstrating a scalable engineering informatics workflow for automated crack-inspection data generation.

</details>


### [22] [Bridging Vision Foundation Model Priors with CLIP for Spatial-aware Few-shot Anomaly Detection in Medical Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12454)
*Juzheng Miao,Yuchen Yuan,Cheng Chen,Pheng-Ann Heng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Spatial-FAD框架，结合视觉基础模型DINO的空间先验与CLIP语义，通过VFM增强适配器、滑动窗口聚合和原型增强支持记忆，实现少样本医学异常检测的精确病灶定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP等视觉-语言模型在少样本医学异常检测中具有强语义对齐能力，但其全局对比预训练缺乏显式空间监督，导致病灶定位不精确；而DINO等视觉基础模型通过自蒸馏和局部-全局一致性学习到空间连贯的补丁表示，能更好地捕捉精细解剖结构。两者互补，但尚未有效结合。

Method: 提出Spatial-FAD框架，包含三个核心模块：1) VFM增强适配器，将DINO的结构亲和先验注入CLIP特征，引导视觉嵌入贴合病灶边界；2) 滑动窗口聚合策略，解决补丁化和输入分辨率限制，生成高分辨率空间密集嵌入；3) 原型增强支持记忆方案，存储正常和异常模式的紧凑原型，融合补丁-原型和图像-文本相似度，降低内存成本并提升性能。

Result: 在肝脏CT、视网膜OCT和脑MRI三个基准数据集上，Spatial-FAD显著优于现有方法，尤其在病灶分割方面。在4-shot场景下，Dice分数平均提升超过11.4%，AUC提升1.8%。

Conclusion: Spatial-FAD有效融合VFM空间先验与CLIP语义，显著提升少样本医学异常检测的定位精度和分割性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models such as CLIP enable effective few-shot medical anomaly detection (AD) via strong image-text semantic alignment. However, their globally contrastive pretraining lacks explicit spatial supervision, limiting precise lesion localization. In contrast, Vision Foundation Models (VFMs) such as DINO learn spatially coherent patch representations via self-distillation and local-to-global consistency, better capturing fine-grained anatomical structures. Leveraging this complementarity, we propose Spatial-FAD, a spatial-aware few-shot medical AD framework that improves lesion localization by combining VFM spatial priors with CLIP semantics. Specifically, we introduce a VFM-enhanced adapter that injects a structural affinity prior derived from DINO into CLIP features. This structure-guided refinement encourages visual embeddings to better adhere to lesion boundaries while maintaining semantic alignment. To address the loss of spatial detail from patchification and the limited input resolution of CLIP, we adopt a sliding-window aggregation strategy. This generates high-resolution, spatially dense embeddings to further enhance localization granularity. Moreover, we introduce a prototype-enhanced support memory scheme to efficiently exploit the few-shot support set. This module stores compact prototypes for normal and abnormal patterns, reducing memory costs while boosting performance by fusing patch-to-prototype and image-text similarities. Extensive experiments on three benchmark datasets, including Liver CT, Retinal OCT, and Brain MRI, demonstrate that Spatial-FAD significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods, especially in lesion segmentation. Notably, in the 4-shot scenario, our method achieves an average improvement of over 11.4% in Dice score and 1.8% in AUC. Code is available at: https://github.com/JuzhengMiao/Spatial-FAD.

</details>


### [23] [Partition-Invariant Tuning for 3D Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12473)
*Hongqiang Lin,Tianle Wang,Shuiwang Li,Dongxu Zhang,Yiding Sun,Zihao Guo,Dongfu Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对大规模场景点云理解中的序列化分区变化问题，提出PointPiT框架，通过场景感知结构适配器(SSA)融合局部几何与全局上下文、结合梯度子空间优化(GSO)筛选稳定更新方向，以不到1%的可训练参数实现与全量微调相当甚至更优的性能，并在多个场景级基准上达到PEFT方法的最优水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 场景级点云理解因几何多样性复杂、空间布局各异而具有挑战性。预训练3D点云基础模型(PFM)虽迁移性强，但全量微调(FFT)计算与存储开销巨大。参数高效微调(PEFT)虽有前景，但现有方法多聚焦于物体级点云，忽视了大规模场景中序列化带来的分区(partition)变化问题，导致场景级应用效果受限。

Method: 提出PointPiT分区不变微调框架，包含两大核心组件：(1) 场景感知结构适配器(SSA)，融合局部几何模式与全局场景上下文，缓解分区导致的表征偏移(representation shift)；(2) 梯度子空间优化(GSO)，选取信息量大且分区稳定的更新方向，抑制优化过程中的分区相关扰动。

Result: 在多个场景级基准数据集上，PointPiT以少于1%的骨干网络参数取得与全量微调相当甚至更优的性能，并在代表性PEFT方法中达到一致的state-of-the-art水平。

Conclusion: 验证了分区不变微调策略对场景级点云理解的有效性：通过场景感知适配器与梯度子空间优化协同，既能以极低参数开销维持PFM的强迁移能力，又能克服大规模场景序列化分区的负面影响，为PEFT在场景级点云任务中的应用提供了新范式。

Abstract: Scene-level point cloud understanding remains challenging due to diverse geometries and spatial layouts. While pre-trained 3D point cloud foundation models (PFMs) offer strong transferability, full fine-tuning (FFT) incurs substantial computational and storage costs. Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) provides a promising alternative, but existing PEFT methods largely focus on object-level point clouds and overlook serialization-induced partition variations in large-scale scenes. To address this issue, we propose PointPiT, a partition-invariant tuning framework for scene-level point clouds. Specifically, a Scene-aware Structural Adapter (SSA) integrates local geometric patterns with global scene context to mitigate partition-induced representation shifts. Moreover, Gradient Subspace Optimization (GSO) selects informative and partition-stable update directions, suppressing partition-dependent variations during optimization. Extensive experiments across multiple scene-level benchmarks demonstrate that PointPiT achieves competitive or even superior performance to full fine-tuning with less than 1% of backbone's parameters, while achieving consistent state-of-the-art performance among representative PEFT methods.

</details>


### [24] [PhysioAI: Clinical Knowledge-Guided Semantic Supervision for Skeleton-Based Physiotherapy Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12491)
*Jie Cao,Euijoon Ahn,Anwar Hassan,Jinman Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhysioAI 是一种临床知识引导的语义监督框架，通过将结构化物理治疗知识（Clinical Knowledge Dictionary）注入骨架表征学习，解决康复场景中运动类别差异细微、类内变异大的动作识别难题。该方法仅在训练阶段使用 CLIP 编码的语义锚点，推理仅需骨架输入，在 KiMoRe 与 UI-PRMD 数据集上分别超越最强基线 0.27 与 1.33 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有骨架动作识别方法多面向大规模日常动作基准，不适用于康复场景：康复数据集规模小、运动类别间仅存在细微运动学差异，且运动障碍患者的动作在幅度、速度、协调性上偏离标准模式，导致类内变异增大，传统模型难以可靠识别。

Method: PhysioAI 将图基时空建模与基于结构化临床知识字典（CKD）的训练期语义锚点相结合。CKD 描述经冻结的 CLIP 模型编码并投影至锚点空间，为骨架表征学习提供类别特定语义目标。语义锚点仅用于训练阶段，推理时只需骨架输入。

Result: 在受试者分离评估下，PhysioAI 在 KiMoRe Overall 上达 99.03±1.34%，在 Hard-67 压力测试上达 94.64±7.36%，在 UI-PRMD Overall 上达 87.44±7.69%；相比各端点最强对比方法分别提升 0.27、2.87、1.33 个百分点。

Conclusion: 结构化临床知识可作为物理治疗动作识别中有效的训练期监督来源，验证了临床知识引导的语义监督能缓解康复场景小样本与高类内变异问题，并支持远程康复中的自动化动作跟踪。

Abstract: Skeleton-based action recognition can support automated tracking of physiotherapy exercises, particularly in remote rehabilitation settings where continuous in-person supervision is impractical. However, most existing methods are developed for large-scale daily-action benchmarks rather than rehabilitation scenarios. Public rehabilitation exercise datasets are typically small, with only subtle kinematic differences between exercise classes. For participants with motor impairments, exercise execution may also deviate from standard movement patterns in amplitude, speed, and coordination, increasing intra-class variability and making reliable recognition more difficult for skeleton-based models. We propose PhysioAI, a clinical knowledge-guided semantic supervision framework that injects structured physiotherapy knowledge into skeleton representation learning. PhysioAI combines graph-based spatiotemporal modelling of human movement with training-time semantic anchors derived from a structured Clinical Knowledge Dictionary (CKD). The CKD descriptions are encoded using a frozen Contrastive Language-Image Pre-training (CLIP) model and projected into an anchor space, where they provide class-specific semantic targets for skeleton representation learning. The resulting CKD-derived anchors are used only during skeleton-model training; inference requires only skeleton inputs. Under subject-disjoint evaluation, PhysioAI achieves $99.03\pm1.34\%$ on KiMoRe Overall, $94.64\pm7.36\%$ on the Hard-67 stress test, and $87.44\pm7.69\%$ on UI-PRMD Overall. These results exceed the strongest comparator for each endpoint by $0.27$, $2.87$, and $1.33$ percentage points (pp), respectively. These findings demonstrate that structured clinical knowledge can serve as an effective source of training-time supervision for physiotherapy action recognition.

</details>


### [25] [RoES: Rotational Equivariant Selective-frequency Fusion for Multimodal Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12497)
*Jiabao Wang,Wenjian Liu,Yaoming Cai,Gengyu Zhang,Boyan Zhao,Zijia Zhang,Yao Ding,Xiaobo Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RoES（旋转等变选择性频率融合网络），针对红外-可见光图像融合中旋转等变性约束的全局均匀施加问题，通过可训练的旋转增强更新器/预测器动态分离低高频分量，并采用双分支融合模块（旋转等变Mamba处理低频结构、极坐标频谱注意力双傅里叶块精化高频细节），在融合质量和下游目标检测上均取得最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 红外-可见光图像融合本质上是不适定问题（ill-posed），现有方法虽依赖结构先验，但通常对所有特征均匀施加旋转等变性约束。这种整体性做法忽略了关键差异：低频共享结构应严格满足等变约束，而高频模态特有细节需要更大灵活性以保留独特信息。现有方法缺乏对频率选择性与等变约束的匹配处理。

Method: 提出RoES网络，核心创新包括：(1) 引入可训练的旋转增强更新器/预测器模块，动态解耦低、高频分量（替代静态分解）；(2) 构建双分支融合模块以保持频谱一致性——低频域采用旋转等变Mamba捕获长程结构依赖，高频域采用基于极坐标频谱注意力的双傅里叶块，在显式低频引导下精化高频细节。

Result: 大量实验表明RoES在融合质量和下游目标检测两个维度上均持续达到最先进（SOTA）性能，验证了调和频率选择性特征与等变约束对多模态融合的有效性。源代码已公开。

Conclusion: RoES通过按频率选择性施加旋转等变约束（低频严格等变、高频灵活），动态解耦频谱分量并配合双分支融合模块，为多模态红外-可见光融合建立了稳健的解决方案，兼顾了结构一致性与模态特有信息保留。

Abstract: Infrared-visible image fusion facilitates robust multimodal perception by integrating complementary textural nuances from visible sensors with thermal signatures from infrared systems. Due to the task's inherently ill-posed nature, existing methods heavily rely on structural priors but typically enforce rotation equivariance uniformly across all features. Such a holistic approach overlooks a critical distinction where low-frequency shared structures strictly adhere to equivariant constraints while high-frequency modality-specific details require greater flexibility to preserve unique information. To bridge this gap, we propose RoES, a Rotational Equivariant Selective-frequency fusion network. Instead of employing static decomposition, we introduce a trainable rotation-enhanced updater/predictor module to dynamically decouple low- and high-frequency components. The resulting representations are then processed through a dual-branch fusion module tailored for spectral consistency. Specifically, a rotation-equivariant Mamba is employed to capture long-range structural dependencies in the low-frequency domain, while a polar spectral attention-based Dual-Fourier block refines high-frequency details under explicit low-frequency guidance. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RoES consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance in both fusion quality and downstream object detection, establishing a robust solution for multimodal fusion by reconciling frequency-selective features with equivariant constraints. The source code is available at https://github.com/BryceLosky/RoES-Fusion.

</details>


### [26] [LettuceVisSim: A Simulator That Generates Lettuce Image Time-series for Vision-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12505)
*Ziye Zhu,Bert van 't Ooster,Congcong Sun,Eldert van Henten,Sjoerd Boersma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了LettuceVisSim，一个面向受控环境农业（CEA）中基于视觉的强化学习（RL）的仿真生菜生长模拟器。该模拟器包含三个核心组件：用于地上干物质（SDW）动态的过程模型（PBM）、从SDW推导冠层布局的算法，以及用于图像生成的Unity渲染引擎。它生成带标注的时序作物图像，以缓解标记作物图像稀缺的问题。五项发现验证了该模拟器：PBM复现SDW（R²=0.84）、分段三次回归映射投影面积（R²=0.94）、冠层布局在12个数据集上复现地面覆盖率动态（R²=0.84/0.40）、Unity渲染小于10毫秒，以及通过仅观察LettuceVisSim生成图像即可学习并应用光照控制策略的应用演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于视觉的强化学习在受控环境农业（CEA）的决策中具有巨大潜力，但其发展受限于标记作物图像的稀缺。这是因为获取大规模、准确标注的作物图像成本高昂且困难，阻碍了视觉RL模型的训练与验证。作者因此提出开发一个能够生成带标注时序作物图像的仿真模拟器，以填补这一数据缺口。

Method: LettuceVisSim模拟器由三部分组成：1) 过程模型（PBM），用于模拟地上干物质（SDW）的动态变化（支持动态植物密度管理）；2) 冠层布局算法，从SDW推导出冠层布局表示（结合分段三次回归将SDW映射到潜在投影面积）；3) Unity渲染引擎，将冠层布局表示转换为RGB图像和分割图像。该方法通过五个方面的实验对每个组件逐一验证，并通过一个光照控制策略学习的演示验证端到端的可用性。

Result: 五项结果支持该模拟器：1) PBM在动态植物密度管理下复现SDW，R²=0.84；2) 分段三次回归将SDW映射到潜在投影面积，R²=0.94；3) 冠层布局表示在12个不同动态环境和间距条件的实验数据集上复现地面覆盖率动态，由实测SDW驱动时R²=0.84，由PBM模拟值驱动时R²=0.40（排除一个离群点后为0.76）；4) Unity渲染引擎在小于10毫秒内将布局转换为图像；5) 演示表明仅通过观察LettuceVisSim生成的作物图像即可学习并应用光照控制策略。

Conclusion: 该研究为基于视觉的强化学习在受控环境农业中的应用提供了概念验证，证明LettuceVisSim能够生成高质量、带标注的合成作物图像，支持视觉RL策略的学习与部署。其分组件验证的框架表明模拟器具备较高的保真度，为缓解CEA中标记数据稀缺问题提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Vision-based reinforcement learning holds strong potential for decision-making in controlled environment agriculture (CEA). However, its development is hindered by the scarcity of labelled crop images. To address this gap, LettuceVisSim, a lettuce growth simulator that generates labelled time series of crop images, was developed and validated. The simulator contains a process-based model (PBM) for shoot dry weight dynamics, a canopy layout algorithm for deriving canopy layout representations from shoot dry weight, and a Unity rendering engine for image generation. Five findings support the simulator. First, the PBM reproduced shoot dry weight under dynamic plant-density management with $\mathrm{R}^{2}=0.84$. Second, a piecewise cubic regression mapped shoot dry weight to potential projected area with $\mathrm{R}^{2}=0.94$. Third, the canopy layout representation was validated using 12 experimental datasets each having different dynamic environmental and spacing conditions. It reproduced the ground coverage ratio dynamics observed in measured images, achieving $\mathrm{R}^{2}=0.84$ when driven by measured shoot dry weight and $\mathrm{R}^{2}=0.40$ (0.76 excluding one outlier) when driven by PBM-simulated values. Fourth, the Unity rendering engine converted canopy layout representations into RGB and segmentation images at less than 10~ms. Fifth, a demonstration showed that a lighting-control policy can be learned and applied by observing only crop images that were generated with LettuceVisSim, providing a proof of concept of vision-based reinforcement learning in CEA using LettuceVisSim.

</details>


### [27] [ChitraMiti: Benchmarking Visual Grounding and Modality Reliance in Bengali Geometric Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12509)
*Khan Raiyan Ibne Reza,Sanjana Aktar Maria,Sumaiya Tabassum Nimi,Md Adnan Arefeen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了 ChitraMiti-12.8k 基准（12,874个孟加拉语平面几何问题，配15属性结构化描述）和 NCTB-500（500个从孟加拉学校教科书手工提取的图表）。通过三阶段协议（仅图表、图表+描述、仅描述），作者发现：仅描述与图表+描述的模型性能统计上无显著差异，说明结构化描述可作为受控评估的充分文本代理；但模型跨模态验证能力差，常被交换的空间关系误导。监督微调可提升 ChitraMiti-1k 和 NCTB-500 的性能，但与最强的零样本模型仍有显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对视觉语言模型（VLM）在多模态数学推理方面的评估，在低资源语言和需要同时阅读图表与问题的几何问题上仍然有限。作者旨在填补这一空白，提供一个标准化方式研究孟加拉语多模态几何推理，并考察VLM是否真正用文本核对所见图像。

Method: 构建了 ChitraMiti-12.8k 合成基准（12,874道孟加拉语平面几何题，每道配结构化15属性描述）和 NCTB-500（500个从教科书手工提取的图表）。采用三阶段评估协议，分离仅图表、图表+描述、仅描述三类输入；在五种开源和闭源VLM上进行测试；并评估在 ChitraMiti-12.8k 上的监督微调效果。

Result: 仅描述输入的表现在统计上与图表+描述无显著差异，确立结构化描述可作为受控评估的充分文本代理。但模型在跨模态验证上表现差，即使能正确回答未修改的项目，也常被交换的空间关系误导。微调能同时提升 ChitraMiti-1k 和 NCTB-500 的性能，但与最强零样本模型仍有显著差距。

Conclusion: ChitraMiti-12.8k、NCTB-500 及评估协议为孟加拉语多模态几何推理提供了标准化研究手段，更广泛地可用于考察VLM是否真正将文本与所见内容交叉核验。数据集与代码已在 Hugging Face 公开。

Abstract: Evaluation of vision-language models (VLMs) for multimodal mathematical reasoning remains limited for low-resource languages and for geometry problems that require reading a diagram and a question together. We introduce ChitraMiti-12.8k, a synthetic benchmark of 12,874 Bengali planar geometry problems paired with structured 15-attribute descriptions, and NCTB-500, a complementary set of 500 diagrams manually extracted from Bengali school textbooks. Using a three-phase protocol that separates diagram-only, diagram-plus-description, and description-only inputs, we show across five open-weight and closed-source VLMs that description-only performance is statistically indistinguishable from diagram-plus-description performance, establishing structured descriptions as a sufficient textual proxy for controlled evaluation. Despite this, models remain poor at cross-modal verification, frequently misled by a swapped spatial relation even when they answer the unmodified item correctly. We further evaluate supervised adaptation on ChitraMiti-12.8k, finding that fine-tuning improves performance on both ChitraMiti-1k and NCTB-500, although a substantial gap to the strongest zero-shot model remains. Together, ChitraMiti-12.8k, NCTB-500, and our evaluation protocol offer a standardized way to study Bengali multimodal geometry reasoning and, more broadly, whether VLMs actually check their text against what they see. Our dataset and code are publicly available on Hugging Face at https://huggingface.co/datasets/RaiyanKhaan/ChitraMiti.

</details>


### [28] [One Skill Does Not Fit All: Automatic Discovery and Taxonomy-Guided Routing of Frame-Selection Skills for Long-Video Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12517)
*Jian Hu,Zixu Cheng,Da Li,Wei Li,Ziquan Liu,Shaogang Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AutoSkill是一种源监督框架，用于长视频问答任务中自动发现和路由可执行的帧选择技能。它仅利用未标注的问题和选项文本，从标注源池迭代生成技能，推断共享语义分类以及类别到技能的映射，无需目标视频或目标答案。在五个长视频基准上，AutoSkill使Qwen2.5-VL-7B提升2.4%、Qwen3.5-4B提升1.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数免训练方法对所有问题应用相同的帧选择策略，但不同问题类型所需的证据存在显著差异。分析表明帧选择策略的相对有效性在不同语义类别和基准间存在差异，因此需要自适应的证据获取方法。

Method: 从小型标注源池出发，LLM智能体迭代地提出、实现、评估和优化候选技能。针对目标基准，仅使用未标注的问题和选项文本来诱导共享语义分类、将标注源示例改写为目标风格、估计类别到技能的映射。推理时为每个问题分配一个技能，该技能选择冻结视频MLLM单次推理所使用的帧。

Result: 在五个长视频基准划分上，AutoSkill使Qwen2.5-VL-7B提升2.4%，Qwen3.5-4B提升1.2%，证明其有效性。

Conclusion: AutoSkill通过源监督的技能发现与类别路由器实现了面向问题类型的自适应帧选择，在免训练、免目标视频/答案的条件下显著提升长视频问答性能，验证了自适应证据获取的价值。

Abstract: Long-Video Question Answering (LVQA) requires locating decisive evidence in hour-scale videos under a limited frame budget. Most training-free methods apply the same frame-selection strategy to all questions, despite substantial variation in the evidence required by different question types. Our analysis shows that the relative effectiveness of frame-selection strategies varies across semantic categories and benchmarks, motivating adaptive evidence acquisition. In this paper, we introduce AutoSkill, a source-supervised framework for automatically discovering and routing executable frame-selection skills. Starting from a small labelled source pool, LLM agents iteratively propose, implement, evaluate, and refine candidate skills. For a target benchmark, AutoSkill uses only unlabelled question and option text to induce a shared semantic taxonomy, rewrite labelled source examples into the target style, and estimate a category-to-skill mapping. Neither target videos nor target answers are used in this process. At inference time, each question is assigned one skill, which selects the frames used in a single inference of the frozen video MLLM. Across five long-video benchmark splits, AutoSkill improves Qwen2.5-VL-7B and Qwen3.5-4B by 2.4% and 1.2%, respectively, demonstrating the effectiveness of our AutoSkill.

</details>


### [29] [Aligned Radiometric RGB-Thermal Fusion for UAV Facade Anomaly Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12521)
*Yuan Yang,Shulei Li,Haobo Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向无人机立面检测的传感器级流程，包含逐传感器校正、RGB-热图像配准、公共支撑裁剪以及对16位辐射热测量值的符号局部对比度编码。该编码保留了局部更热/更冷区域的区分，并为紧凑单流检测器提供第四输入通道。作者引入M3T数据集（674对RGB与辐射热样本，来自5个立面检测项目，覆盖8类组件与异常类别），并通过项目分组四折评估和消融实验，刻画了对齐辐射对比度在紧凑立面筛查中的类别特定收益与局限。中位残差配准误差为3.384像素，四折mAP为0.168（IoU 0.5–0.95），每图28.50 GFLOPs。消融显示分层检测较RGB-only和替代热输入有所提升，但总体精度未超过单独RGB；在RGBT-Tiny上的评估表现参差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机立面检测可将RGB与热测量结合以筛查表面和次表面异常，但传感器之间的几何差异和热图像渲染会导致空间对应模糊、削弱弱温度对比。作者旨在解决RGB与热传感器之间的空间配准误差以及弱热对比度对异常检测的干扰，从而提升立面筛查的精度与鲁棒性。

Method: 提出传感器级流水线：逐传感器校正、RGB到热的配准、公共支撑裁剪、以及对16位辐射测量的符号局部对比度编码（保留局部更热/更冷区分），作为紧凑单流检测器的第四输入通道。构建M3T数据集（674对样本、8类组件与异常类别），并采用项目分组四折评估、受控位移分析及单次划分消融进行验证。

Result: 中位残差配准误差为3.384像素；受控位移分析刻画了局部对比度响应随受控位移的变化；项目分组四折评估在IoU 0.5–0.95下获得平均精度0.168，每图计算量为28.50 GFLOPs；单次划分消融显示分层检测较RGB-only和替代热输入有提升，但总体精度未优于单独RGB；RGBT-Tiny上表现参差。

Conclusion: 结果刻画了对齐辐射对比度对紧凑立面筛查的类别特定收益与局限：分层等特定类别的检测得到改善，但总体上热通道并未显著超越单独RGB性能，说明热配准对比对部分类别有价值，却不足以全面提升聚合精度；强调需结合类别需求权衡热信息的适用性。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicle facade inspection can combine red, green, and blue (RGB) imagery with thermal measurements to screen surface and subsurface anomalies. However, geometric discrepancies between the sensors and thermal image rendering can obscure spatial correspondence and weak temperature contrasts. This article presents a sensor-level pipeline comprising per-sensor correction, RGB-to-thermal registration, common-support cropping, and signed local contrast encoding of 16-bit radiometric measurements. The encoding preserves the distinction between locally hotter and colder regions and supplies the fourth input channel of a compact single-stream detector. We introduce M3T, a dataset of 674 paired RGB and radiometric thermal samples from five facade-inspection projects covering eight component and anomaly categories. The median residual registration error is 3.384 pixels, and a controlled-displacement analysis characterizes how the local contrast response changes under controlled displacement. Project-grouped four-fold evaluation yields mean average precision of 0.168 over intersection-over-union thresholds from 0.5 to 0.95, using 28.50 billion floating-point operations per image. A separate single-split ablation shows improved delamination detection over RGB-only and alternative thermal inputs, although aggregate accuracy does not improve over RGB alone. Evaluation on RGBT-Tiny shows mixed performance with rendered thermal imagery. These results characterize the category-specific benefits and limitations of aligned radiometric contrast for compact facade screening.

</details>


### [30] [Earth-Agent-Pro: Towards Real-World Full-Chain Earth Observation with Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12533)
*Zhutao Lv,Chenhao Dang,Yi Feng,Yanpei Gong,Xiaolei Wang,Junyan Ye,Conghui He,Weijia Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Earth-Agent-Pro，一个面向真实世界地球观测（EO）任务的执行自适应"计划-执行"（Plan-and-Execute）框架。它利用专家编写的技能约束规划和运行时工具使用，并通过"工作流中心的结构化记忆"记录计划步骤、已接受证据及其依赖关系，仅在运行时证据使某一步失效时修复受影响的后续工作流。框架采用独立的大语言模型适配器：规划器通过序列级监督微调进行工作流组合，执行器通过节点级群体相对策略优化及局部可验证奖励进行工具参数接地。作者还构建了Earth-Bench-Pro基准，包含248个专家策展任务核心、744个问题及三种匹配场景，其中248个开放式执行问题涵盖RGB影像、光谱观测和遥感产品。基于共享GPT-5主干，Earth-Agent-Pro在LLM-as-Judge准确率上达到66.13%，比ReAct高出20.95个百分点，在Tools-In-Order指标上高出24.44个百分点；联合适配器调优使Qwen3.5-9B的准确率从38.31%提升至50.00%，提升11.69个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的地球观测智能体必须将高层科学问题转化为可执行工作流，以获取观测数据、准备数据、执行领域计算并从运行证据中得出结论。然而，现有的EO智能体通常从已提供的观测数据开始工作，而基准往往提供准备好的输入或候选答案，导致"全链路开放世界EO执行"在很大程度上未经过测试。这正是本文要填补的空白。

Method: 提出Earth-Agent-Pro，一个执行自适应的Plan-and-Execute框架。核心组件包括：(1) 专家编写的技能库，用于约束规划和运行时工具使用；(2) 工作流中心的结构化记忆，记录计划步骤、已接受证据及其依赖关系，当运行证据使某一步失效时只修复受影响的后续工作流片段；(3) 两个独立的大语言模型适配器——规划器采用序列级监督微调进行工作流组合，执行器采用节点级群体相对策略优化配合局部可验证奖励进行工具参数接地。同时构建Earth-Bench-Pro基准：248个专家策展任务核心、744个问题、三种匹配场景，包含248个涵盖RGB影像、光谱观测和遥感产品的开放式执行问题。

Result: 在共享GPT-5主干下，Earth-Agent-Pro的LLM-as-Judge准确率达到66.13%，比ReAct高出20.95个百分点，在Tools-In-Order指标上高出24.44个百分点。联合适配器调优使Qwen3.5-9B的LLM-as-Judge准确率从38.31%提升至50.00%，提升11.69个百分点。仅规划评估和参考工作流执行显示，适配器分别改善了工作流组合和参数接地。

Conclusion: Earth-Agent-Pro通过专家技能约束和运行时自适应修复机制，显著提升了全链路开放式EO任务的执行能力，并在多个指标上优于基线方法；适配器调优能有效增强小型模型的规划和参数接地能力。代码和数据集将公开发布。

Abstract: Real-world Earth observation (EO) agents must translate high-level scientific questions into executable workflows to acquire observations, prepare data, perform domain computations, and derive conclusions from runtime evidence. Existing EO agents typically start from supplied observations, while benchmarks typically provide prepared inputs or candidate answers, leaving full-chain open-world EO execution largely untested. We present Earth-Agent-Pro, an execution-adaptive Plan-and-Execute framework using expert-authored skills to constrain planning and runtime tool use. Workflow-centered structured memory records planned steps, accepted evidence, and their dependencies, enabling repair of only the affected workflow suffix when runtime evidence invalidates a step. Separate large language model adapters use sequence-level supervised fine-tuning for planner workflow composition and node-level group relative policy optimization with locally verifiable rewards for executor tool-argument grounding. Earth-Bench-Pro instantiates 248 expert-curated task cores as 744 questions under three matched regimes. Its 248 Open-World Execution questions span RGB imagery, spectral observations, and remote sensing products, pairing high-level requests with runtime data requirements, executable trajectories, and open-ended answers grounded in execution evidence. With a shared GPT-5 backbone, Earth-Agent-Pro achieves 66.13% LLM-as-Judge accuracy, exceeding ReAct by 20.95 points in this metric and 24.44 points in Tools-In-Order. Joint adapter tuning raises Qwen3.5-9B LLM-as-Judge accuracy from 38.31% to 50.00%, an 11.69-point gain over the untuned configuration. Planning-only evaluation and execution with the reference workflow show that the adapters improve workflow composition and argument grounding, respectively. Code and datasets will be released soon.

</details>


### [31] [RelateAnything: Real-Time Open-Vocabulary Relation Prediction From Any Inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12552)
*Maëlic Neau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RelateAnything 是一个 53M 参数的关系预测模型，它接受任意来源的图像和区域，并在推理时以字符串形式给定谓词词表，从而突破传统场景图模型对固定约 50 个谓词和标签条件化架构的限制。该模型通过 19,103 个谓词上的正-未标记监督训练，在 20ms/帧下运行，在跨数据集基准上相比同等规模的最强开放词表方法有 2.3-3.5x 的平均召回率优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放词表检测和可提示分割已使类别分类法成为输入，但关系预测仍是封闭的：场景图模型仍仅训练和评估于一种标注风格下的 50 或 56 个谓词，其关系头以物体标签为条件，因而绑定单一检测器。作者指出三个障碍：缺乏既自由文本又经核验的关系语料；标签条件化架构无法接受训练集外的词表；标准指标奖励与训练语料的一致性，使更大词表反而被视为回归。

Method: RelateAnything 将谓词词表作为文本嵌入库（而非学习到的分类器），物体标签不作为输入，因此区域来源可替换而无需重训练。训练覆盖 19,103 个谓词，采用正-未标记（PU）监督，并使用能区分反义词的文本编码器（contrastive 编码器将反义词嵌入为余弦 0.95）。作者构建 RA-4M 语料（474k 图像、4.3M 关系、10,102 个自由文本谓词），基于编号框标记生成并经几何验证。

Result: 在四个基准（含一个零样本）上，RelateAnything 的均值召回率是同等规模最强开放词表方法的 2.3-3.5 倍，优势在真实检测器下依然成立，并以不到 2% 的参数超越 3B VLM 场景图模型。此外发现域内测量会约 5 倍高估迁移收益。

Conclusion: 关系预测可以被真正开放词表化：以文本嵌入为词表、以任意区域为输入，实现在推理时接受任意谓词词表且不依赖物体标签。作者发布了模型、语料（RA-4M）和评测基准（OV-SGG-Bench，六个轴），并指出标准召回指标无法满足跨数据集的先验需求，域内指标会显著高估泛化能力。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary detection accepts any class list at inference, and promptable segmentation returns regions without class names: the taxonomy has left the model and become an input. Relation prediction has not. Scene-graph models are still trained and evaluated on the 50 or 56 predicates of one annotation style, their relation head conditioned on object labels and so tied to one detector. Three obstacles explain this, none primarily modelling: no relation corpus is both free-text and verified, a label-conditioned architecture cannot accept a vocabulary it was not trained on, and the standard metric rewards agreement with the training corpus, so a larger vocabulary scores as a regression.
