Conclusion: 该框架证明段落级文本挖掘方法能有效弥补现有整篇文档分类工具在发现和组织社会临界点证据方面的空白；模块化、开放的设计使工作流可部署且可扩展，在有标签段落上更高的准确率和kappa值表明，当段落确实包含相关证据时，模型表现尤为可靠，为气候文献证据的系统化检索与组织提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The climate literature has grown faster than review teams can read it. That gap matters most for a concept like the environmental social tipping point, the threshold at which a small change triggers rapid, self-reinforcing change in a social system. Evidence of this kind of shift is usually contained in one or two paragraphs within a longer document. As a result, existing text mining tools-which categorize entire documents by topic or highlight isolated claims-leave an expanding set of important evidence without any systematic method for discovery or organization. This paper presents an open and modular transformer-based framework that detects and structures social tipping point evidence at the passage level. The framework joins five components into a single deployable workflow: a DistilBERT boundary splitter for segmentation, an iteratively augmented RoBERTa classifier for detection, a Mistral 7B model that rewrites each detected passage for clarity, a LLaMA 3.2 3B model that rates the passage against five published social tipping point criteria, and a Milvus vector store for semantic retrieval. The system is wrapped in a Streamlit interface backed by MinIO object storage. Evaluated on a 163-passage benchmark labelled by GPT-4.1 and a 51-passage set reviewed by experts, the splitter surpassed three competing methods on a nine-metric composite score (6.137). The tuned RoBERTa model achieved 71.4 percent accuracy with a Cohen's kappa of 0.337 on the full benchmark, and 87.5 percent accuracy with a kappa of 0.742 on passages with labels, outperforming both a climate-focused model and untuned language models.

</details>


### [83] [HypoKG: Evidence-Disciplined Biomedical Hypothesis Generation Beyond Endpoint Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12260)
*Dominic Okonkwo,Adetayo Okunoye,Ismailcem Budak Arpinar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究构建了由KEGG、Rhea和UniProt三大生物数据库整合而成的新型生化知识图谱，并据此构建了包含550条路径、13,200个假设的基准数据集。通过在不同信息条件下测试六个LLM，发现模型在同时获得源酶和疾病端点时能生成得分最高的假设，但证据基础较弱；而在获得完整生物学路径时，模型生成的假设与已知机制关系更一致，这种能力被称为"证据约束推理"（evidence-disciplined reasoning）。通过打乱中间路径而保持端点不变，确认了模型确实利用了路径结构进行推理（证据基础显著下降，delta=-0.793，p<0.001）。结论表明知识图谱在假设生成中发挥双重作用：识别文献中不存在的新型生物学端点对，并通过机制路径引导LLM的推理过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM能够生成生物医学假设，但尚不清楚它们是从科学证据中真正推理，还是仅仅产生听起来令人信服的构想。本研究旨在区分这两种情况，通过系统地控制模型接收到的生物学信息量，揭示LLM假设生成过程中证据使用的真实机制。

Method: 将KEGG、Rhea和UniProt三个主要生物数据库整合为统一的生化知识图谱，构建包含550条连接酶来源到罕见病终点的路径基准，从六个LLM在四种不同信息条件下（仅有源酶、完整生物学路径、源酶和疾病终点、以及对照组）生成13,200个假设。采用专家制定的五个标准评分量表，对每条路径按1-5分制进行评分。通过打乱中间路径步骤而保持端点不变的对照实验来验证证据约束效应。

Result: 同时获得源酶和疾病端点的模型往往生成得分最高的假设，表明LLM能从极少信息中生成引人注目的构想，但这些假设与证据的关联较弱。而获得完整生物学路径的模型生成的假设与已知机制关系更一致。在打乱中间路径、保持端点不变的实验中，证据基础显著下降（delta=-0.793，p<0.001），证实模型确实在推理过程中利用了路径结构。

Conclusion: 知识图谱在支持假设生成方面具有双重作用：一是识别文献中未曾出现的生物学端点对，二是通过机制路径引导LLM在端点之间进行推理，即所谓的"证据约束推理"。这一发现为如何设计能够进行真正基于证据推理的LLM生物医学假设生成系统提供了方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate biomedical hypotheses, but it remains unclear whether they truly reason from scientific evidence or simply produce convincing-sounding ideas. To study this, we combine three major biological databases: the Kyoto Encyclopedia of Genes and Genomes (KEGG), Rhea, and UniProt, into a unified biochemical knowledge graph and construct a benchmark of 550 paths connecting enzyme sources to rare disease endpoints, yielding 13,200 hypotheses from six LLMs under four conditions varying the biological information each model receives: source enzyme only, full biological path, or source and disease endpoint only. Hypotheses are scored using an expert-derived five-criterion rubric on a 1-5 scale per criterion. We find that models given both the source and disease endpoint often produce the highest-scoring hypotheses, showing that LLMs can generate compelling ideas from minimal information. However, these hypotheses are less grounded in the evidence. In contrast, models given the full biological path generate hypotheses more consistent with known mechanistic relationships. We call this evidence-disciplined reasoning. To confirm this effect, we shuffled intermediate path steps while keeping endpoints fixed. Evidence grounding dropped significantly (delta = -0.793, p < 0.001), confirming models genuinely used path structure during reasoning. Our findings show that knowledge graphs support hypothesis generation in two ways: they identify biological endpoint pairs absent from the literature, and their mechanistic paths guide how LLMs reason between them.

</details>


### [84] [EAR: Entity-Aware Partitioning Approach for Retrieval-Augmented Generation Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12268)
*Cenab Batu Bora,Oylum Alatlı,Sebnem Bora,Oguz Dikenelli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 EAR（Entity-Aware Partitioning，实体感知分区），一种面向多项选择题问答（MCQA）的检索增强生成（RAG）语料分区方法。EAR 从问题、答案选项和语料中提取归一化的表层锚点，检索锚点周围的局部窗口，并可通过抽取式摘要附挂更大的父段落。在 153 个经自动语料支持启发式筛选的 MMLU 子集上，与固定大小分块相比，EAR 实体窗口在 top-k=3 和 top-k=8 下将检索词数减少 37.5–40.2%，但各模型上的准确率变化（+5.2/+1.3/-3.9 与 +5.9/-3.3/-4.6）均无统计显著性。其贡献聚焦于方法论层面：提供紧凑可检视的检索单元，而基于规则的锚点抽取器仍具领域特异性，迁移前需单独验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG 能提升知识密集型问答，但最首要的设计选择——如何将源语料划分为可检索单元——常被忽视。固定大小的分块往往返回与问题关系仅隐含的长段落，检索效率与相关性均不理想，因此需要一种更紧凑、与实体对齐的检索单元划分方式。

Method: EAR 的核心流程为：从问题、答案选项和语料中抽取归一化的表层锚点（surface anchors）；检索匹配锚点周围的局部窗口作为检索单元；可选地通过抽取式摘要将更大的父段落附加到检索结果上，从而在紧凑性与上下文完整性之间取得平衡。评估采用清洗后的 MMLU 风格子集（153 题，由自动语料支持启发式筛选），并使用去污染的公开教科书文本作为语料。

Result: 在相同协议下对 top-k=3 与 top-k=8 分别使用 Mistral、Gemma 和 DeepSeek 扫描，EAR 实体窗口相对分块减少了 37.5–40.2% 的检索词数。准确率变化在 top-k=3 下为 +5.2、+1.3、-3.9 分，在 top-k=8 下为 +5.9、-3.3、-4.6 分；所有实体窗口差异均未达统计显著。

Conclusion: 该工作的贡献被限定为方法论层面：EAR 提供了一种紧凑且可检视（inspectable）的检索单元，有利于检索结果的可解释性与效率。但其基于规则的锚点抽取器具有领域特异性，在迁移到其他领域或任务前需要单独验证。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) can improve knowledge-intensive question answering, but the first design choice is easy to overlook: how should the source corpus be partitioned into retrievable units? Fixed-size chunks often return long passages whose relation to the question is only implicit. We introduce EAR, an Entity-Aware Partitioning approach for multiple-choice question answering (MCQA). EAR extracts normalized surface anchors from the question, answer options, and corpus; retrieves local windows around matching corpus anchors; and can attach a larger parent passage through an extractive summary. We evaluate EAR on a cleaned Massive Multitask Language Understanding (MMLU)-style subset of 153 questions selected by an automatic corpus-support heuristic and using decontaminated public textbook text. Across same-protocol top-k = 3 and top-k = 8 sweeps with Mistral, Gemma, and DeepSeek, EAR entity-window reduces retrieved words by 37.5-40.2% relative to chunks. Observed accuracy changes are +5.2, +1.3, and -3.9 points at top-k = 3, and +5.9, -3.3, and -4.6 points at top-k = 8; none of the entity-window differences is statistically significant. The scoped contribution is methodological: EAR provides a compact and inspectable retrieval unit, while its rule-based anchor extractor remains domain-specific and requires separate validation before transfer.

</details>


### [85] [Breaking the Token Ceiling: Distilling Smaller, Stronger Byte Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12303)
*Kalyani Marathe,Artidoro Pagnoni,Tomasz Limisiewicz,Margaret Li,Mike Lewis,Luke Zettlemoyer,Srinivasan Iyer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次在大规模（约10亿参数、最高1万亿字节数据）条件下系统比较了蒸馏的字节模型与词元模型在缩放趋势上的差异。提出了两种将词元logits转换为字节logits的方法：近似法Marginalize-It和精确法End-Of-Token。发现Token-1B在低算力（FLOP）区段占优但会饱和，而字节模型虽然起步较差但随计算量增加最终超越词元模型，达到更高的下游任务性能上限。外推缩放定律预测蒸馏的End-Of-Token-1B渐进地比蒸馏Token-1B高出4%，且仅需六分之一训练数据即可匹配其性能，同时将logits存储成本降至约五分之一。此外这些模型无需top-k截断，并预测其渐进超越Llama 3.2-1B、Gemma-3-1B-pt和Gemma 2B模型分别达6.5%、8.1%和2.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识蒸馏普遍假设小模型与更大教师模型共享词元化（tokenization）方案，但字节模型与词元模型在训练数据扩展和计算扩展下的缩放行为是否一致尚无大规模实证。作者希望回答：当计算与数据增加时，蒸馏的字节模型与词元模型在缩放趋势上表现是否相同，从而为选择更高效、数据利用率更高的蒸馏范式提供依据。

Method: 提出两种将词元logits高效转换为字节logits的变体：1）近似法"Marginalize-It"；2）精确法"End-Of-Token"（EOT）。随后开展首个大规模"过度训练"研究，同时变化两个维度：词元化方案（Tokens、Bytes、Bytes w/ eot）与训练目标（蒸馏 vs 交叉熵），在层参数匹配的前提下扫描约10亿参数模型并喂入最高1万亿字节数据。评测覆盖八个基准、三个类别（多项选择QA、语言生成、机器翻译），并建立top-1误差与验证BPB的缩放定律进行外推。

Result: 在低FLOP区段Token-1B优于两种字节模型，但随后达到平台期；字节模型（End-Of-Token-1B与Bytes-1B）起步较差，却在更多算力下反超词元模型，获得更高下游性能上限。缩放定律外推显示蒸馏的End-Of-Token-1B渐进优于蒸馏Token-1B达4%，且数据效率更高——用六分之一训练数据即可匹配Token-1B性能。字节模型采用仅256字节的小词表，规避了logits倾倒时的top-k截断需求，并将logits存储成本降至约五分之一。下游性能缩放定律预测这些模型渐进超越Llama 3.2-1B、Gemma-3-1B-pt和Gemma 2B分别达6.5%、8.1%和2.1%。

Conclusion: 蒸馏的字节模型（尤其是End-Of-Token变体）虽然在小算力预算下不及词元模型，但在更大算力下展现出更高的性能天花板与显著的数据效率优势，并对logits存储、top-k截断等方面带来实用收益。研究为未来大规模蒸馏模型选择字节级词元化与End-Of-Token目标提供了强有力的缩放实证依据。

Abstract: Small models are made more capable through distillation from a larger one that shares their tokenization scheme. However, do distilled byte and token models behave similarly in terms of scaling trends as compute and data increases? To enable this comparison, we introduce two variants to efficiently convert token logits to Byte Logits: 1) approximate: Marginalize-It, and 2) exact: End-Of-Token. We then present the first large scale study of overtraining decoder-only dense transformer models varying two dimensions simultaneously: the tokenization scheme (Tokens, Bytes, Bytes w/ eot) and the training objective (Distillation vs. Cross-Entropy), sweeping layer-parameter-matched models with roughly 1 billion parameters up to 1 trillion bytes of data. Across eight benchmarks spanning three categories: Multiple Choice QA, Language Generation, and Machine Translation, we find that Token-1B models outperform byte models (End-Of-Token-1B and Bytes-1B) in the low-FLOP regime but eventually plateau; byte models start worse yet surpass Token-1B models with more compute, reaching a higher downstream task performance ceiling. Extrapolating the average top-1 error vs. validation BPB scaling laws predicts that, asymptotically, distilled End-Of-Token-1B outperforms distilled Token-1B by up to 4%. They are also far more data efficient, matching the performance of distilled Token-1B using only one-sixth of the training data. Moreover, by operating over a small vocabulary of 256 bytes instead of on the order of 100K tokens, they circumvent the need for top-k truncation during logit dumping, while also reducing logit storage costs to roughly one-fifth. Finally, our downstream performance scaling laws predict that our distilled End-Of-Token-1B models asymptotically surpass the Llama 3.2-1B, Gemma-3-1B-pt, and Gemma 2B models on averaged downstream tasks by up to 6.5%, 8.1%, and 2.1%, respectively.

</details>


### [86] [ESTS at WMT26: Routing-Informed Expert Pruning for Model Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12310)
*Liu O. Martin,Lucas Bandarkar,Nanyun Peng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文描述了ESTS团队在WMT26模型压缩共享任务中的六份提交（针对英-简体中文和英-埃及阿拉伯语两个方向）。所有压缩方案均基于GPT-OSS-20B，采用任务特定路由质量对专家排序、跨语言路由发散度跨层分配保留容量，并物理移除低重要性专家；随后在GPT-5.1生成的合成翻译数据上对专家进行恢复微调，再对保留的专家投影权重应用MXFP4量化。同时实现了适用于指令条件WMT26设置的稳健推理系统（含类别推断、输出验证、重试、分段回退和源所有JSON重建）。六份提交参数量为4.186B至7.770B，打包产物大小为4.55至6.33 GiB，内部采用GPT-5.1伪参考的xCOMET-XL评估对各压缩操作点进行比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在WMT26模型压缩共享任务中，需要在不显著损失翻译质量的前提下大幅压缩大规模翻译模型（20B级），以满足部署时的存储和推理资源约束。作者旨在探索基于MoE架构的高效压缩方法，通过专家剪枝与量化相结合，在多个压缩操作点上实现性能和规模的平衡。

Method: 方法包含三阶段：1) 基于任务特定路由质量对MoE专家进行重要性排序，并利用跨语言路由发散度在各层间分配保留容量，随后物理剪除低重要性专家（得到"专家化"模型）；2) 在GPT-5.1生成的合成翻译数据上对剪枝后的专家进行恢复微调（recovery-tuning）；3) 对保留的专家投影权重应用MXFP4量化进一步压缩。此外构建了针对指令条件WMT26设置的稳健推理系统，涵盖类别推断、输出验证、重试机制、分段回退及源所有JSON重建等模块。

Result: 针对两个翻译方向各提交三个压缩操作点，参数量范围4.186B至7.770B，打包产物规模4.55至6.33 GiB，相比源模型实现了约64%-79%的参数压缩。内部评测采用GPT-5.1伪参考配合xCOMET-XL，对六个提交的压缩操作点进行了横向比较。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了基于路由感知的专家剪枝（结合跨语言路由信息）与MXFP4量化组合在高压缩比下保持翻译能力的技术路线，并展示了面向生产级指令条件翻译场景的完整压缩与推理方案。六个不同压缩操作点为模型压缩研究提供了可复现的系统性比较基线。

Abstract: We describe six submissions under the team name ESTS to the unconstrained WMT26 Model Compression Shared Task for English--Simplified Chinese and English--Egyptian Arabic. We submit three compression operating points per translation direction, all derived from GPT-OSS-20B. We use task-specific routing mass to rank experts and cross-lingual routing divergence to allocate retained capacity across layers, then physically remove low-importance experts. The resulting specialists are recovery-tuned on GPT-5.1-generated synthetic translation data and further compressed by applying MXFP4 quantization to the retained expert projection weights. We additionally implement a robust inference system for the instruction-conditioned WMT26 setting, including category inference, output validation, retries, segmented fallback, and source-owned JSON reconstruction. Across our six submissions, parameter counts range from 4.186B to 7.770B and packed artifact sizes from 4.55 to 6.33~GiB. Internal xCOMET-XL evaluation using GPT-5.1 pseudo-references provides an internal comparison across the submitted compression operating points.

</details>


### [87] [I Am No One: Style-Aware Paraphrasing for Text Anonymization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12341)
*Ahmed Sohair Khan,Estrid He,Monica Wachowicz,Elham Naghizade*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于预训练大语言模型的风格感知、提示驱动的文本匿名化方法，通过构造紧凑的风格画像并重写文本，在保持内容质量和可读性的同时将作者归属F1分数降低60-70%，显著优于基于差分隐私的基线方法，从而降低作者重新识别的隐私风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者归属模型能从看似匿名化的文本中通过稳定的风格指纹重新识别用户，即便显式标识符已被移除，这对文本发布和数据分析构成日益增长的隐私风险。该风险同样存在于语音派生文本（如会议和客服对话的ASR转录），即使经过声学匿名化，风格泄漏仍可能持续。而基于差分隐私的匿名化往往会严重损害文本质量和实用性。

Method: 提出一种风格感知的、提示驱动的匿名化方法，利用预训练大语言模型从最小样本中构造紧凑的风格画像，并重写文本以抑制可识别的风格标记，同时保持语义内容不丢失。

Result: 在博客和评论数据集上，该方法将作者归属F1分数降低60-70%，同时保持内容质量和可读性，显著优于基于差分隐私和非差分隐私的基线方法。

Conclusion: 实验证明，基于LLM的风格感知重写方法能够在保障内容质量的同时有效削弱风格指纹，为文本隐私保护提供了一条优于DP方案的可行路径。

Abstract: Authorship attribution models can re-identify users from seemingly anonymized text by exploiting stable stylistic fingerprints, even after explicit identifiers are removed, posing a growing privacy risk for text publishing and analytics. This risk extends to speech-derived text such as ASR transcripts of meetings and call-center conversations, where stylometric leakage can persist even after acoustic anonymization. Differential privacy-based anonymization often severely degrades text quality and utility. We propose a style-aware, prompt-driven anonymization approach that uses pretrained large language models to construct compact stylistic profiles from minimal samples and rewrite text to suppress identifiable style markers while preserving meaning. Across blog and review datasets, our approach reduces authorship attribution F1 by 60-70% while maintaining content quality and readability, substantially outperforming DP-based and non-DP baselines.

</details>


### [88] [SynthSentry: Detecting Synthetic Data Contamination in Language Model Training Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12353)
*Praveen Kumar Myakala,Ravichandra Namburi,Sowmya Keragodu Jayaramu,Sooraj George Thomas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SynthSentry 是一种针对训练前语料库的、与模型无关的合成污染筛查信号，通过三个统计量（词汇多样性坍缩、n-gram 尾部截断、参考模型困惑度方差）计算分布散度，无需访问生成模型或合成标签，即可根据污染严重度对语料排序，并以校准后的阈值控制假阳性预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在基于自身或他人输出递归训练时会经历"模型坍缩"（分布尾部和事实准确性退化，流畅性保留）。既有研究多为训练后诊断，而可操作的问题在于训练前筛查来源不明的语料。因此需要一种在训练前端识别合成污染的手段。

Method: 提出 SynthSentry：一种语料级、模型无关的污染信号。核心分数是三个统计量上的分布散度——词汇多样性坍缩、n-gram 尾部截断、跨参考模型的困惑度方差。无需访问生成模型、生成历史或合成标签。使用留一生成器协议评估，并在领域分层研究中测量自然重复人类文本（法律、临床、源代码）上的假阳性。通过协方差收缩与 bootstrap 阈值（替代朴素分位数）实现按领域校准，使假阳性接近名义预算。

Result: 分数能在几乎不损失的情况下按严重度对语料排序，即使完全留出整个生成器家族也能保持性能。逐领域校准在采用协方差收缩与 bootstrap 阈值后接近名义假阳性预算，而朴素分位数会超过预算四倍。下游微调检查未见污染导致的准确性缺陷；但一旦修剪超过真实污染比例会出现过度修剪风险。

Conclusion: 将筛查定位为数据策展的防御性手段而非事后诊断，并发布评分工具包。研究规模较小、范围限定于英文批量语料筛查；污染源为单次生成或人工撰写而非递归生成，故结论针对一般性合成污染而言，不涉及递归深度。

Abstract: Large language models trained recursively on their own or other models' outputs undergo model collapse, in which distributional tails and factual accuracy deteriorate while fluency survives. Prior work diagnoses collapse after training; the actionable problem is screening a corpus of unknown provenance before training. We introduce SynthSentry, a corpus-level, model-agnostic contamination signal requiring no access to the generating model, no generation history, and no synthetic labels. The score is a distributional divergence over three statistics: lexical diversity collapse, n-gram tail truncation, and perplexity variance across reference models. We evaluate on corpora contaminated by small open-weight generators and an instruction-tuned open-weight model under a leave-one-generator-out protocol. A domain-stratified study measures false positives on naturally repetitive human text (legal, clinical, source code). The score ranks corpora by severity with little loss when whole generator families are held out. Per-domain calibration holds near its nominal false-positive budget once covariance shrinkage and a bootstrap threshold replace a naive quantile, which runs four times over budget. A downstream fine-tuning check showed no contamination-driven accuracy deficit at our scale, so whether pruning recovers one remains open; the same run shows over-pruning risk once pruning exceeds the true contamination fraction. We frame screening as a data-curation defense rather than a post-hoc diagnosis and release the scoring toolkit. All results are small-scale; scope is English-language, batch-mode corpus screening. Contamination sources are single-generation or hand-authored rather than recursively generated, so results speak to synthetic contamination generally and not to recursion depth.

</details>


### [89] [CueMem: Cue-Guided Context Reconstruction for Long-Term Conversational Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12354)
*Changjian Wang,Rongzhen Li,Weili Guan,Shuming Shi,Quan Lu,Ning Jiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CueMem，一个基于线索引导的框架，用于长时对话问答。它将抽取的记忆记录视为检索线索而非自包含证据，通过线索回溯到源对话轮，并利用图结构（时序邻近+语义相关）在图谱上扩展以重构与查询相关的原始对话上下文，供LLM生成答案。在LoCoMo和LongMemEval上一致优于代表性长时记忆基线，并能恢复支撑性对话证据、降低查询时输入token和延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时对话智能体需要从扩展的对话历史中回忆信息来回答用户查询，但直接使用完整历史成本高昂且往往不可靠；而压缩式记忆单元可能丢失问答所需的细粒度证据。作者借鉴自传体记忆的重构性观点，提出记忆应作为检索线索而非自包含证据这一核心动机。

Method: CueMem框架包含两阶段：(1)记忆构建阶段：从对话轮中抽取细粒度记忆线索，并将每条线索链接到其源对话轮；(2)查询阶段：检索与查询相关的线索，映射到源对话轮锚点，并在捕捉时序邻近性与语义相关性的对话轮图谱上从锚点进行扩张，重构出紧凑的、来自原始对话的证据上下文，供LLM生成答案。

Result: 在LoCoMo和LongMemEval数据集上，CueMem一致优于代表性的长时记忆基线方法。进一步分析表明，基于图谱的上下文重构能帮助恢复支撑性的对话证据，同时相比完整历史的LLM设置，减少了查询时的输入token和延迟。

Conclusion: 结果凸显了检索线索作为长时对话问答中自包含记忆证据的有效替代方案，验证了重构式记忆方法在平衡证据保真度与推理效率方面的价值。

Abstract: Long-term conversational agents must answer user queries by recalling information from extended dialogue histories, yet directly using the full history is costly and often unreliable, while compressed memory units may lose fine-grained evidence needed for question answering. Motivated by the reconstructive view of autobiographical memory, we propose CueMem, a cue-guided framework that treats extracted memory records as retrieval cues rather than self-contained evidence and reconstructs query-relevant dialogue context from their source turns. During memory construction, CueMem extracts fine-grained memory cues from dialogue turns and links each cue to its source turn. At query time, it retrieves query-relevant cues, maps them to source-turn anchors, and expands from these anchors over a turn graph that captures temporal proximity and semantic relatedness, reconstructing a compact evidence context from the original dialogue for LLM answer generation. Experiments on LoCoMo and LongMemEval show that CueMem consistently outperforms representative long-term memory baselines. Further analyses show that graph-based context reconstruction helps recover supporting dialogue evidence while reducing query-time input tokens and latency compared with the full-history LLM setting. These results highlight retrieval cues as an effective alternative to self-contained memory evidence for long-term conversational question answering.

</details>


### [90] [ORQA: An Occupation-Realistic Question and Answer Framework for LLM Professional Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12366)
*Shreyas Krishnan,Serina Chang,Abhishek Nagaraj*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ORQA方法，通过将O*NET职业与可信的职业特定网站（如监管机构、执照机构、专业组织和政府出版物）关联，并将这些内容转化为可追溯来源的问答对，来测试大语言模型在职业层面的知识。该方法结合自动化流水线与人工审核，覆盖SOC全部21个职业大类的116个职业，共480个问题来自187个网站。测试15个前沿和开源模型发现，Claude Opus 4.6、GPT-5.4和Claude Sonnet 4.6表现最佳（约58-62%），小型开源模型约33-41%，且性能在不同职业间差异显著（医疗保健类最高78%，办公室与行政支持约40%，部分职业如钣金工和渔猎管理员性能几乎为零）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么通过任务定义将抽象LLM技能映射到职业，要么依赖难以规模化获取且成本高昂的专家知识。作者希望提供一种可扩展、低成本且基于可信来源的方法来评估LLM在职业领域的知识与能力，从而弥补这两种方法的不足。

Method: ORQA将O*NET职业与值得信赖的职业特定网站相关联（包括监管机构、执照机构、专业组织和政府出版物），并将这些内容转换为可追溯来源的问答对。采用自动化流水线与人工审核相结合的流程生成高质量问题，每个问题旨在探测与该职业相关的真实世界技能问题。数据集覆盖SOC全部21个主要职业组中的116个职业，共480个问题，来源于187个不同网站。

Result: 对15个前沿及开源重量模型进行测试：Claude Opus 4.6、GPT-5.4和Claude Sonnet 4.6并列最佳，准确率约58-62%；较小开源模型约33-41%。性能在不同职业间差异显著：医疗保健相关职业最高（78%），办公室与行政支持类约40%，而如钣金工和渔猎管理员等个别职业性能几乎为零。开放式问题和按工资总额加权均不会显著改变模型的排名。

Conclusion: 利用现有的可信职业特定信息来测试LLM在专业领域的知识是一项可扩展且有价值的评估方法，有望成为未来评估职业层面AI性能的有效途径。相关结果和数据已发布于orqabench.org。

Abstract: We present ORQA, a method for testing occupation-level knowledge in large language models. Prior methods either map abstract LLM skills to occupations via task definitions or utilize expert knowledge which is difficult to obtain at scale and expensive. ORQA complements both of these methods by connecting O*NET occupations to trusted occupation-specific websites (such as regulatory agencies, licensing bodies, professional organizations, and government publications) and converting these into source-traceable question-answer pairs. A combination of an automated pipeline and human review produces a set of high quality questions about occupations. The question set created via our method covers 116 occupations from all 21 major groups in the SOC, with 480 questions sourced from 187 different websites. Each question is designed to probe a real-world skill question that is relevant to the occupation in question. We test 15 state-of-the-art frontier and open-weight models via this method. Claude Opus 4.6, GPT-5.4 and Claude Sonnet 4.6 all perform the best at approximately 58-62% while smaller open-weight models achieve approximately 33-41% performance. Performance varies significantly across occupations. Healthcare-related occupations achieve the highest performance (78%) while Office and Administrative Support achieve approximately 40%. Performance on individual occupations (e.g. Sheet Metal Workers and Fish and Game Wardens) is essentially zero. We also find that open-ended questions and weighting by wage bill do not significantly affect the ranking of models on this benchmark. We believe that leveraging existing trusted occupation-specific information to test LLM knowledge in professional domains may be a scalable and useful method for evaluating occupation-level AI performance in the future. Results and data are available at orqabench.org.

</details>


### [91] [Representation-based Masked Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12382)
*Yangrong Hu,Ding Huang,Xueyu Zhou,Jian Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出基于表示学习的掩码扩散模型（RMDM），通过预训练编码器将文本编码为连续语义空间，并学习可逆变换将表示分布归一化为高斯先验，以该潜在语义表示作为全局语义引导来协调并行token更新，从而解决现有掩码扩散模型并行采样中独立更新导致的缺乏全局协调和输出不连贯问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码扩散模型（MDMs）已成为语言建模的有力范式，支持高效的并行文本生成。然而现有并行采样方法通常独立更新多个掩码token，忽略了掩码token之间的复杂相互依赖关系，这种独立更新机制缺乏全局协调，可能导致输出不连贯。作者旨在通过显式编码全局语义来克服这一局限。

Method: RMDM框架包含三个步骤：(1) 利用预训练编码器将文本编码为连续语义空间；(2) 学习一个可逆变换，将表示分布归一化为高斯先验，以便在生成过程中高效采样；(3) 以该潜在语义表示为条件，训练掩码扩散模型学习条件文本分布，其中表示充当全局语义引导来协调并行token更新并忠实逼近目标分布。

Result: 实验结果表明，RMDM显著提升了生成质量，尤其在激进的少步采样（few-step sampling）场景下表现突出。

Conclusion: RMDM通过引入全局语义表示作为协调机制，有效弥补了掩码扩散模型并行采样中因token独立更新而导致的全局协调缺失问题，在少步采样下显著改善生成质量，为高效并行的文本生成提供了新思路。

Abstract: Masked Diffusion Models (MDMs) have emerged as a compelling paradigm for language modeling, offering the capability for efficient parallel text generation. However, existing parallel sampling methods typically update multiple masked tokens independently and ignore the complex mutual dependencies among the masked tokens. This independent updating mechanism lacks global coordination and might lead to incoherent outputs. To address this limitation, we propose Representation-based Masked Diffusion Model (RMDM), a framework that leverages the text representation to explicitly encode global semantics and help to parallel update tokens more precisely. Specifically, we first encode text into a continuous semantic space using a pretrained encoder and learn an invertible transformation that normalizes the representation distribution to a Gaussian prior, facilitating efficient sampling during generation. Conditioned on this latent semantic representation, we train a masked diffusion model to learn the conditional text distribution, where the representation serves as global semantic guidance to coordinate parallel token updates and faithfully approximate the target distribution. Empirical results demonstrate that RMDM significantly improves generation quality, particularly in aggressive few-step sampling regimes.

</details>


### [92] [GraphProfiler: Source-Linked Sensitive Attribute Inference via Personal Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12448)
*Ahmed Sohair Khan,Estrid He,Chenglong Ma,Monica Wachowicz,Elham Naghizade*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出GraphProfiler，一种可审计的基于LLM的用户画像系统。它通过将用户的帖子历史构建为带来源链接的个人知识图谱（节点和边追溯到原始帖子），将属性预测解析为被引用的图谱记录和源文本，从而在保持高攻击成功率（SynthPAI上86.7%，PANDORA上84.6%）的同时，为超过98%的预测提供支持证据，实现针对性的隐私缓解（仅删除或改写真正泄露属性的帖子）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM基于大量普通帖子中的间接线索聚合可以高精度推断年龄、收入、职业等敏感属性，构成大规模隐私威胁。然而现有基于LLM的画像工具无法说明哪些具体帖子、概念和关系促成了推断，这阻碍了针对性的隐私缓解——仅改写少数真正泄露属性的帖子而非扰动整个历史记录。因此需要对画像过程的可审计性和证据溯源进行改进。

Method: GraphProfiler将每位用户的帖子历史表示为带来源链接的个人知识图谱，其中节点和边均追溯到其来源帖子。系统将属性预测解析（resolve）到被引用的图谱记录和源文本，从而提供可审计的推断路径。通过受控消融实验（删除被引用帖子与随机删除同等数量帖子对比）验证被引用帖子对攻击成功的因果贡献。

Result: 在八个属性的SynthPAI基准上达到86.7%的攻击成功率，仅比强文本基线低两个百分点；在PANDORA上达到84.6%；超过98%的预测提供了支撑证据。消融实验表明，移除被引用的帖子比移除同等数量的随机帖子能更大幅度地降低攻击成功率，证明被引用帖子确实对攻击成功有贡献。

Conclusion: GraphProfiler在保持与纯文本基线相当的高攻击成功率的同时，实现了对用户属性推断的可审计性和证据溯源能力，为针对性的隐私缓解（精确的帖子删除或改写）提供了基础，在隐私保护与画像精度之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Sensitive attributes such as age, income, and occupation can be inferred from user-generated content by aggregating indirect cues across many ordinary posts. LLM-based profilers can perform this aggregation automatically and with high accuracy, which makes large-scale personal attribute inference a major privacy threat. Existing LLM-based profilers, however, offer limited insight into which specific posts, concepts, and relationships made an inference possible, which is key to targeted privacy mitigation, i.e., redacting or rewriting only the few posts that actually leak an attribute, rather than perturbing entire histories. We introduce GraphProfiler, an auditable LLM-based profiler that represents each user's post history as a source-linked personal knowledge graph where nodes and edges trace back to the originating post and resolves attribute predictions to cited graph records and source texts. GraphProfiler reaches 86.7% attack success rate on the eight-attribute SynthPAI benchmark, within two points of strong text-only baselines, and 84.6% on PANDORA, while citing supporting evidence for over 98% of predictions. Our controlled ablation experiments provide evidence that the cited posts contribute to attack success, as removing them reduces the attack success rate substantially more than removing an equal number of random posts.

</details>


### [93] [Not All Speech Is Intent: Adaptive Self-Correcting Inference Layer for Post-ASR False Wake-Up](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12469)
*Preeti Saraswat,Divya Neelagiri,Anil Yadav*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出反馈驱动的自适应自纠错推理层（ASCIL），一种互补的ASR后纠错框架，用于解决对话式AI中由语音相似唤词引发的错误唤醒激活问题。ASCIL通过融合声学嵌入、语言线索、设备上下文和过往误分类模式，在响应生成前重新评估唤醒意图，并利用隐式（犹豫、脱离、沉默）与显式（取消、重复）行为信号进行在线模式更新，无需人工标注。在包含3,667次交互、14种声学与上下文条件的人标注数据集上，ASCIL在会话不相交子集上实现54.27%相对错误率降低，在阈值0.90的问题标注切片上实现最高24.39%相对错误率降低，同时改善有意接受率，中位附加延迟低于60ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 错误唤醒激活是对话式AI中持续存在的挑战：与唤醒词语音相似的语音可产生语法有效且语义连贯的ASR转录，导致助手错误执行。现有系统大多在孤立环境中做单一意图决策，缺乏从反复错误中学习和针对个体用户进行个性化适配的机制，因此无法持续降低误唤醒率。

Method: ASCIL是一个互补的ASR后纠错框架，在响应生成前通过融合声学嵌入、语言线索、设备上下文和过往误分类模式重新评估唤醒意图。它将犹豫、脱离、沉默等隐式信号以及取消、重复等显式信号视为自动推断的噪声行为误分类指标，驱动无需人工标注的在线模式更新；而用于离线评估的故意/非故意参考标签则由人工标注。框架能从先前错误中泛化，在推理时应用纠错调整，并与自然语言执行并行持续更新。

Result: 在包含3,667次交互、具有人工标注故意/非故意参考标签并覆盖14种声学与上下文条件的专有数据集上，ASCIL在由基线失败构建的会话不相交子集上实现了54.27%的相对错误率降低，在阈值0.90的问题标注评估切片上实现最高24.39%的相对错误率降低。这些收益在提升故意接受率的同时实现，报告基准中位附加延迟低于60ms。

Conclusion: ASCIL通过在线自适应学习和推理时纠错，在降低错误唤醒率的同时提升有意唤醒接受率，且附加延迟极低（中位<60ms），证明该互补框架能有效克服固定单一决策系统的局限，为对话式AI的误唤醒问题提供了可持续改进与个性化适配的解决方案。

Abstract: False wake-up activations remain a persistent challenge in conversational AI. Speech phonetically similar to a device's wake word can produce a syntactically valid and semantically coherent ASR transcript that the assistant incorrectly executes. Most existing systems make a single intent decision in isolation, without a mechanism to learn from recurring errors over time or adapt to individual users through personalized learning. We introduce the Feedback-Driven Adaptive Self-Correcting Inference Layer (ASCIL), a complementary post-ASR correction framework that re-evaluates wake-up intent before response generation by fusing acoustic embeddings, linguistic cues, device context, and patterns from past misclassifications. ASCIL interprets implicit signals, including hesitation, disengagement, and silence, and explicit signals, including cancellation and repetition, as automatically inferred, noisy behavioral indicators of potential misclassification. These signals drive online pattern updates without manual annotation, whereas the intentional/unintentional reference labels used for offline evaluation are human-annotated. It generalizes from prior errors, applies corrective adjustments at inference time, and continuously updates in parallel with natural-language execution. Evaluated on a proprietary dataset of 3,667 interactions with human-annotated intentional/unintentional reference labels spanning 14 acoustic and contextual conditions, ASCIL achieves 54.27% relative error reduction on a session-disjoint subset constructed from baseline failures, and up to 24.39% relative error reduction at threshold 0.90 on the issue-tagged evaluation slice. These gains are achieved while improving intentional acceptance rates, with a median added latency below 60 ms in the reported benchmark.

</details>


### [94] [AMDKernelVault: Large-Scale Datasets and Agentic Training for AMD GPU Kernel Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12471)
*Ji Liu,Saptarshi Majumder,Yiqing Huang,Wenwen Ouyang,Umang Pandey,Zeping Li,Chushi Chen,Zihao An,Puyuan Yang,Zekai Li,Sina Rafati,Ziqiong Liu,Pratik Prabhanjan Brahma,Dong Li,Zicheng Liu,Sharon Zhou,Emad Barsoum*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了 AMDKernelVault，一个面向 AMD CDNA GPU 的开源 HIP 与 Triton 内核语料库及训练框架。针对现有 LLM 内核代理以 CUDA/NVIDIA 为中心的问题，作者构建了 HIPKernelGen 与 TritonKernelGen 两条智能体驱动的流水线，将 PyTorch 参考代码转换为 HIP/Triton 内核，并在 ROCm 环境下编译验证与延迟评测。语料库包含 62,153 个经执行验证的 HIP 内核样本、2,377 条生产级 ROCm 库 QA 记录及 39,893 个 Triton 内核。作者还基于该语料对 Qwen3-8B 进行监督微调与执行感知强化学习，在固定评测预算下，该模型在 PyTorch-to-HIP（34.0% Pass@1）、TritonBench-G（33.2% Corr@3）和 ROCmBench（41.94% Corr@3）上取得最高正确率，但在编译通过率与速度指标上并非全面领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 LLM 的自动内核生成代理高度依赖 CUDA/NVIDIA 生态，缺乏面向 AMD 平台的生成、反思与优化工具链；同时缺少大规模、经执行验证的 AMD HIP/Triton 内核语料库，导致针对 AMD CDNA GPU 的自动化内核生成研究受限。

Method: 构建 AMDKernelVault 语料库与训练框架。核心方法包括：(1) 设计 HIPKernelGen 与 TritonKernelGen 两条智能体驱动流水线，将 PyTorch 参考实现生成 HIP 或 Triton 内核，在 ROCm 下编译与验证，并在 AMD 硬件上进行延迟剖析；(2) 收集并构建含 62,153 个执行验证 HIP 样本、2,377 条 ROCm 库 QA 记录和 39,893 个 Triton 内核的语料库；(3) 对 Qwen3-8B 进行监督微调（SFT）与执行感知强化学习（execution-aware RL）训练，以验证语料库的可用性。

Result: 在固定评测预算下，训练后的模型在 PyTorch-to-HIP 上取得 34.0% Pass@1，在 TritonBench-G 上取得 33.2% Corr@3，在 ROCmBench 上取得 41.94% Corr@3，均为对比模型中的最高正确率；但在编译通过率与内核运行速度等指标上并未全面领先。

Conclusion: AMDKernelVault 填补了 AMD GPU 内核生成语料与训练框架的空白，证明基于大规模执行验证语料训练的模型能在正确性上取得领先，但在编译与性能优化维度仍有提升空间；相关语料与代码已开源，为 AMD 生态的 LLM 内核生成研究提供了基础设施。

Abstract: We introduce AMDKernelVault, an open HIP and Triton kernel corpus and training framework for recent AMD CDNA GPUs. Existing LLM-based kernel agents are largely CUDA/NVIDIA-centric and often depend on repeated frontier-LLM calls for generation, reflection, and optimization. To address this gap, we develop HIPKernelGen and TritonKernelGen, agent-driven pipelines that transform PyTorch references into HIP or Triton kernels, compile and validate candidates under ROCm, and latency-profile them on AMD hardware. The corpus contains 62,153 execution-verified HIP kernel samples, 2,377 production-grounded ROCm Libraries QA entries, and 39,893 Triton kernels. We further train Qwen3-8B with supervised fine-tuning and execution-aware reinforcement learning as a demonstration of the corpus's utility. Under fixed evaluation budgets, it achieves the highest correctness among the compared models on PyTorch-to-HIP (34.0% Pass@1), TritonBench-G (33.2% Corr@3), and ROCmBench (41.94% Corr@3), but does not uniformly lead compilation or speed metrics. The corpus and documentation are available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/amd/AIG-Datasets, and the associated training and kernel-generation code is available at https://github.com/AMD-AGI/hip_kernel_llm_lab.

</details>


### [95] [Zipbench: Low-Cost Framework for Compressing Comprehensive Benchmarks of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12475)
*Zhongzhan Huang,Junxin Li,Guoming Ling,Yupei Lin,Shanshan Zhong,Hefeng Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ZipBench 是一种低成本、简单且具有理论误差和排名一致性保证的基准压缩方法(BCM)。它仅评估少量锚定LLM，通过合成伪评估结果来扩大覆盖面，学习紧凑的样本表示，并选择小而有代表性的子集。基于此构建的 ZipBench Zoo 包含覆盖文本、多模态和智能体任务的100多个基准代理，平均绝对误差仅0.002-0.02，平均Spearman相关系数约0.98。该方法显著降低了LLM评估和紧凑基准构建的成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多广泛使用的LLM基准存在冗余，导致评估成本不必要地昂贵。虽然现有的基准压缩方法(BCM)可以缓解成本问题，但很多强力的BCM需要依赖大量LLM的海量逐样本评估结果来识别代表性样本，而构建这样的评估集合本身就很昂贵，除非这些结果已经公开，这使得这些方法难以扩展到新发布的基准上。因此需要一个低成本、易于扩展的基准压缩方案。

Method: ZipBench 的核心流程包括：(1) 仅评估少量锚定(anchor)LLM；(2) 合成伪评估结果以拓宽覆盖范围；(3) 学习紧凑的样本表示；(4) 选择一个小而具有代表性的样本子集。该方法具有理论误差界和排名一致性保证。在此基础上构建 ZipBench Zoo，包含100多个覆盖文本、多模态和智能体任务的基准代理(compact versions)。

Result: ZipBench Zoo 中的紧凑基准在完整基准上达到平均绝对误差0.002-0.02，平均Spearman相关系数约0.98。该方法同时降低了LLM评估和紧凑基准构建的成本，代码已开源(https://github.com/MilkThink-Lab/ZipBench)。

Conclusion: ZipBench 以极低的成本实现了高效、准确的基准压缩，降低了计算受限研究者参与广泛LLM研究的门槛，提供了一种可扩展、有理论保证的基准压缩新范式。

Abstract: Comprehensive benchmark suites are essential for improving large language models (LLMs), but many widely used benchmarks are redundant, making evaluation unnecessarily expensive. Although recent benchmark compression methods (BCMs) can mitigate this cost, many strong BCMs rely on large collections of per-sample evaluation results from numerous LLMs to identify representative samples. Building such collections is also expensive unless they are already public, making these methods difficult to extend to newly released benchmarks. To address this challenge, we present ZipBench, a simple and low-cost BCM with theoretical error and rank-consistency guarantees. ZipBench evaluates only a small set of anchor LLMs, synthesizes pseudo evaluation results to broaden coverage, learns compact sample representations, and selects a small yet representative subset. Building on it, we create ZipBench Zoo, a collection of compact versions of 100+ benchmark proxies spanning text, multimodal, and agent tasks. These benchmark achieve mean absolute errors of 0.002--0.02 and average Spearman correlations of ~0.98 with the full benchmarks. Overall, ZipBench reduces the cost of both LLM evaluation and compact benchmark construction, lowering the barrier to broad LLM research for compute-constrained researchers. The code has been released in https://github.com/MilkThink-Lab/ZipBench.

</details>


### [96] [The House with a Million Windows: Interactive Fiction for Narrative Restorying](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12537)
*Cody Kommers,Sarah G Immel,Drew Hemment,Mina Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为"拥有一百万扇窗户的房子"（HWAMW）的基于大语言模型（LLM）的交互式小说系统，旨在解决AI辅助写作导致叙事扁平化、同质化的问题。该系统基于心理学中的重构（restorying）干预范式，让用户在文本叙事中讲述个人故事后，通过LLM生成的不同文学风格的"窗口"对故事进行重新框定，从而帮助用户探索个人故事中潜在的广度与深度意义。实证证据表明HWAMW能增强用户的叙事认同感，专家评审则深入探究了其实现机制。研究结论认为HWAMW促进了故事重构，为AI辅助写作提供了一种有价值的范式——LLM不是替我们讲故事，而是帮助我们发现自己所讲故事中更大的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助写作可能使人类叙事扁平化，产出同质化内容，削弱了讲故事所需的刻意努力和意义建构过程。作者希望应对这一挑战，帮助用户探索个人故事中潜在意义的广度与深度。

Method: 作者设计了HWAMW系统，一种基于LLM的交互式小说系统。用户先在文本叙事中讲述自己的故事，随后系统基于心理学中的重构干预范式，通过LLM生成一系列不同文学风格的"窗口"对故事进行重新框定。研究采用实证评估（测量叙事认同感）与专家评审相结合的方法来检验系统效果及实现机制。

Result: 实证证据表明HWAMW能够提升用户的叙事认同感；专家评审进一步揭示了这一效果是如何实现的。研究结果支持该系统确实促进了故事重构干预。

Conclusion: HWAMW为AI辅助写作提供了一种有价值的范式：LLM并非取代人类去讲述故事，而是帮助用户认识并发掘自己故事中蕴含的更大潜力，从而在保持人类能动性、意义建构的同时利用AI增强叙事能力。

Abstract: AI-assisted writing can flatten meaning in human storytelling, enabling the production of homogeneous outputs without the intentional effort and sense-making writing entails. To address this challenge, we present The House with a Million Windows (HWAMW), an LLM-based interactive fiction system designed to help users explore both the breadth and depth of potential meanings within their personal stories -- drawing on a psychological paradigm called the restorying intervention. In HWAMW, users play through a text-based narrative in which they tell a story, then encounter a set of LLM-generated "windows" reframing it according to different literary styles. Empirical evidence shows that HWAMW increases users' sense of narrative identity, while an expert review explores how this effect is achieved. Our findings suggest that HWAMW facilitates restorying and offers a valuable paradigm for AI-assisted writing, wherein LLMs do not tell our stories but rather help us see greater potential in the stories we tell.

</details>


### [97] [Agent as Policy for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12541)
*Mengzhao Jia,Yang Lin,Xixin Zhang,Zhihan Zhang,Xiaobai Liu,Meng Jiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Agent as Policy (AGP)框架，使通用智能体无需任务或环境特定训练，直接通过推理、编程和交互驱动物理机器人完成多种操作任务，在三个积木构建配置上分别达到100%、100%和80%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人策略通常需要任务或环境特定的训练，缺乏通用性。作者希望探索通用智能体能否直接作为机器人策略，通过运行时推理与编程实现物理世界的自主操作。

Method: AGP将任务规划与执行置于智能体控制之下：给定任务和机器人接口后，智能体解释视觉证据、编写可执行程序、发出运动命令，并根据物理结果修订动作。该框架跨越精度操作、动态运动和可变形物体等多种真实世界任务进行验证。

Result: 在多项真实操作任务中（如从人类视频装配、基于目标图像的积木构建、模具重定向、定向投掷、双手毛巾折叠），AGP在三种积木构建配置上分别获得100%、100%和80%的成功率。

Conclusion: 研究表明通用智能体可以作为机器人策略，通过运行时推理、编程和交互将自主性扩展到物理操作领域，为通用智能体在机器人中的应用开辟了可行路径。

Abstract: We demonstrate that a general-purpose agent can directly drive a physical robot throughout task execution without any task-specific or environment-specific training. We introduce Agent as Policy (AGP), which places task planning and execution under the agent's control. Given a task and a robot interface, the agent interprets visual evidence, writes executable programs, issues motion commands, and revises its actions in response to physical outcomes. This brings the agent's reasoning and programming capabilities into continuous interaction with the physical world. We study AGP across multiple real-world manipulation tasks spanning precision manipulation, dynamic motions, and deformable objects. These include assembly from human videos, block construction from goal images, die reorientation, targeted throwing, and bimanual towel folding. AGP achieves success rates of 100%, 100%, and 80% on three block construction configurations. These findings establish a path for general-purpose agents to act as robotic policies, extending their autonomy to physical manipulation through runtime reasoning, programming, and interaction.

</details>


### [98] [Meddies-PII: A Multilingual Framework for Personally Identifiable Information Extraction in Clinical De-identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12544)
*Linh Uyen Le,Christian Hoang,Huy Hoang Ha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了Meddies-PII-Dataset，一个包含一百万篇合成临床文档的多语言语料库，覆盖17种语言和9种PII标签，通过属性条件提示生成并进行13道确定性门控验证。基于该数据集训练的Meddies-PII-Model（BIOES分词分类器）在15个外部基准上取得平均F1值0.827，显著优于最强基线（0.658），展示了合成数据在临床去标识化任务中的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床去标识化依赖于精确识别个人身份信息（PII），但人工标注数据集构建成本高昂，而现有的合成替代方案往往缺乏生成过程的详细信息或依赖较简单的合成策略，限制了多语言临床去标识化研究的发展。

Method: 使用属性条件提示（attribute-conditioned prompts）生成一百万篇合成临床文档，覆盖17种语言和9种PII标签；通过13道确定性门控（deterministic gates）验证文档的结构和标注一致性；基于该数据集训练Meddies-PII-Model，这是一个BIOES分词分类器，并与现有PII抽取系统进行对比评估。

Result: Meddies-PII-Model在所有报告的基准上均取得最高性能，在15个外部基准上的平均F1值为0.827，而最强基线仅为0.658，表明该模型在准确性和泛化性上均显著优于现有系统。

Conclusion: Meddies-PII-Model在15个外部基准上的平均F1达到0.827，相比最强基线的0.658有显著提升，验证了高质量合成数据对多语言临床去标识化的有效性；作者将在论文录用后公开数据集、基准套件、模型、生成框架和评估代码，以支持该领域的研究。

Abstract: Clinical de-identification relies on accurately identifying personally identifiable information (PII). However, manually annotated datasets are costly to construct, while existing synthetic alternatives often provide limited details about their generation process or rely on relatively simple synthesis strategies. We introduce Meddies-PII-Dataset, a corpus of one million synthetic clinical documents spanning seventeen languages and nine PII labels. The documents are generated using attribute-conditioned prompts and validated through thirteen deterministic gates that enforce structural and annotation consistency. To evaluate the dataset's utility, we train Meddies-PII-Model, a BIOES token classifier, and compare it with existing PII extraction systems using exact-match entity-level F1. Meddies-PII-Model achieves the highest performance among the evaluated systems on all reported benchmarks, with a mean F1 of 0.827 across fifteen external benchmarks, compared with 0.658 for the strongest baseline. Upon acceptance, we will publicly release the dataset, benchmark suite, model, generation framework, and evaluation code to support research on multilingual clinical de-identification.

</details>


### [99] [Calibrated Ambiguity in Multimodal Language Models: Humans reach for cultural references, while models describe the picture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12575)
*Cody Kommers,Mingrui Ye,Evelyn Gius,Daniela Mihai,Hoyt Long,Zheng Yuan,Drew Hemment*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"校准的模糊性"（calibrated ambiguity）这一概念，借由桌游《Dixit》设计任务，比较人类与多模态语言模型在生成线索时的差异。结果发现模型表现出"模糊性坍缩"（过度指定、不留多重解释空间）和"文化扁平化"（几乎不引用文化背景知识）两大缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流AI研究将模糊性视为需要消除的"缺陷"，但作者指出，在人类交流与文化中（幽默、政治、艺术），模糊性是一种生成性资源：表达既要足够开放以容纳多元解读，又要足够受约束以可被解读。作者希望通过实证比较，揭示当前多模态模型在这一维度上的不足。

Method: 作者将"校准的模糊性"操作化定义为一项源自桌游《Dixit》的任务（根据卡片图像生成既能指向又能留有余地的线索）。研究中设计了新颖的编码评分体系，对人工生成线索与多模态语言模型生成线索进行系统比较，并测试模型在被提示使用典故与比喻性语言时的表现。

Result: 研究结果表明：1）模型持续表现出"模糊性坍缩"，即输出过度指定、过于具体，不给多个合理解读留下空间；2）与人类线索不同，AI生成的线索还表现出"文化扁平化"，即便被明确提示使用典故和比喻性语言，也几乎从不引用文化背景知识。

Conclusion: 当前多模态语言模型在"校准的模糊性"方面与人类存在系统性差距，缺乏生成既有开放性又有约束性表达的能力，且缺少文化情境化的参照。这提示未来AI系统需关注如何在表达中保留合理的模糊性与文化意蕴，而非一味追求明确性。

Abstract: Ambiguity is often treated as a bug for AI systems to resolve---but in human communication and culture, ambiguity can also be a generative resource. From humour to politics to art, people express themselves in words and images that are open enough to invite different interpretations, yet constrained enough to be interpretable. We operationalise this notion of calibrated ambiguity with a task drawn from the parlour game Dixit. We compare differences in clues generated by human vs multimodal language models, based on a novel coding rubric for calibrated ambiguity, and find that models consistently exhibit ambiguity collapse (i.e., their outputs are over-specified, leaving no room for multiple legitimate interpretations). Unlike human clues, AI-generated clues also exhibit cultural flattening; they almost never make reference to culturally-situated knowledge, even when prompted to use allusion and figurative language.

</details>


### [100] [SWARM: A Multilingual Human-Annotated Dataset for Russian Propaganda Detection in Search Engine Results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12653)
*Manuel Tonneau,Abhinav Dubey,Farhan Shaikh,Ilaria Vitulano,Martha Stolze,Hale Dedeoglu,Clara Riechert,Ella Kuka,Maryna Sydorova,Mykola Makhortykh,Elizaveta Kuznetsova*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍SWARM数据集，包含九种语言、2,183条搜索引擎结果，并标注是否支持俄罗斯宣传叙事。基准测试显示来源黑名单效果差，内容级分类器表现有限，最强LLM的F1为0.73，监督分类器约0.5，小LLM易过度预测。文章强调需按语言进行内容级评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算研究大多仅关注单一社交媒体平台、一两种语言，且多分析来源而非内容，无法全面捕捉跨语言、跨域的网络宣传。

Method: 构建SWARM数据集：收集九种语言的2,183个搜索引擎结果，覆盖新闻、博客、政府网站等多样域；由训练编码员标注是否支持重复出现的俄罗斯宣传叙事。对比基于来源的黑名单、监督分类器和零样本LLM的性能。

Result: 黑名单漏掉大多数支持宣传的文档，因为此类内容也出现在主流媒体而非仅限被标记的“宣传”渠道。最强的LLM在正类F1上达到0.73，监督分类器仅约0.5，较小LLM过度预测支持，将主题相关性误认为认可。

Conclusion: 检测搜索引擎带来的宣传需要逐语言的内容级评估，SWARM数据集和评估代码可为此提供支持。

Abstract: Russian state propaganda spreads across many languages and online spaces. Yet, most computational work examines only one such space, usually social media, in one or two languages, and analyses sources rather than content. We introduce SWARM (Search-Web documents Annotated for Russian propaganda, Multilingual), a dataset of 2,183 search engine results across nine languages and diverse web domains (e.g., news, blogs, government sites), each annotated by trained coders for whether it supports a recurring Russian propaganda narrative. We benchmark a source-based blocklist, supervised classifiers, and zero-shot LLMs against these labels. The blocklist misses most propaganda-supporting documents, because such content is not confined to flagged "propaganda" outlets but also appears on mainstream ones. Content-level analysis helps, though how much depends on the model: the strongest LLM reaches a positive-class F1 of 0.73, whereas the supervised classifiers reach only about 0.5, with the smaller LLMs over-predicting support, mistaking topical relevance for endorsement. Detecting search-borne propaganda thus requires per-language, content-level evaluation, which we hope SWARM and our evaluation code enable.

</details>


### [101] [LifeMem: Enabling Lifelong Experience Reuse for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12655)
*Yuli Qiu,Yutong Li,Wei Su,Zeming Liu,Wanxiang Che,Heyan Huang,Haifeng Wang,Yuang Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LifeMem是一个终身学习框架，通过聚类交互轨迹提取可复用技能，使智能体能在多环境中跨任务迁移经验并缓解灾难性遗忘。在10个环境、超过13k任务和2k条新标注轨迹上的实验表明，该方法实现了高效的经验复用、降低遗忘并提升跨任务迁移效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于记忆的智能体在跨环境迁移可复用经验方面表现不佳，且随经验累积会遭遇灾难性遗忘问题，这限制了大型语言模型智能体在终身学习中持续适应新任务和环境的能力。

Method: LifeMem框架包含两个阶段：学习阶段根据底层工作流对累积的交互轨迹进行聚类，以提取可复用的技能；推理阶段智能体召回相关的技能和轨迹来指导当前新任务的行动。

Result: 在10个环境、超过13k个任务以及2k条新标注交互轨迹上的实验验证显示，LifeMem在终身学习中实现了有效的经验复用，在所学任务上降低了遗忘，同时在跨任务迁移上表现更优。深入分析还发现任务流会影响学习效果，而记忆中对结构相似轨迹的整合能提升性能。

Conclusion: LifeMem证明了通过在记忆中对结构相似轨迹聚类整合并利用工作流提取技能，可以有效缓解灾难性遗忘并提升跨环境知识迁移，为大型语言模型智能体的终身学习提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Large language model agents are expected to continuously adapt to new tasks and environments over their lifetime by reusing past experience. However, existing memory-based agents struggle to transfer reusable experience across environments and suffer from catastrophic forgetting as experience accumulated. To address these challenges, we propose LifeMem, a lifelong learning framework that enables agents to transfer knowledge across multiple environments. During learning, LifeMem clusters accumulated interaction trajectories based on underlying workflows to extract reusable skills. When solving a new task at inference time, the agent recalls relevant skills and trajectories to guide actions. To validate our method, we conduct experiments across 10 environments and over 13k tasks with 2k newly annotated interaction trajectories. Results show that LifeMem enables effective experience reuse in lifelong learning, achieving both reduced forgetting on learned tasks and superior cross-task transfer. Further analysis reveals that task streaming impacts learning, while consolidating structurally similar trajectories within memory boosts performance.

</details>


### [102] [Doc2FRC: Length-Consistent Document-Level Machine Translation via Fixed-Range Chunking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12674)
*Xiaotian Wang,Youyuan Lin,Zhan Shen,Hitomi Yanaka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出固定区间分块（FRC）方法，利用动态规划将文档切分为长度在预定义区间内的块，并在训练和推理阶段保持一致的分块策略，从而消除训练-推理之间的长度分布不匹配，改善文档级机器翻译（DocMT）中的重复与质量退化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文LLM虽能减少DocMT中的输入截断，但直接的Doc2Doc翻译仍存在n-gram重复和渐进式质量退化问题。主流缓解手段是分块，但传统基于规则的分块方法无法应对训练与推理之间的长度分布不匹配，制约了翻译质量。

Method: （1）引入固定区间分块（FRC）：通过动态规划将任意长度的文档切分为落入预定义长度区间的块，训练和推理统一采用FRC，使输入映射到相同的长度分布，显著降低训练-测试长度不匹配；（2）围绕FRC提出轻量级双边界匹配算法用于块对齐；（3）设计四种不同的训练策略。

Result: 实验表明，基于FRC的微调显著优于7B LLM上的直接Doc2Doc微调，并在IWSLT2017上超越现有DocMT方法；此外，作者构建了与主流DocMT训练源无关的10语种测试集GlobVDoc，验证了FRC能改善分布外（OOD）文档翻译。

Conclusion: FRC通过统一训练与推理的块长度分布，有效缓解了长文档翻译中的重复、质量退化及长度分布不匹配问题，在基准测试和跨域/跨语种泛化上均表现出明显优势，是一种轻量且通用的DocMT改进方案。

Abstract: Advanced large language models (LLMs) with long context windows can substantially reduce input truncation in document-level machine translation (DocMT). However, direct Doc2Doc translation remains prone to n-gram repetition and progressive quality degradation. A common remedy is to segment the document into finer-grained chunks. Nonetheless, conventional rule-based chunking approaches fail to handle the length distribution mismatch between training and inference. To address this, we introduce Fixed-Range Chunking (FRC), utilizing dynamic programming to partition documents into chunks within a predefined length interval. By consistently applying FRC during training and inference, the input documents of any length are mapped to the same length distribution, substantially reducing train-test length mismatch. Centered on FRC, we propose a lightweight dual-boundary matching algorithm for chunk alignment, alongside four distinct training strategies. Experimental results show that FRC-based fine-tuning substantially improves 7B LLMs over direct Doc2Doc fine-tuning and outperforms existing DocMT methods on IWSLT2017. We further construct GlobVDoc, a 10-language test set independent of mainstream DocMT training sources, and show that FRC improves out-of-distribution document translation.

</details>


### [103] [Cognition on Graph: Navigating Massive Knowledge Space via Cognitive Cycles and Bidirectional Graph-Text Synergy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12791)
*Gengxian Zhou,Jian Xu,Zichen Tang,Shiming Xiang,Haihong E,Cheng-Lin Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CoG提出了一种受认知科学启发的、无需训练的RAG框架，通过"规划-探索-反思"循环实现自适应知识探索，并在结构化图与非结构化文本间建立深度双向协同，在七个多跳问答基准上显著超越现有方法且探索效率更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG方法在处理大规模、异构知识库（知识图谱与文本语料）进行复杂推理时存在两个关键缺陷：一是采用反应式、图驱动的探索策略，盲目跟随图拓扑结构，无法根据问题上下文或逐步探索进展进行自适应调整；二是缺乏图和文本之间深度的双向协同。这限制了模型在知识密集型任务上的推理能力

Method: CoG模拟人类问题求解过程，执行连续的"规划-探索-反思"循环：1）主动制定调查计划（plan）；2）进行双源检索，同时从结构化图和非结构化文本中获取信息（explore）；3）动态反思当前探索进展并调整策略（reflect）。其核心创新在于建立图与文本间的深度双向协同：从文本中提取的实体可动态引导图的探索，从而弥合知识缺口。整个框架是训练无关的（training-free）。

Result: 在七个多跳问答基准数据集上的大量实验表明，CoG显著优于现有最先进方法，同时展现出更优越的探索效率。

Conclusion: CoG证明了受认知科学启发的"规划-探索-反思"范式能有效解决异构知识库上的复杂推理问题，通过图与文本的深度双向协同实现了更强的自适应性和探索效率，为无需训练的知识增强推理提供了新思路。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has empowered Large Language Models (LLMs) to tackle knowledge-intensive tasks. However, navigating global, heterogeneous knowledge bases (large-scale knowledge graphs and text corpora) for complex reasoning remains a challenge. Existing methods typically employ reactive, graph-driven exploration strategies, which blindly follow graph topology without adapting to the question context or evolving exploration progress, and lack deep bidirectional synergy between graph and text. To address these limitations, we propose CoG (Cognition on Graph), a cognitive-inspired, training-free framework for adaptive knowledge exploration. Drawing inspiration from human problem-solving, CoG performs a continuous plan-explore-reflect cycle, where it proactively formulates investigation plans, performs dual-source retrieval, and dynamically reflects on progress to adjust strategies. Crucially, it establishes deep bidirectional synergy between structured graph and unstructured text, where entities extracted from text dynamically guide graph exploration to bridge knowledge gaps. Extensive experiments on seven multi-hop QA benchmarks demonstrate that CoG significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods while achieving superior exploration efficiency. Our code and datasets are available at https://github.com/zhougengxian/CoG.

</details>


### [104] [DuplexDrama: A Synthesized Dialogue Dataset with Scenarios, Full-Duplex Behaviors, Expressive Speech, and Sound Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12872)
*Qingxiang Guo,Wenke Fan,Shuofeng Zhao,Dawei Yang,Zhiyang Zhou,Yingxin Shang,Hongwei Cai,Zhou Wang,Weixu Wang,Lin Yang,Shuran Zhou,Yang Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了 DuplexDrama，这是首个同时覆盖完整人物设定与场景设置、三种全双工行为（打断、反馈、不完整语句）、具有人物对齐情感标签的表达性语音以及脚本感知声音事件四个维度的合成口语对话数据集。该数据集通过4阶段流水线构建，并经过脚本与合成音频的质检，产出超过2000小时的音频数据，涵盖64种音色、13种人物与5个年龄段，其中3.8%的轮次携带至少一个全双工行为。作者将发布6,400个双语对话（800小时，中文约500小时+英文约300小时）的子集以推动全双工口语对话模型研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有口语对话数据集通常缺乏对全双工对话的完整建模，难以同时覆盖人物/场景设定、全双工行为、情感表达和声音事件等多个维度，制约了全双工口语对话模型的发展。DuplexDrama旨在填补这一空白，为相关研究提供高质量、大规模且维度完整的数据资源。

Method: 采用4阶段流水线构建数据集：完整的人物与场景设定、三种全双工行为（打断、反馈、不完整）、具有人物对齐情感标签的表达性语音、脚本感知声音事件。通过脚本与合成音频的质量验证确保数据质量，并利用内部全双工模型训练进行有效性验证。

Result: 产出了超过2000小时的音频数据，包含64种音色、13种人物、5个年龄段；3.8%的轮次携带至少一个全双工行为。将发布6,400个双语对话（800小时，中文约500小时+英文约300小时）的精选子集，并附带演示页面样例与LLM评判评估提示。

Conclusion: DuplexDrama通过多维度、大规模的数据设计有效支撑了全双工口语对话模型研究，其筛选出的双语子集与评估提示将公开释放，有望推动该领域的进一步研究与应用。

Abstract: We present DuplexDrama, the first synthesized spoken dialogue dataset that simultaneously covers four dimensions: (i) complete persona and scenario settings; (ii) three full-duplex behaviors (interruption, backchannel, incomplete); (iii) expressive speech with persona-aligned emotion labels; and (iv) script-aware sound events. DuplexDrama is built via a 4-stage pipeline; quality validation on both scripts and synthesized audio confirms its quality. We have produced more than 2,000 hours audio data with a 64-voice timbre pool spanning 13 personas and 5 age buckets; 3.8% of all turns carry at least one full-duplex behavior. This data has been validated through internal full-duplex model training. We will release a curated subset of 6,400 bilingual dialogues (800 h, Chinese ~500 h + English ~300 h) to advance full-duplex spoken dialogue model research. Data samples are available at our demo page and LLM-judge evaluation prompts will be released with the dataset.

</details>


### [105] [Parameter-Efficient Retrievers for Polish and European Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12913)
*Sławomir Dadas,Rafał Poświata,Małgorzata Grębowiec,Michał Perełkiewicz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种三阶段训练流程，用于开发紧凑且高效的稠密检索模型，使其在远小于大模型体积的情况下保持竞争力。该流程结合跨语言对齐、关系知识蒸馏和对比微调，完全依赖强嵌入模型和重排序器生成的监督信号，无需原始真实相关性标签。基于此流程开发了PolDense（六种波兰语检索器，17M至1B参数）和EuroDense（435M参数，支持九种欧洲语言），并在41项波兰语和150项多语言检索任务上验证了其优越的质量-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密检索系统日益依赖数十亿参数的大型语言模型，其内存和计算需求使得大规模索引构建、频繁语料库更新和低延迟服务成本高昂。作者旨在通过训练紧凑高效的检索器来缓解这一成本问题，同时保持与更大模型相当的性能。

Method: 提出一条三阶段训练流程：(1)跨语言对齐；(2)关系知识蒸馏；(3)对比微调。该流程无需原始真实相关性标签，仅利用强嵌入模型和重排序器作为教师生成的监督信号。基于该流程构建了PolDense（六个波兰语检索器，参数规模17M到1B）和EuroDense（435M参数，支持九种欧洲语言），两者均支持高达8,192 token的上下文。

Result: 在41项波兰语和150项多语言检索任务上的广泛评估显示，PolDense-1B优于评估中参数高达9B的检索器，且PolDense家族在模型规模维度上构成Pareto前沿。在低于1B参数的模型中，EuroDense在任务平均与语言平均性能上均排名第一，且在九种语言中的七种领先。所有模型均已公开。

Conclusion: 通过三阶段训练流程，能够在无真实标签的前提下蒸馏大型教师模型的能力，训练出与大规模模型竞争的紧凑检索器，实现了优异的质量-效率权衡。PolDense和EuroDense的发布为高效多语言稠密检索提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Dense retrieval systems increasingly rely on multi-billion-parameter language models, whose memory and computational requirements make large-scale indexing, frequent corpus updates, and low-latency serving costly. We present a three-stage training pipeline for developing compact and efficient retrievers that remain competitive with substantially larger models. The pipeline combines cross-lingual alignment, relational knowledge distillation, and contrastive fine-tuning. It requires no original ground-truth relevance labels, relying exclusively on supervision generated by strong embedding models and rerankers utilised as teachers. Using this pipeline, we develop PolDense and EuroDense, both supporting contexts of up to 8,192 tokens. PolDense is a family of six Polish retrievers ranging from 17M to 1B parameters. EuroDense is a 435M-parameter retriever supporting nine European languages. We conduct an extensive evaluation covering 41 Polish and 150 multilingual retrieval tasks. The results demonstrate strong quality-efficiency trade-offs. PolDense-1B outperforms the evaluated retrievers with up to 9B parameters, while the PolDense family forms the Pareto frontier across model sizes. Among the evaluated models below 1B parameters, EuroDense ranks first in both task-averaged and language-averaged performance and leads in seven of nine languages. We release all models publicly.

</details>


### [106] [LLM-Enhanced Dual-Branch Learning for Large-Scale Multi-Label Text Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12915)
*Hui Ye,Jing Zhang,Xiulong Yang,Rajshekhar Sunderraman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DualMLC是一个双分支框架，通过自回归解码器语言模型和双向编码器并行处理同一文档，并利用晚期logit融合整合两部分得分，在大规模多标签文本分类的三个基准上取得最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模多标签文本分类方法多依赖单一主编码器，或在单一排序器内融合辅助特征，未能充分探索不同语言模型之间的互补性，限制了语义表示的利用。

Method: 构建双分支架构：一个分支使用自回归解码器语言模型，另一分支使用双向编码器，两分支各自维护独立表征通路并独立估计共享标签空间上的相关分数；最终通过晚期logit融合合并两个分数向量，使共享证据强化相关标签，分支特异性证据弥补另一分支的表征局限。

Result: 在三个广泛使用的大规模多标签文本分类基准上取得最新最优结果；消融实验证实异构预测器的集成比任一单分支产生更强的排序性能。

Conclusion: 异构语言模型的互补性融合能有效提升大规模多标签文本分类的性能，双分支late-fusion框架优于单一表征方法的方案。

Abstract: Large-scale multi-label text classification assigns a small subset of relevant labels to each document from a vocabulary containing thousands or tens of thousands of candidate labels. Although pretrained language models have improved semantic text representations, most representation-based approaches center their prediction pipelines on a primary encoder or combine auxiliary features within a single ranker. The complementarity between heterogeneous language models therefore remains insufficiently explored. We propose DualMLC, a dual-branch framework that processes the same document through an autoregressive decoder-only language model and a bidirectional encoder. Each branch maintains its own representation pathway and independently estimates relevance scores over the shared label space. DualMLC combines the two score vectors through late logit fusion, allowing shared evidence to reinforce relevant labels and branch-specific evidence to compensate for limitations in the other branch's representation. DualMLC achieves state-of-the-art results on three widely used large-scale multi-label text classification benchmarks. Ablation results further confirm that integrating the heterogeneous predictors produces stronger rankings than either branch alone. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/huiyegit/DualMLC.

</details>


### [107] [Fewer Words, Not Fewer Tokens: Measuring the Sanskrit Tokenization Penalty per Proposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12960)
*Devansh Sharma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文考察梵语的形态信息密度是否能经受子词分词（subword tokenization）的检验。在真实部署的分词器下，梵语每命题（proposition）消耗的token数是英语的1.774-2.187倍，是印地语的1.325-1.353倍；但在匹配的分词器控制下（相同算法与词表大小），这种"梵语更贵"的结论会翻转甚至消失——在32k和64k词表下所有8组匹配对照均高于1.0（95%置信区间排除1.0），词表扩大至128k时域内降至0.983。比例可分解为字符长度比与每字符token数比，后者始终接近1，说明真正留存下来的是字符级长度差异。结论是：关于部署实践（人们实际使用的分词器）的稳健表述成立——在当代散文与FLORES上，以SLP1转写的梵语相较o200k英语基线，每命题需消耗1.831-2.899个英语token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梵语将格、数、人称和时态融合进词尾，并把从句链入复合词，因此每词信息密度极高。但这种密度能否在子词分词（按意义单元而非按词切分）下幸存，是一个独立且未被系统回答的问题。作者旨在通过受控对照实验，厘清"梵语token更贵"这一主张在何种条件下成立，识别真实的成本来源。

Method: 在一致的FLORES-200 devtest内容上，对比部署中的分词器（词表20万+id）的token成本；随后构建匹配的BPE对照实验：同一算法、同一词表大小，分别训练于梵语侧与英语侧（配对控制与字节控制），在32k、64k、128k三档词表下比较8组匹配对。将token比例分解为字符长度比 × 每字符token数比，以定位差异来源。所用转写为SLP1。

Result: 部署分词器下，梵语每命题token数约为英语的1.774-2.187倍、印地语的1.325-1.353倍；但在匹配控制（同为英语侧训练的BPE）下，32k和64k时所有8组配对均>1.0（95%CI排除1.0），128k时域内降至0.983、域外1.025、FLORES 1.116。分解显示每字符token数比接近1，差异主要来自字符长度比：SLP1梵语散文为英语的1.028，梵语诗歌仅0.596。真实部署场景（o200k英语枢轴）下每命题成本为1.831-2.899个英语token。

Conclusion: 关键结论是结论取决于比较基准：与梵语同算法同词表训练的英语对照相比，"梵语更贵"的优势翻转消失，幸存的是字符级长度而非形态密度编码的代价；但在人们实际部署的分词器下（以英语o200k为枢轴），梵语确实显著更贵。这提示评估信息密度应基于匹配的分词器而非部署设置，且真实成本源是字符长度而非分词粒度。代码、结果快照与所有表格均已公开。

Abstract: Sanskrit fuses case, number, person and tense into word endings and chains clauses into compounds, so it is information-dense per word. Whether that density survives subword tokenization is a separate question, to be asked per unit of meaning rather than per word. On identical FLORES-200 devtest content, Sanskrit costs 1.774-2.187 times the English tokens under deployed tokenizers with vocabularies of 200,019 ids or more, but only 1.325-1.353 times the Hindi tokens. Against a deployed English tokenizer, Sanskrit-trained BPE arms then look cheaper per proposition than English on contemporary prose (0.887). Against a matched English control, the same algorithm and vocabulary trained on the English side of the same corpus, that flip disappears: at 32,000 and 64,000 pieces all 8 matched pairs, each size-matched arm against both a pair-matched and a byte-matched control, sit above 1.0 on prose with 95% intervals excluding it. The gap closes as the vocabulary grows: at 128,000 pieces the BPE pair reads 0.983 in domain while staying above parity out of domain (1.025) and on FLORES (1.116). The ratio factorises into a character-length ratio and a tokens-per-character ratio, the second near 1 throughout: what survives matched tokenization is character-level length, which Sanskrit prose lacks over English in SLP1 (1.028) and Sanskrit verse has (0.596). The robust statement is about deployed practice: on contemporary prose and on FLORES, with the Sanskrit side in SLP1 against the deployed o200k English pivot, Sanskrit costs 1.831-2.899 English tokens per proposition under the tokenizers people actually ship. Code, the results snapshot and every table here are public.

</details>


### [108] [Judging by the Cover: Cleaning LLM Truthfulness Benchmarks to Avoid Surface-Level Feature Leakage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13003)
*Foad Namjoo,Remy Ogasawara,Amirali Abdullah,Cullen Anderson,Narmeen Fatimah Oozeer,Jeff M. Phillips*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示二元选择真值基准（如TruthfulQA）中存在可由表面特征（表面级线索）引发的“作弊漏洞”：模型无需进行预期推理即可仅凭正确/错误答案在表层特征上的系统性差异超越随机水平。作者证明该缺陷可被检测并利用（一个简单的六特征逻辑回归分类器即可在TruthfulQA上显著区分正确与错误答案），并发现类似缺陷也存在于其他基准中。为此，他们提出了通用清理机制，通过移除最具泄漏强化的样本对来净化数据，发布了表面特征泄漏已降至接近随机水平的TruthfulQA版本，并提供了Audit-Prune机制供数据集在发布前清理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二元选择真值基准的设计初衷是迫使模型在正确与错误答案之间进行语义推理以评判其真实性，但若两种答案在表层特征上存在系统性差异，模型即可走捷径，在不执行预期推理的情况下超越随机水平。这种泄漏不仅损害基准的有效性，还会误导评估结论，因此亟需检测并消除此类伪影以恢复基准的可靠性。

Method: 作者首先验证了泄漏的可检测性与可利用性：在TruthfulQA上使用一个仅含六个特征的逻辑回归分类器对正确/错误答案进行分类，获得显著高于随机的准确率，证明表层特征足以区分答案类型。随后在多个其他基准上检查了类似的表面级伪影。针对该问题，提出了一种通用的数据清理机制——审计并移除最具泄漏强化作用的样本对（答案配对），使剩余数据中表面特征与正确性之间的关联降至接近随机水平；该机制以Audit-Prune工具的形式提供。

Result: 实验表明，简单的六特征逻辑回归分类器就能在TruthfulQA上以相当高的准确率区分正确与错误答案，证实了泄漏的存在且可被下游分类器利用；类似表面级缺陷也在其他基准中被发现。经过清理机制处理后，TruthfulQA的表面特征泄漏被降低到接近随机水平，并发布了这一净化版本。

Conclusion: 表面特征泄漏是二元选择真值评估中普遍存在的隐患，会令基准失效并误导模型能力评估。本文提出的审计—清理机制（Audit-Prune）能够有效降低此类泄漏，为未来的数据集构建提供了发布前的质量检查与净化手段，从而提升真值评估基准的可靠性与有效性。

Abstract: Binary-choice truth benchmarks ask models to choose between a correct and an incorrect answer, but if the two answers differ systematically in surface-level features, models can exceed chance without performing the intended reasoning. We show that this failure mode is detectable and can be exploited by downstream classifiers. In TruthfulQA, a simple six-feature logistic classifier achieves substantial accuracy in separating correct from incorrect answers. We further show that similar surface-level artifacts are present in additional benchmarks. To counteract this, we developed a general mechanism to clean them by removing the most leakage-reinforcing pairs. We release a version of TruthfulQA with surface-feature leakage reduced close to chance and provide a mechanism, Audit-Prune, so that the datasets can be cleaned before release.

</details>


### [109] [Tasks over Application Manuals: Revealing Gaps in Long-Horizon Procedural Reasoning for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13005)
*Utkarsh Soni,Syed Shariyar Murtaza,Yifan Nie,Sachin Chandrasekhar,Eugene Wen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了TAM（Tasks over Application Manuals）基准，用于评估大语言模型（LLM）在长程程序性推理上的能力。TAM从两个真实世界领域构建任务：ICD-10-CM临床编码（将医学状况映射为诊断代码）和美国联邦量刑（计算犯罪量刑指南中的offense level），并配有经过人工验证的标签。每个任务都要求遵循包含数万条规则的权威手册，并跨不同章节执行一系列相互依赖的步骤以产生精确答案。实验发现，包括检索增强生成、ReAct风格提示和基于GPT-5的agent-harness基线在内的多种通用提示方法，其精确匹配性能极低：ICD-10-CM编码任务为1%，量刑任务为15.5%。结果表明当前基准可能高估了LLM的推理能力，并忽视了可靠遵循长程基于规则程序这一关键挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM虽然已在各种自然语言任务和多跳推理基准上表现优异，但这些基准通常是短程的，仅需少量检索或推理步骤。它们无法充分反映LLM在真实世界任务中可靠性方面的能力——这些任务需要遵循跨越数百页、包含复杂且相互依赖指南的手册。作者旨在填补这一评估空白。

Method: 作者构建了TAM基准：从ICD-10-CM临床编码和美国联邦量刑两个真实领域精心整理任务，并提供人工验证的标签。每个任务要求遵循包含数万条规则的手册，执行跨不同章节的、相互依赖的多个程序步骤以获得精确答案。作者评估了多种通用提示方法，包括检索增强生成（RAG）、ReAct风格提示，以及基于GPT-5的agent-harness基线，采用精确匹配（exact-match）作为评估指标。

Result: 在TAM基准上，所有被评估的方法表现均极低：ICD-10-CM编码任务的精确匹配性能为1%，量刑任务为15.5%。即使是使用GPT-5的最强基线，其最佳表现也远未达到实用水平。

Conclusion: TAM基准揭示了现有LLM推理评估的一个盲区：当前短程基准可能高估了LLM的能力，而可靠地遵循长程、基于规则的程序性任务仍是LLM面临的关键挑战。作者公开了完整的TAM数据和代码，以推动该方向的研究。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved strong performance on a wide range of natural language tasks, and recent benchmarks suggest that they are increasingly adept at multi-hop reasoning. However, these benchmarks are typically short-horizon, requiring only a small number of retrieval or inference steps, and provide limited evidence of reliability on real-world tasks that involve following manuals spanning hundreds of pages with complex, interdependent guidelines. In this paper, we introduce Tasks over Application Manuals (TAM), a benchmark for evaluating long-horizon procedural reasoning. We construct TAM by curating real-world tasks from two domains: ICD-10-CM clinical coding (mapping medical conditions to diagnostic codes) and U.S. federal sentencing (computing crime sentencing guideline outcomes, specifically offense levels), with human-validated labels. Each task requires following an authoritative manual with tens of thousands of rules and executing a sequence of interdependent steps across different sections to produce an exact answer. We evaluate general-purpose prompting approaches, including retrieval-augmented generation, ReAct-style prompting, and an agent-harness baseline on GPT-5, and find that the best exact-match performance remains extremely low: 1% on ICD-10-CM coding and 15.5% on sentencing tasks. These results show that current benchmarks may overestimate LLM reasoning ability and miss a key challenge: reliably following long, rule-based procedures. The complete TAM data and code are publicly available.

</details>


### [110] [Kraken: LLM-based Speech-to-Speech Translation via Low-bitrate VQ and Dual-path Source Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13045)
*Hayato Futami,Hassan Shahmohammadi,Tushar Dhyani,Alkis Koudounas,Raphaël Lafargue,Yosuke Kashiwagi,Quentin Jodelet,Emiru Tsunoo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Kraken，一种基于低比特率语音token的语音到语音翻译（S2ST）模型。该模型采用单层向量量化重建自监督学习（SSL）特征的低比特率token，并设计了名为Autowave-X的独立token到波形解码器（同时以源语音为条件以提升非语言信息传递），从而放宽训练数据对齐约束。Kraken基于Qwen3-8B构建，使用15万小时多语言多任务语音数据训练，翻译质量优于SeamlessM4T-Large v2和Qwen2.5-Omni，且说话人和韵律迁移能力更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于语音LLM的S2ST模型虽然能实现联合优化并保留非语言信息，但面临两大挑战：一是LLM难以直接预测高比特率语音token；二是严重依赖训练数据中说话人身份和韵律理想对齐的约束。本文旨在通过更优的token设计和解码器架构来缓解这两大问题。

Method: 1）采用基于单层向量量化（single-layer VQ）训练重建SSL特征的低比特率token，降低LLM预测难度；2）设计独立的token到波形解码器Autowave-X，并以源语音为条件输入，提升非语言信息（说话人和韵律）的迁移能力，从而放宽训练数据约束；3）将预训练LLM（Qwen3-8B）增强为接收语音特征输入、输出低比特率token，再由Autowave-X声码器合成波形；4）使用15万小时多语言多任务语音数据进行训练。

Result: 实验表明Kraken在翻译质量上优于SeamlessM4T-Large v2和Qwen2.5-Omni，并在说话人特征和韵律迁移能力上也有显著提升。

Conclusion: 通过低比特率token与源语音条件声码器的结合，Kraken证明了可以在降低LLM预测难度的同时提升非语言信息迁移质量，从而放宽了S2ST对训练数据对齐的要求，为基于LLM的S2ST提供了一条有效路径。

Abstract: Speech-to-speech translation (S2ST) has advanced significantly with speech LLMs, offering the potential for joint optimization and preserving non-linguistic information. However, these models struggle with predicting high-bitrate speech tokens in LLMs, and face the challenge of relying on S2ST training data with ideally aligned speaker identity and prosody. We propose using low-bitrate tokens based on single-layer vector quantization, trained to reconstruct self-supervised learning (SSL) features. We also employ a separate token-to-waveform decoder named Autowave-X, which is also conditioned on the source speech to improve non-linguistic transfer, thereby relaxing the training data constraints. With the integration of these techniques, we propose an S2ST model named Kraken, which augments a pre-trained LLM with speech feature inputs and the low-bitrate token outputs, followed by Autowave-X vocoder. We built the model upon Qwen3-8B and trained it using 150k hours of multilingual and multitask speech data. We demonstrated that our model exhibited better translation quality than SeamlessM4T-Large v2 and Qwen2.5-Omni, along with improved speaker and prosody transfer capabilities.

</details>


### [111] [Expert-Space Exploration in MoE Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13058)
*Hongyi He,Zhenghao Lin,Xiao Liu,Peng Cheng,Yan Lu,Yeyun Gong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出专家空间探索强化学习（ESRL），一种面向MoE大模型的架构感知RL框架。它通过扰动专家路由来增加rollout多样性（类似提高解码温度），同时仅在高置信度锚点专家与合理候选池中进行随机路由以避免质量下降，根据路由器熵自适应调整扰动强度，并在策略优化中回放实际使用的专家路径以缓解路由失配。实验表明ESRL在多种MoE路由（top-K、top-1、共享专家）及数学、科学、代码任务上均达到最优，且无需额外采样或计算成本；在Qwen3-30B-A3B上比GRPO平均提升Pass@1 3.2个百分点、Pass@8 4.5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对MoE模型的RL研究主要聚焦优化稳定性与训练效率，将专家选择视为固定组件。作者通过实证发现，扰动专家路由能有效改变模型输出并增加rollout多样性（效果类似提高解码温度），但直接扰动会激活不合适的专家、大幅降低rollout质量。因此需要一种架构感知的框架来有控制地探索专家路由空间。

Method: 提出ESRL框架：1) 将高置信度专家保留为锚点，把随机路由限制在可行的候选池内，保留可靠计算路径；2) 依据路由器熵自适应调整扰动强度以避免过度扰动；3) 在rollout时记录实际使用的专家路径，并在策略优化阶段回放这些路径以缓解扰动引入的路由失配。

Result: 实验表明ESRL在top-K、top-1、共享专家等多种MoE路由结构以及数学、科学、代码任务上均取得最佳性能，且不增加额外采样或计算成本。在Qwen3-30B-A3B上取得所有对比方法中最佳结果，较GRPO平均提升Pass@1 3.2个百分点、Pass@8 4.5个百分点。专家利用率和训练动态分析进一步验证了利用MoE路由结构对RL训练的益处。

Conclusion: 利用MoE特有的路由结构进行有控制的专家空间探索，可有效增加rollout多样性并提升RL后训练性能，同时避免直接扰动带来的质量退化。ESRL证明了架构感知的随机路由探索在MoE强化学习中的价值。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become central to post-training of large language models. Recent advances in RL for Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models have primarily focused on improving optimization stability and training efficiency, while treating the expert selection as a fixed component. Since routing determines the sparse computation paths that induce output distributions, expert selection offers an additional source of rollout diversity. Through empirical analysis, we find that perturbing expert routing effectively alters model output and increases rollout diversity, which is similar to increasing the decoding temperature. However, direct perturbation can activate unsuitable experts and substantially degrade rollout quality. Motivated by these observations, we introduce Expert-Space Exploration Reinforcement Learning (ESRL), an architecture-aware framework that explicitly explores the expert-routing space of MoE models. ESRL preserves high-confidence experts as anchors, and restricts stochastic routing to a plausible candidate pool, thereby retaining reliable computation paths. The perturbation strength is further adapted according to router entropy to avoid over-perturbation. To mitigate the routing mismatch introduced by perturbation, ESRL records the expert paths used during rollout and replays them during policy optimization. Experiments demonstrate that ESRL achieves the best performance across MoE backbones with top-K, top-1, and shared-expert routing, as well as across mathematics, science, and code tasks without additional sampling or computational cost. Specifically, ESRL on Qwen3-30B-A3B achieves the best among all compared methods, improving average Pass@1 and Pass@8 over GRPO by 3.2 and 4.5 percentage points, respectively. Further analyses of expert utilization and training dynamics provide insights into how exploiting MoE-specific routing structure benefits RL training.

</details>


### [112] [Continue, Adapt, or Yield: In-Turn Adaptation to Overlapping Speech in Full-Duplex Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13117)
*Yunqi Lu,Tyler Baumgartner,Nikhil Johri,Brandon Tai,Candice Fan,Luc Debaupte,Ruben Aguilar,Bill Wang,Yi Zhong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Duplex Cue，一种用于全双工语音智能体“话轮内适应”（in-turn adaptation）评估的新方法，将听者意图（反馈、协作或打断）与说话者行为（继续不变、话轮内适应、让位）分离。基于300个人类确认的语音线索单模型案例研究，人类说话者适应占68.2%，而PersonaPlex仅34.8%，表明自然语音交互评估必须同时测量智能体如何回应听者贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全双工评估仅用“是否继续说话或停止”这种二元指标，无法表达人类常用的第三种反应：在继续说话的同时融合听者刚贡献的内容（如遗漏词、纠正或澄清）。这一缺陷导致评估无法捕获自然对话中关键的适应性行为。

Method: 引入Duplex Cue评估框架：将听者意图（反馈backchannel、协作collaboration、打断interruption）与说话者行为（继续不变、话轮内适应、让位）分离；适应包括认可（acknowledgment）和内容修订（content revision）。在单模型案例研究中，收集300个来自非脚本英语对话且经人工确认的线索，对比记录的人类回应与回放听者音频下PersonaPlex生成的续接，保留208对有效样本。

Result: 在66个协作对中，记录的说话者在68.2%的情况下表现出适应，而PersonaPlex仅34.8%；模型在其余情况下继续不变（42.4%）或让位（22.7%）。

Conclusion: 研究证明，评估自然语音交互不仅需要衡量智能体是否持续说话，还需要衡量其如何回应听者的贡献；二元“继续说/停止”评估不足以反映人类日常使用的复杂交互模式。

Abstract: Full-duplex evaluation often emphasizes whether an agent keeps speaking or stops. That binary cannot express a third response humans use routinely: continuing to speak while incorporating what the listener just contributed. The contribution may be a missing word, a correction or a clarification. We introduce Duplex Cue, an evaluation of this \emph{in-turn adaptation} in full-duplex voice agents. Duplex Cue separates listener intent (backchannel, collaboration, or interruption) from speaker behavior: continuing unchanged, adapting within the turn, or yielding. Adaptation includes acknowledgment as well as content revision. In a single-model case study using 300 human-confirmed cues from unscripted English conversations, we compare recorded human responses with PersonaPlex continuations generated while replaying the listener's audio. We retain 208 pairs with the ongoing speaker active at cue onset and a scorable response in each condition. On the 66 collaborative pairs, recorded speakers adapt in 68.2\% of cases, compared with 34.8\% for PersonaPlex. The model otherwise continues unchanged (42.4\%) or yields (22.7\%). These findings show why evaluating natural voice interaction requires measuring how an agent responds to a listener's contribution as well as whether it keeps speaking.

</details>


### [113] [SAS: Simple Attention Sparsification via End-to-End Optimization of Context Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13141)
*Zhiwei Li,Lei Zhu,Hao Gu,Xiang Hu,Yan Wang,Haitao Mi,Sirui Han,Leo Liang,Zhijiang Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出简单注意力稀疏化（SAS），一种门控稀疏注意力机制，通过将选择器的连续分数注入注意力 logits，使上下文单元排序能够端到端地由语言建模损失优化，从而解决现有可训练的 Top-K 稀疏化方法因硬选择阻断梯度、依赖稠密注意力蒸馏而产生的排序与预测影响不对齐问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可训练注意力稀疏化方法采用轻量选择器对上下文单元（token或块）打分，再通过硬 Top-K 选择，这会阻断来自语言建模损失的梯度。为此这些方法普遍蒸馏逐层稠密注意力分布，使选择器按原始模型的稠密注意力权重排序，但这种排序并未直接对齐其在固定注意力预算下对预测的影响，可能将有限的预算浪费在低价值单元上。

Method: SAS 的核心思想是在训练时将选择器的连续分数注入注意力 logits，使损失能够通过标准反向传播更新选择器。论文识别出几个关键设计选择：门控以对数形式置于注意力 softmax 内部、使用归一化 softmax 门控以校准历史上下文与始终保留的当前块、保留连续选择器分数以使模型学习相对优先级而非仅硬选择。同时实现了一个内存高效的 Triton 内核，将 SAS 集成到 FlashAttention 风格的计算中以支持长序列训练。

Result: 在推理、长上下文理解和智能体任务上，SAS 在各种注意力预算下持续优于可训练稀疏注意力基线，尤其是在紧预算下取得更大增益，证明了其在下游任务中更有效的上下文排序能力。

Conclusion: 通过端到端优化上下文排序而非简单的稠密注意力蒸馏，SAS 更有效地利用有限的注意力预算，为预训练 Transformer 的后训练注意力稀疏化提供了更实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Post-training attention sparsification reduces the quadratic cumulative attention cost of pretrained Transformers by selecting a small set of context units (tokens or blocks) for each query. Existing trainable methods usually use a lightweight selector to score context units, followed by hard Top-K selection that blocks gradients from the language modeling loss. Consequently, these methods commonly distill layer-wise dense attention distributions. Although this encourages the selector to rank context units by dense attention weights in the original model, the ranking is not directly aligned with their impact on predictions under a fixed attention budget (i.e., the number of attended context units per query), potentially wasting the limited budget on less useful units. To address this misalignment, we propose Simple Attention Sparsification (SAS), a gated sparse attention mechanism that optimizes context ranking end-to-end with the language modeling loss. The key idea is to inject the selector's continuous scores into attention logits during training, allowing the loss to update the selector through standard backpropagation. We identify several choices crucial for this simple design to work well in practice: placing the gate inside the attention softmax in log form, using normalized softmax gates to calibrate historical context against the always-retained current block, and preserving continuous selector scores so the model learns relative priorities rather than only hard selections. To support long-sequence training, we implement a memory-efficient Triton kernel that integrates SAS into FlashAttention-style computation. Across reasoning, long-context understanding, and agentic tasks, SAS consistently outperforms trainable sparse attention baselines across attention budgets, with especially large gains under tight budgets, demonstrating more effective context ranking for downstream tasks.

</details>


### [114] [Type Diversity Enables Transformers to Generalise Compositionally](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13144)
*Anssi Moisio,Mathias Creutz,Mikko Kurimo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出，Transformer在结构泛化上比词汇泛化更难并非模型固有属性，而是源于此前数据集中词汇类型多样性高、结构类型多样性低的偏差。作者通过语法框架（Grammatical Framework）生成了COGS和SLOG数据集的语言学多样性变体，系统改变词法与结构类型多样性，发现类型多样性与词汇和结构测试案例中的组合泛化同等相关，从而支持其假设，并与前人关于"复合发散"解释的命题相矛盾。文章还进一步考察了其他数据集属性（如新颖测试结构之外的其他类型多样性、逻辑语义格式的表层属性）对组合泛化的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前人研究发现结构泛化比词汇泛化更难，但这种差异可能并非Transformer架构的固有局限，而是源于特定数据集在词法类型与结构类型多样性上的不均衡设计。作者希望通过控制类型多样性来检验这一差异的真正成因，从而澄清组合泛化难点的来源。

Method: 作者定义"类型多样性"为某种类型下不同构造子（constructor）的数量，而非具体的词组合。利用语法框架生成COGS和SLOG数据集的语言学多样化变体，系统性地改变词法类型和结构类型的多样性水平，以此测试多样性对词汇与结构测试案例组合泛化的影响。

Result: 实验发现，类型多样性在词汇和结构测试案例中与组合泛化的相关性相同，即结构泛化难度并非Transformer固有属性，而是由词法与结构类型多样性的不平衡造成。同时，结果与前人提出的"复合发散"（compound divergence）解释存在矛盾。

Conclusion: 结构泛化之所以看似更难，其实是数据集类型多样性配置不均所致；类型多样性是解释组合泛化表现的关键因素。此外，其他数据集属性（如非新颖测试结构中的类型多样性、逻辑语义格式的表层特性）也会影响组合泛化，值得进一步研究。

Abstract: Compositional generalisation has been divided into lexical and structural generalisation. Previous work has found that structural generalisation is harder than lexical for Transformers. We propose that this difference is not inherent to Transformers, but due to the high diversity of lexical types and low diversity of structural types in the specific datasets of these previous works. By type diversity we mean the number of different constructors of that type, instead of, for example, the specific word combinations that might populate the structure. To test this, we vary the amounts of type diversity of lexical and structural types in previously published datasets. We create linguistically diverse variants of the COGS and SLOG datasets using Grammatical Framework. We find that type diversity correlates with compositional generalisation equally in lexical and structural test cases, supporting our hypothesis. We note a contradiction with the proposition in previous work that compound divergence explains the difficulty in compositional generalisation tasks.
