Method: LAMAE是多模态、结构感知的掩码自编码器。与事后融合不同，它通过共享潜在注意力模块在潜在空间中直接交换信息，该模块基于"研究-视图-实体"层级结构运行，能够聚合不同数量的观测并优雅处理缺失模态，在自监督预训练阶段联合学习患者级表征。

Result: 在超120万MIMIC-IV住院记录上预训练后，LAMAE在院内死亡预测、ICD-10/DRG编码、住院时长等多模态住院任务上超过模态特异性预训练及相关对比/视觉-语言基线，在单模态任务上仍具竞争力。即便测试时仅提供单一模态，这些优势依然存在。

Conclusion: 同时建模模态内与模态间结构能够产生更鲁棒、更具迁移性的表征，验证了在预训练阶段进行结构感知的多模态潜在空间融合优于事后融合或单模态预训练。

Abstract: Cardiovascular diagnosis rests on integrating complementary modalities, like ECG, echocardiography, chest radiographs, and clinical variables, each capturing distinct but correlated aspects of cardiac physiology. Yet most medical foundation models remain modality-specific, combining modalities only for finetuning or post-training. This discards the cross-modal evidence clinicians naturally integrate and ignores the structure within each modality. We introduce Latent-Attention Masked Autoencoders (LAMAE), a multimodal, structure-aware masked autoencoder that jointly learns patient-level representations during self-supervised pretraining. Rather than fusing modalities post hoc, LAMAE exchanges information directly in the latent space through a shared latent-attention module operating over a study-view-entity hierarchy, enabling aggregation of variable observations and graceful handling of missing modalities. Pretrained on over 1.2 million MIMIC-IV hospital stays, LAMAE outperforms modality-specific pretraining and strong contrastive and vision-language baselines across multimodal hospital-stay tasks, such as in-hospital mortality, ICD-10 and DRG coding, and length of stay, while remaining competitive on unimodal tasks. These gains persist even when only a single modality is available at test time, showing that modeling both intra- and inter-modal structure yields more robust, transferable representations.

</details>


### [214] [Language Is an Insufficient Substrate for Quantitative Reasoning, and Consequential Domains Need Large Quantitative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12105)
*Reuben Vandeventer,David Imrem,David J. Wild*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出，将大语言模型（LLM）直接应用于关键定量决策（如定价风险、资本配置、患者分诊、遏制网络入侵）的前提假设是错误的。因为LLM基于人类语言描述训练，而描述是对定量记录的“有损编码”，这种损失不可逆，任何规模的模型都无法从描述中恢复其未编码的信息。作者将此形式化为训练表示的性质而非模型容量，并识别出关键场景所需的另外三个性质——可复现性、从每个输出回溯到源记录的血统（lineage）以及校准的不确定性——这些都无法由语言基座（substrate）通过构造保证。基于此，作者论证这些性质定义了一个独特的模型类别，即“大定量模型”（LQM）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战机器学习领域的主流假设：即大语言模型的进步将推动定价风险、资本配置、患者分诊、入侵遏制等关键定量决策的进步。作者指出，语言模型训练于人类描述产生的世界表征，而描述是对定量记录的“有损编码（lossy encoding）”，这种损失是不可逆的——任何规模的下游模型都无法从描述中恢复描述未编码的信息。这一根本性缺陷促使作者重新审视问题本质并定义新模型类。

Method: 将可恢复性缺陷形式化为“训练所用表征的性质，而非模型容量的性质”，从而将问题焦点从模型规模转移到数据表示层面。随后进一步识别出关键定量场景对模型额外要求的三个性质：可复现性（reproducibility）、从每个输出回溯至产出它的源记录的血统（lineage）、以及校准的不确定性（calibrated uncertainty）。作者论证这些性质无法由语言基座通过构造保证（by construction），进而提出这些性质定义了一个独特模型类别——大定量模型（LQM）。

Result: 论文的核心结果是提出并论证了一个新的模型类别“大定量模型（LQM）”。其关键论点包括：（1）语言表征对定量记录的有损编码是不可逆的，这是表征层面而非容量层面的根本限制；（2）语言基座无法从构造上保证可复现性、完整血统和校准的不确定性这三个关键场景的关键性质；（3）因此需要一种区别于LLM的模型类别来应对关键定量决策。

Conclusion: 作者得出结论：LLM在语言层面上的进步不足以支撑关键定量决策的可靠应用，因为语言描述本身是有损且不可逆的编码，且语言基座无法构造性地满足可复现性、血统和校准不确定性等关键性质。这些性质共同定义了“大定量模型”（LQM）这一独特的模型类别，为关键定量决策领域指明了需要不同于语言模型的技术路径。

Abstract: The prevailing assumption in applied machine learning is that progress on consequential quantitative decisions such as pricing risk, allocating capital, triaging patients, or containing a network intrusion will follow from progress in large language models (LLMs). A language model is trained on a representation of the world that was produced by human description; description is a lossy encoding of the quantitative record, and the loss is irreversible: no downstream model, at any scale, can recover from a description what the description did not encode. We formalize this as a property of the representation on which a model is trained rather than of the model capacity, and we identify three further properties that consequential settings demand of a model and that a language substrate cannot supply by construction: reproducibility, lineage from every output back to the source records that produced. it, and calibrated uncertainty. We argue that these properties define a distinct model class, which we call the Large Quantitative Model (LQM).

</details>


### [215] [DU-NO: A Parameter-Efficient Double U-Shaped Neural Operator for Phase-Resolving Wave Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12115)
*Enrique Hernandez Noguera,Md Meftahul Ferdaus,Nathan Cooper,Elias Ioup,Mahdi Abdelguerfi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DU-NO（双U型神经算子），一种多尺度U型谱神经算子，仅在两个最浅的编码器和解码器层级附加轻量卷积U-Net分支。通过深度递减的模态调度将模型控制在364万参数（比U-FNO低一个数量级），在FUNWAVE-TVD基准上取得最优自回归推演误差，比U-FNO提升14.9%且参数减少10.8倍；参数匹配对照实验证实增益来自架构本身（最优基线仍落后28.6%）；在2D Navier-Stokes和PDEBench浅水推演上也表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相位解析波浪模型（如FUNWAVE-TVD）是近岸动力学精度标准，能解析单个波浪的浅化、折射和破碎，但计算成本使其无法用于业务化预报所需的集合模拟、不确定性量化和实时预警。神经算子承诺以极小成本实现求解器级精度，但现有精确方案（如U-FNO）参数量巨大，需要更高效的架构设计。

Method: 提出DU-NO架构，采用双U型结构的谱神经算子：仅在两个最浅的编码器和解码器层级附加轻量卷积U-Net分支。其放置依据采样论证——高波数内容仅存在于细网格上，因此局部全频带通路放在高波数内容所在处（浅层细网格），而粗网格的带限层级保持纯谱处理。采用深度递减模态调度，将模型控制在364万参数。

Result: 在公开的FUNWAVE-TVD基准上，六个相同训练架构中DU-NO取得最佳自回归推演误差，比U-FNO提升14.9%且参数减少10.8倍；频段分析显示增益在所有频段均成立，包括截断谱算子失效的高波数频段。参数匹配对照组证实增益是架构性的：调整到相同360万参数预算后，最优基线仍落后28.6%。在2D Navier-Stokes上与最强基线相当，在PDEBench浅水推演上明显胜出。

Conclusion: DU-NO通过将局部卷积通路有选择性地部署在浅层（高波数内容所在处），以远低于现有混合谱卷积算子（如U-FNO）的参数量，实现了更优的波浪场预测精度，且泛化到Navier-Stokes和浅水方程等其他流体动力学问题，为业务化波浪预报中的集合模拟与实时预警提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Phase-resolving wave models such as FUNWAVE-TVD are the accuracy standard for nearshore dynamics, resolving the shoaling, refraction, and breaking of individual waves, but their cost rules them out for the ensembles, uncertainty quantification, and real-time warning that operational forecasting demands. Neural operators promise solver-level accuracy at a fraction of that cost, yet on wave-dominated fields the accurate ones are large: hybrid spectral-convolutional operators such as U-FNO (the strongest baseline in our study after DU-NO) buy their fidelity with tens of millions of parameters. We introduce DU-NO (Double U-shaped Neural Operator), a multiscale U-shaped spectral operator that attaches lightweight convolutional U-Net branches only at its two shallowest encoder and decoder levels. The placement follows a sampling argument: high-wavenumber content exists only on fine grids, so the local, full-band pathways go where that content lives, while the coarse, band-limited levels stay purely spectral. A depth-decaying mode schedule holds the model to 3.64M parameters, an order of magnitude below U-FNO. On our publicly released FUNWAVE-TVD benchmark, DU-NO attains the best autoregressive rollout error of six identically trained architectures, improving on U-FNO by 14.9% with 10.8x fewer parameters, and a frequency-band analysis shows the gain holds across all bands, including the high-wavenumber band where truncated-spectral operators collapse. Parameter-matched controls confirm the gain is architectural: rescaled to the same 3.6M budget, the best baseline still trails DU-NO by 28.6%. The advantage carries beyond nearshore waves: DU-NO matches the strongest baselines on 2D Navier-Stokes and wins clearly on PDEBench shallow-water rollouts. Code, trained models, and evaluation artifacts are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/duno-code-5A7B/.

</details>


### [216] [Mined from Scientific Literature: Process Schemas for Atomic Layer Deposition and Etching in Materials Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12139)
*Sameer Sadruddin,Eleni Poupaki,Alex Watkins,Bora Karasulu,Adriaan J. M. Mackus,Erwin Kessels,Sören Auer,Jennifer D'Souza*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对原子层沉积（ALD）和原子层刻蚀（ALE）在实验与模拟文献中报告方式不一致、难以比较和机器复用的问题，提出了四个经领域专家评审的JSON Schema，分别覆盖ALD/ALE的实验与模拟过程。这些Schema通过schema-miner工具构建，并基于QUDT（量化、单位、维度、类型本体）进行语义锚定，涵盖材料、工艺条件、配置以及测量或预测结果。作者比较了各Schema的范围、结构和语义基础，并通过ORKG模板展示了其在文献结构化提取与发布中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ALD和ALE在材料科学中的实验与模拟文献报告存在高度异质性，导致结果难以跨文献比较与机器可操作复用，阻碍了数据共享与自动化分析。

Method: 采用schema-miner/schema-miner pro工具构建Schema，并基于QUDT进行语义锚定，由领域专家评审校验；随后比较各Schema的范围、结构与语义基础，并通过ORKG模板验证其用于文献提取与结构化记录发布的效果。

Result: 成功生成了四个经领域专家评审的JSON Schema（ALD实验、ALD模拟、ALE实验、ALE模拟），实现了对ALD/ALE过程数据的结构化描述，并通过ORKG模板演示了Schema引导的文献提取与结构化记录发布。

Conclusion: 所提出的Schema为ALD与ALE领域提供了标准化、语义锚定的数据描述框架，促进实验与模拟数据的可比较性和机器可复用性。

Abstract: Atomic layer deposition (ALD) and atomic layer etching (ALE) are reported heterogeneously across experimental and simulation literature in materials science, hindering comparison and machine-actionable reuse. We present four domain-expert-reviewed JSON Schemas for ALD and ALE experimental and simulation processes. Curated with schema-miner and grounded in QUDT using schema-miner pro, the schemas structure materials, process conditions, configurations , and measured or predicted results. We compare their scope, structure, and semantic grounding, and demonstrate their use for schema-guided literature extraction and publication of structured records through ORKG templates.

</details>


### [217] [Can LLMs in Draft-Verify-Revise Pipelines Resolve Deictic Ambiguity?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12162)
*Obinna I. Ekekezie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM编排模式"草稿-验证-修订"（draft-verify-revise）中的指示转移（deictic shift）现象：随着上下文在三个阶段间级联，不同阶段的大语言模型可能对"previous"这类上下文依赖表达产生不同的指代解析。研究使用10个基础示例的三条件合成数据集，测试了六种模型（来自三家供应商）在21种推理努力配置下的表现，采用e值进行序贯检验。结果显示平衡准确率从低于随机水平的0.156到接近完美不等，其中GPT-5.2从无推理时的0.156提升到最高推理水平时的0.942，而Gemini 3 Pro在所有水平下均保持在0.94以上，且在低推理水平下以约5%的成本超越了GPT-5.2在xhigh推理水平下的表现。当元评估器出错时，倾向于依赖表面线索而非操作性推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: draft-verify-revise是扩展推理时计算（inference-time compute）的常见LLM编排模式，但不同阶段模型对上下文依赖表达式（如"previous"）的指代解析可能不一致，导致指示转移（deictic shift）问题。作者旨在系统量化这一现象的影响，并考察推理努力（reasoning effort）配置对元评估器修正能力的作用，为上下文工程（context engineering）实践提供理论依据。

Method: 采用合成数据集（10个基础示例×3种条件），固定共享组件不变，系统变化草稿阶段模型（assistant）与验证阶段模型（grader）是否正确解析表达式，以及修订阶段模型（meta-evaluator）所需独立推理量。对三家供应商的六种模型在21种推理努力配置下进行测试，使用e值进行序贯假设检验。主实验与消融实验（移除grader的误差分类标签）并行，另用独立LLM分析meta-evaluator错误裁决的陈述理由。评估指标为平衡准确率（敏感度与特异度的未加权均值）。

Result: 平衡准确率跨度极大，从0.156（低于随机）到接近完美。GPT-5.2从无推理的0.156升至最高推理水平（0.942），而Gemini 3 Pro在任何水平下均高于0.94。Gemini 3 Pro在低推理水平下的成绩超过GPT-5.2在xhigh推理水平下的成绩，且单次试验成本约为其5%。分析表明，meta-evaluator出错时倾向于借助表面线索而非操作性推理（operational reasoning）。

Conclusion: 指示转移是draft-verify-revise管道中的真实风险，且不同模型对推理努力（reasoning effort）的敏感性差异显著，成本效益差异巨大。实施此类管道的上下文工程师应对指示转移保持警惕，并在每个阶段显式标注预期指代对象（intended referent），以降低跨阶段上下文级联导致的歧义解析错误。

Abstract: Draft-verify-revise is a common LLM orchestration pattern for scaling inference-time compute. One LLM drafts, a second critiques the draft and provides feedback, and a third uses that feedback to revise the draft into the final output. As context cascades between stages, LLMs at different stages can resolve a context-dependent expression such as "previous" differently. When that happens, the expression undergoes a deictic shift, a change in what it refers to. This phenomenon was studied with a synthetic dataset of 10 base examples, each rendered in three conditions. Holding the shared components constant, the conditions varied whether the draft stage LLM (the assistant) or the verify stage LLM (the grader) resolved the expression correctly, and how much independent reasoning the revise stage LLM (the meta-evaluator) needed to determine which reading was correct. Six models from three providers were tested across 21 reasoning effort configurations using e-values for sequential testing, in a primary experiment and an ablation experiment that removed error classification labels from the grader's feedback. A separate LLM analyzed the meta-evaluator's stated rationale for each wrong verdict. Balanced accuracy (the unweighted mean of sensitivity and specificity) ranged from 0.156, below chance, to near-perfect. GPT-5.2 rose from 0.156 without reasoning to 0.942 at its highest reasoning effort level, while Gemini 3 Pro stayed above 0.94 at every level. Gemini 3 Pro at low reasoning effort outscored GPT-5.2 at xhigh reasoning effort for roughly 5% of the cost per trial. When the meta-evaluator erred, it tended to rely on surface cues rather than operational reasoning. Context engineers implementing draft-verify-revise pipelines should be wary of deictic shifts and make the intended referent explicit at each stage.

</details>


### [218] [GLARE: Generative Learning via Adversarial Reward Estimation For Social Dynamics Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12165)
*Tenghao Huang,Zhaoxuan Tan,Muhao Chen,Jonathan May,Mengting Wan,Longqi Yang,Pei Zhou,Sihao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出会议动态预测基准MDFB（含2,207场真实会议和24,794个面向未来的查询），用于评估模型在给定会议转录前缀和活动问题下生成多轮续篇的能力；并引入GLARE方法，将对抗模仿学习适配到条件语言生成，通过判别器奖励和KL正则化提升生成续篇的实用性和人类相似度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 会议续接需要追踪议程、发言角色、参与者意图和分歧，现有基准大多要求精确复现真实未来，不适用于开放式的多轮生成。本文旨在构建一个不依赖精确匹配、能评估实用性和人类相似度的基准，并开发能提升续篇质量的生成方法。

Method: 构建MDFB基准，包含真实会议和未来面向的查询；设计评估协议，从效用（对问题的推进程度）和人类相似度（对话流畅性与角色一致性）两个维度评价。提出GLARE方法：将对抗模仿学习用于条件语言生成，判别器对真实续篇和当前actor样本排序，其得分作为KL正则化的策略奖励，并通过当前策略负样本重训练让奖励景观随actor演化。

Result: GLARE在人类评估中平均胜率为0.66（效用）和0.70（人类相似度），优于SFT和SPIN，但仍低于真实人类续篇。MDFB还可作为社会推理竞技场，通过参考辅助判断比较通用模型（包括闭源系统）。

Conclusion: MDFB既能用于任务特定学习，也能用于基于输出的会议行为评估；GLARE展示了对条件语言生成进行对抗模仿学习的有效性，但仍有提升空间。

Abstract: Meeting continuation requires tracking the agenda, speaker roles, participant intentions, and disagreement across long multi-party discussions. We introduce the Meeting Dynamic Forecasting Benchmark (MDFB), constructed from 2,207 real-world meetings and 24,794 future-facing queries. Given a transcript prefix and an active question, a model generates a plausible multi-turn continuation in one call. We evaluate utility---progress toward the question---and human-likeness---plausible conversational flow and role consistency---without requiring exact reproduction of the observed future. We further present GLARE, an adaptation of adversarial imitation learning to conditional language generation. A discriminator ranks the observed continuation above samples from the current actor, and its score supplies a KL-regularized policy reward; retraining on current-policy negatives allows the reward landscape to evolve with the actor. GLARE attains average human-evaluated win rates of 0.66 on utility and 0.70 on human-likeness, outperforming SFT and SPIN while remaining below the observed human continuation. We also demonstrate MDFB as a social reasoning arena for comparing general-purpose models, including closed-source systems, through reference-assisted judgments. Together, these studies illustrate the benchmark's use for both task-specific learning and output-based evaluation of meeting behavior.

</details>


### [219] [Soft Symbol Grounding for Prototypical Concepts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12247)
*Marcos Galván-López,Nijesh Upreti,Hiram Calvo,Carlos Aguilar-Ibáñez,Vaishak Belle*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Soft-PNet，一种无需任务特定损失函数设计的神经符号原型网络方法。它将概念接地重构为在预计算的可行符号解缓存上的 Metropolis 游走，由每个概念仅单个标注锚点的原型分布引导，并通过单一 KL 目标进行训练。该目标跨任务完全一致，且在解空间无法枚举时仍适用。在 MNIST-EvenOdd、Visual Sudoku 和 Kand-Logic 上，稀疏监督条件下 Soft-PNet 在概念与标签层面匹敌损失工程化的原型网络，能恢复软接地基线遗漏的概念，且无需损失工程、训练时间更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经符号模型通常仅用最终标签监督训练，中间概念未被观测，导致多种概念赋值与给定标签一致，模型可能预测正确标签却恢复错误概念，即"推理捷径"问题。原型网络通过将每个概念锚定到少量标注样例来缓解捷径，但现有方法仍需针对每个任务手工设计任务特定的可微损失，耦合感知与推理，无法跨任务复用。

Method: Soft-PNet 移除了任务特定的可微损失，将概念接地重构为在预计算的可行符号解缓存上的 Metropolis 游走；用每个概念仅一个标注锚点构建原型分布，在原型加权缓存与网络概念预测之间通过单一 KL 目标训练模型。此目标跨任务一致，并能在解空间无法穷举时生效。

Result: 在 MNIST-EvenOdd、Visual Sudoku 和 Kand-Logic 三个基准上、稀疏监督条件下，Soft-PNet 在概念级和标签级指标上均匹敌（或达到）损失工程化的原型网络，并成功恢复了软接地基线未能捕获的概念；同时无需损失工程，训练时间更低。

Conclusion: Soft-PNet 证明通过统一、跨任务的 KL 目标（基于预计算符号解缓存与单锚原型分布）即可实现概念接地的正确性，无需手工设计任务特定损失，在降低训练开销的同时有效缓解推理捷径，为神经符号结合提供了更通用且可扩展的范式。

Abstract: Neuro-symbolic models are usually trained with supervision only on final labels, leaving the intermediate concepts unobserved. Since many concept assignments are consistent with a given label, training can predict labels correctly while recovering the wrong concepts, a failure known as a reasoning shortcut. Prototypical networks reduce shortcuts by anchoring each concept to a few labeled examples, but existing methods still couple perception and reasoning through a hand-crafted, task-specific differentiable loss that must be redesigned for every task. We introduce \textbf{Soft-PNet}, which removes this loss: it reframes concept grounding as a Metropolis walk over a precomputed cache of feasible symbolic solutions, guided by a prototype distribution built from a single labeled anchor per concept, and trains against one KL objective between the prototype-weighted cache and the network's concept predictions. The objective is identical across tasks and remains applicable when the solution space cannot be enumerated. On \texttt{MNIST-EvenOdd}, Visual Sudoku, and \texttt{Kand-Logic} under scarce supervision, Soft-PNet matches loss-engineered prototypical networks at the concept and label levels and recovers concepts that soft-grounding baselines miss, with no loss engineering and lower training time.

</details>


### [220] [GTA: Graph Theory Agent and Benchmark for Algorithmic Graph Reasoning with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12265)
*Zixiang Xu,Yanbo Wang,Chenxi Wang,Lang Gao,Zirui Song,Yue Huang,Zhaorun Chen,Xiangliang Zhang,Xiuying Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 GT Bench，一个覆盖 24 个经典图论问题、44 种任务结构设置、超过 100,000 个样本的基准，跨越自然语言、结构化语言、邻接表和邻接矩阵四种表示。评估八个 LLM 发现：准确率与输入表示强相关，最优表示随图的密度、规模、拓扑和模型而变化，且这种敏感性在最强推理模型中依然（减弱地）存在。据此提出 Graph Theory Agent (GTA)，通过偏好训练的表示选择器加上 plan-and-decompose 脚手架包裹冻结的执行 LLM，在 easy/hard split 上将 Phi-4 分别从 53.5%/33.0% 提升至 69.1%/41.5%，超越八个提示与智能体基线，且无需重训练即可迁移至 GraCoRe 和 NLGraph。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估存在局限：任务简单、图规模小、偏重代码生成而非对图本身的推理、或固定单一输入格式，无法可靠衡量 LLM 在语言中执行多步图算法的能力，因此缺乏一个系统、全面、对表示敏感的图论推理基准。

Method: 构建 GT Bench 基准（24 个经典图问题 × 44 种任务结构设置 × 4 种表示，超 10 万样本），在八个 LLM 上系统评估表示、图密度/规模/拓扑与准确率的关系；基于观察提出 GTA：一个偏好训练的表示选择器负责挑选最优表示，配合 plan-and-decompose 推理脚手架，调用冻结（frozen）的 executor LLM 完成图推理。

Result: 表示选择显著影响准确率，最优表示随图属性与模型变化；GTA 将 Phi-4 的 easy split 从 53.5% 提升到 69.1%、hard split 从 33.0% 提升到 41.5%，优于八个提示与智能体基线，且可无重训练迁移到 GraCoRe 与 NLGraph。

Conclusion: LLM 在图论推理上存在强烈的表示敏感性，且这种敏感即使在最强推理模型中仍有衰减性保留；通过自动选择适配表示并配合规划-分解脚手架，可显著且可迁移地提升冻结 LLM 的多步图推理能力，为结构数据上的 LLM 推理提供了新的基准与方法路径。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly asked to reason over structured data such as graphs, yet how reliably they can carry out multi-step graph algorithms in language remains unclear. Existing evaluations tend to use simple tasks on small graphs, to score code generation rather than reasoning over the graph itself, or to fix a single input format. We introduce Graph Theory Bench (GT Bench), a benchmark covering 24 classical graph problems in 44 task-structure settings, with over 100,000 examples across four representations: natural language, structured language, adjacency list, and adjacency matrix. Evaluating eight LLMs on GT Bench shows that accuracy is strongly tied to the input representation, that the best representation shifts with graph density, size, and topology as well as with the model, and that this sensitivity persists, attenuated, in the strongest reasoning models. Building on these observations, we propose the Graph Theory Agent (GTA), which pairs a preference-trained representation selector with plan-and-decompose scaffolding around a frozen executor LLM. GTA lifts Phi-4 from 53.5% to 69.1% on the benchmark's easy split and from 33.0% to 41.5% on its hard split, outperforming eight prompting and agent baselines, and transfers without retraining to GraCoRe and NLGraph. Code for benchmark generation and evaluation: https://github.com/xzx34/GTA. The project homepage is available at https://xzx34.github.io/gta/.

</details>


### [221] [Learning Symbolic Constraint Representations from Examples: A Neuro-Symbolic Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12267)
*Nassim Belmecheri,Arnaud Gotlieb,Nadjib Lazaar,Helge Spieker*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种神经符号框架用于自动约束获取（CA），通过引入可学习的神经Oracle Transformer模型来模拟用户响应并泛化概念知识，大幅减少对人工交互的依赖。该学习型Oracle与专用CA引擎FastCA协同工作，将神经网络预测系统地提炼为一致、可解释的约束网络，从而无需先验领域知识即可从数据中恢复结构化符号模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有约束获取方法虽然能学习用户定义的概念为约束网络，但严重依赖与人类Oracle的密集交互，导致学习过程在时间和查询次数上代价高昂。作者旨在减少用户参与度，实现自动化的约束获取。

Method: 提出神经符号框架：1) 引入神经网络Oracle Transformer模型，基于已有示例训练以模拟用户响应并泛化概念知识；2) 该学习型Oracle与专门的CA引擎FastCA交互，后者系统性地将Oracle响应精炼为健全（sound）、一致（consistent）且可解释的约束网络；3) 通过神经符号交互实现从数据中恢复结构化符号模型，无需先验领域知识。

Result: 实验结果表明，该神经符号交互有效地将数据驱动的模式识别与符号推理相结合，为组合领域中自动化模型构建提供了稳健的途径。

Conclusion: 神经符号协同作用能够有效弥合数据驱动模式识别与符号推理之间的鸿沟，实现组合领域中模型构建的自动化，减少人工参与并保持结果的可解释性与健全性。

Abstract: Learning user-defined concepts as constraint networks has been extensively studied in the constraint acquisition (CA) literature. However, existing approaches typically rely on intensive interactions with a human oracle, making the learning process costly in terms of time and number of queries. In this paper, we propose a neuro-symbolic framework for automatic CA that significantly reduces user involvement by introducing neural Oracle Transformer models which learn to emulate user responses and to generalize conceptual knowledge. Trained on previously available examples, the learned oracle interacts with a dedicated CA engine, FastCA, which systematically refines the oracle's responses into a sound, consistent, and interpretable constraint network. This neuro-symbolic interaction enables the recovery of structured symbolic models from data without prior domain knowledge. Our results demonstrate that this neuro-symbolic interplay effectively aligns data-driven pattern recognition with symbolic reasoning, offering a robust approach to automating model construction in combinatorial domains.

</details>


### [222] [T-GADE: Thermodynamical Generative-AI-Driven Evolution of LLM Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12286)
*Kyoko Ogawa,Naoki Mori*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出T-GADE，通过LLM遗传算子和工件级多样性评估扩展热力学遗传算法，以演化同时具有描述和代码的结构化工件。采用统一自由能目标支持世代与稳态更新，Fermi型占有排除重复基因型而Bose型允许重复。在EPO线上装箱任务中，世代Bose型T-GADE在T=0.003时将中位训练超量从1.152%降至0.815%（约29%降幅，p=0.042），验证选取达到与EoH相同的中位迁移超量0.496%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将演化计算与大语言模型（LLM）结合需要同时控制种群多样性与生成能力。LLM输出中具有显式结构（如描述与代码配对）的工件需要专门的演化与多样性控制机制，以更有效地搜索启发式规则。

Method: 提出T-GADE算法：通过LLM遗传算子（变异等）和工件级多样性评估扩展热力学遗传算法。采用统一自由能目标支持世代（generational）与稳态（steady-state）两种更新模式，其中Fermi型占有排除重复基因型，Bose型允许重复基因型。建立了精确的单成员移除条件，并给出恢复演化启发式（EoH）零温生存规则的条件。

Result: 在EoH论文的在线装箱任务上：训练超量用搜索实例衡量（相对体积下界的箱数开销），迁移超量用另一箱容量的实例衡量。世代Bose型T-GADE在T=0.003时，20次运行/配置下中位训练超量从1.152%降至0.815%（约29%降幅；双侧Mann-Whitney p=0.042，Cliff's δ=0.378）。验证选取其两个最高排名最终候选之一，达到与EoH相同的中位迁移超量0.496%。

Conclusion: 结果表明热力学选择机制以及基于验证对保留工件的使用具有实用性，T-GADE在训练和迁移性能上均有效，验证选择能等同甚至匹配现有EoH方法的迁移表现。

Abstract: Integrating evolutionary computation and large language models (LLMs) requires control of population diversity as well as generative capability. Among LLM outputs, those with explicit structure, such as a description paired with code, are structured artifacts; we use artifact for short. We propose T-GADE, which evolves these artifacts by extending thermodynamical genetic algorithms through LLM-based genetic operators and artifact-level diversity evaluation. A common free-energy objective supports generational and steady-state updates, with Fermi-type occupancy excluding repeated genotypes and Bose-type occupancy permitting them. We establish exact one-member removal and conditions for recovering the zero-temperature survival rule of Evolution of Heuristics (EoH). On the online bin-packing task studied in the EoH paper, excess measures relative bin-count overhead above a volume lower bound. Training excess uses search instances; transfer excess uses instances with another bin capacity. Generational Bose-type T-GADE at $T=0.003$ reduced median training excess by approximately 29%, from 1.152% to 0.815%, over 20 runs per configuration (two-sided Mann-Whitney $p=0.042$, Cliff's $δ=0.378$). Validation selection among its two highest-ranked final candidates reached the same median transfer excess as EoH, 0.496%. These results demonstrate the utility of thermodynamical selection and validation-based use of retained artifacts.

</details>


### [223] [Robust Prototypical Networks for Few-Shot Sensor Fault Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12287)
*Mohammed Ayalew Belay,Amirshayan Haghipour,Pierluigi Salvo Rossi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出多片段原型网络（MEPN），通过聚合多个不相交支持片段的原型均值作为最终类代表，在不改变编码器架构的前提下降低原型方差，从而提升工业故障诊断在极低样本（few-shot）场景下的稳定性与准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业故障诊断通常仅有少量带标签故障样本，少样本学习具有吸引力。标准原型网络简单有效，但在极低样本（very-low-shot）场景下，每个类别决策仅依赖很小的支持集，导致类原型不稳定、方差大，进而影响分类性能。

Method: 提出多片段原型网络（MEPN）：从多个不相交的支持片段（support episodes）中分别计算各类原型，再取它们的均值作为最终类别代表。该方法不改变编码器架构，仅通过原型聚合降低原型估计方差。

Result: 在DeFACTO传感器数据集上采用五路故障分类（合成偏置、漂移、尖峰、噪声故障注入真实工业测量数据）。100次独立运行下，MEPN在单片段单样本设置（K=1，聚合N_agg=10个支持片段）下显著高于单片段基线。在等量10样本支持预算下，MEPN与K=10的ProtoNet在统计上无显著差异，证实原型累积是提升机制而非固定预算下学习能力更强。

Conclusion: MEPN通过多片段原型聚合有效降低原型方差，在极低样本故障诊断中优于单片段原型网络。与等预算的ProtoNet（K=10）性能相当，说明其优势来自原型累积机制而非额外样本信息，验证了该方法在不改变编码器条件下提升低样本鲁棒性的可行性。

Abstract: Industrial fault diagnosis often operates with only a handful of labeled fault examples, making few-shot learning attractive for sensor monitoring. Standard prototypical networks are simple and effective; however, their class prototypes may become unstable in the very-low-shot regime because each decision relies on a small support set. We propose \emph{Multi-Episode Prototypical Networks} (MEPN), which aggregate prototypes from multiple disjoint support episodes and use their mean as the final class representative, reducing prototype variance without changing the encoder architecture. We evaluate MEPN on the DeFACTO sensor dataset using five-way fault classification with synthetic bias, drift, spike, and noise faults injected into real industrial measurements. Over 100 independent runs, MEPN reaches \textbf{\SensorOneShotGcpn\%} in the per-episode one-shot setting ($K\!=\!1$ shot, aggregated over $N_{\text{agg}}\!=\!10$ support episodes), substantially above single-episode baselines. Under an equal 10-sample support budget, MEPN and ProtoNet at $K\!=\!10$ are statistically indistinguishable, confirming prototype accumulation as the mechanism rather than superior fixed-budget learning.

</details>


### [224] [Hybrid Physics-AI Framework of Body Center of Mass Dynamics from Wrist-Worn Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12304)
*Shuhao Que,Valentina Breschi,Ying Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种简化运动学模型（KM），用于将腕部IMU测量映射到身体质心（COM）加速度，并进一步提出了三种混合AI建模方法（HKM-NN），即串行学习（ser-）和两种并行学习（sim1-、sim2-）。在10名健康志愿者、六种步态活动和坐站转移（SS）动作的数据集上验证，KM模型误差为6.7%-12.5%（步态）和5.6%（SS），而HKM-NN模型将误差降至5.3%-9.3%（步态）和3.9%（SS）。在噪声测试下，sim1-/sim2-对高斯扰动更具鲁棒性，KM对椒盐噪声更具鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腕部佩戴的IMU虽被广泛用于日常健康监测，但它无法完全代表全身动力学特征，而身体质心（COM）被认为是生理参考标准。因此，需要构建一种能够利用腕部IMU测量来估计COM加速度的方法，以更准确地进行健康监测。

Method: 首先提出一个基于多项简化假设的简化运动学模型（KM），使动态方程可仅凭腕部IMU测量求解。随后提出三种混合AI建模方法（HKM-NN），将灰盒模型（KM的生物力学结构）与黑盒模型（神经网络）相结合，包括串行学习（ser-）和两种并行学习（sim1-、sim2-）方式。使用自有数据集训练和测试，包含10名健康志愿者在六种步态活动和坐站转移（SS）期间的腕部IMU数据及COM真值测量。

Result: KM模型误差为步态活动6.7%-12.5%、SS为5.6%。HKM-NN模型性能显著提升：步态误差降至5.3%-9.3%，SS最佳误差为3.9%。鲁棒性方面，sim1-/sim2-在高斯扰动下保持更强鲁棒性，而KM模型在椒盐噪声下表现出相对较强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 从腕部IMU测量估计COM加速度是可行的。将生物力学结构与数据驱动学习相结合，能显著提升可穿戴传感应用在不完美、含噪测量条件下的性能，凸显了混合建模方法在该领域的重要价值。

Abstract: Wrist-worn IMU has been widely used for daily-life health monitoring. Yet, it does not fully represent whole-body dynamics, for which the body center of mass (COM) is considered the physiological reference standard. Therefore, this work proposes a simplified kinematic model (KM), which is designed to map the wrist IMU to the COM acceleration. It is built upon several reductive assumptions that enable the solvability of the dynamic equations based on wrist IMU measurements alone. This work further proposes three types of hybrid AI modeling methods, namely human kinematic model-based neural network (HKM-NN) models, to leverage the power of both grey-box and black-box modeling. The HKM-NN methods include serial learning (ser-) and two approaches of simultaneous learning (sim1- and sim2-). The proposed models are trained and tested using our dataset, which includes wrist IMU measurements and ground-truth COM measurements from 10 healthy volunteers during six gait activities and sit-to-stand (SS) transitional movement. The results demonstrate the feasibility of estimating COM acceleration from wrist IMU measurements. Our KM model yields satisfactory results, with an error ranging from 6.7% to 12.5% for gait activities and 5.6% for the SS. In comparison with the KM model, our HKM-NN models significantly enhance the performance, achieving 5.3% to 9.3% errors for gait activities, and the best error of 3.9% for the SS. In addition, the HKM-NN models demonstrate distinct robustness characteristics under noisy test conditions, with sim1-/sim2- generally maintaining greater robustness under Gaussian perturbations, while the KM model exhibits comparatively strong robustness under salt-and-pepper noise. These findings highlight the importance of combining biomechanical structure with data-driven learning for wearable sensing applications operating under imperfect and noisy measurement conditions.

</details>


### [225] [Do Influence-Derived Data Perturbations Enable Machine Unlearning? A Controlled Study of Three Plausible Roles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12313)
*Chenkai Wu,Chrispine Kambimbi,Qinyang Zeng,Jun Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了深度扰动学习（DPL）在机器遗忘中的三种角色：直接删除信号、效用保持正则化器、对抗遗忘的冷启动。作者发现公开实现存在两个正确性问题（图像方向计算在增强归一化张量上却应用于原始图像，标签扰动低于float32分辨率而未改变标签）。修正图像扰动流程后，DPL在CIFAR-10/ResNet-18上全部三个配对种子均未通过直接删除标准；其效用影响在不同种子间符号不一致，计入方向计算时间后表现不如简单冷启动基线。单种子Tiny ImageNet检查同样不偏好DPL；由于发布的代码预处理不一致，直接比较无法得出结论。研究仅覆盖随机实例删除，未排除其他删除机制下基于影响的方案。作者发布了角色匹配的评估协议和扰动型删除声明的审计清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作将DPL定位为机器遗忘的三种角色（直接删除信号、效用保持正则化器、对抗遗忘的冷启动），且较弱的角色证据常被用来支持较强的直接删除主张。作者旨在通过严格的匹配协议和精确种子重训练基线，分别独立检验每种角色，以澄清DPL的真实有效性并揭露实现中的缺陷。

Method: 采用角色匹配的评估协议，使用精确种子重训练作为基线。作者对公开实现进行审计，识别出两个正确性bug并予以修正。随后在CIFAR-10/ResNet-18上以三个配对种子分别测试直接删除、效用保持和冷启动三种角色；并在单种子Tiny ImageNet上进行补充验证。最后发布角色匹配评估协议和扰动型删除声明的审计清单。

Result: 审计发现图像方向在增强归一化张量上计算但应用于原始图像，以及标签扰动低于float32精度导致标签实际未变。修正图像扰动后，DPL在三种子下均未通过直接删除标准；效用影响符号在不同种子间不一致；计入方向计算时间后DPL劣于简单冷启动基线。Tiny ImageNet单种子检查不支持DPL作为正则化器或冷启动，且因代码预处理不一致使直接比较不具结论性。研究范围仅限随机实例删除。

Conclusion: DPL在机器遗忘中最强的直接删除主张不成立，其效用保持与冷启动角色的证据亦不充分且不稳定。先前用较弱角色支持较强角色的论证逻辑存在缺陷。作者呼吁采用角色匹配的严格协议与审计清单，以更可靠地评估扰动型删除方法；同时也指出本研究仅针对随机实例删除，未否定影响型方法在其他删除机制下的价值。

Abstract: We evaluate Deep Perturbation Learning (DPL), which perturbs training images and labels along influence-derived directions, in three roles in which prior work has positioned it for machine unlearning: a direct deletion signal (the strongest claim), a utility-preserving regularizer, and a warm start for adversarial unlearning. Evidence for the weaker roles has been used to support the stronger one, so we test each role separately under a matched protocol with exact-seed retraining baselines. An audit of the public implementation identifies two correctness issues: image directions are computed on augmented, normalized tensors but applied to raw images, and the label perturbation falls below float32 resolution, leaving labels unchanged. After correcting the image-perturbation pipeline, DPL fails the direct-deletion criterion on CIFAR-10/ResNet-18 in all three paired seeds. Its utility effects are inconsistent in sign across seeds, and once direction-computation time is counted it underperforms simple warm-start baselines. A one-seed Tiny ImageNet check likewise does not favor DPL as a regularizer or warm start; preprocessing inconsistencies in the released code make the direct comparison there inconclusive. These results cover random instance deletion only and do not rule out influence-based methods in other deletion regimes. We release a role-matched evaluation protocol and an audit checklist for perturbation-based deletion claims.

</details>


### [226] [AIM: A Privacy-Aware Interoperable Memory Framework for Multi-Agent Multi-User LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12320)
*Zachary Johnson,Nigel Boachie Kumankumah,Somya Chatterjee,Tejas Sathyamurthi,Min Chen,Xinyi Alice Li,Xiao Wang,Emily Morgan Gelchie,Jessica Lin,Sadid A. Hasan,Sulaiman Vesal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AIM（Agentic Interoperable Memory），一个统一且隐私感知的多智能体、多用户大语言模型记忆框架，可持久化地管理私有与共享记忆，并通过索引级访问控制实现隐私保护。同时引入MUMBench（多用户记忆基准），这是首个用于多用户环境下评估检索、创建、更新、删除等多类记忆操作的数据集。在MUMBench上，AIM取得了96.0%的可见性分类准确率、58.8%的严格操作准确率和70.5%的状态感知操作准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统大语言模型被限定于单个用户会话，知识局限于单次对话，无法学习随时间演化的用户偏好。现有智能体记忆系统虽然解决了这一问题，但通常仅在单用户层面运作，限制了可跨用户共享以改进下游响应的公共知识。因此需要一种既能保护私有数据又能实现跨用户有益知识共享的统一记忆框架。

Method: AIM框架动态地将信息分类为私有（仅限单个用户访问，对其他用户不可见）或公共（所有用户均可访问），并实施索引级访问控制，使私有记忆仅能被其所有者检索，从而保护敏感数据的同时允许有益的共享知识提升协调与一致性。此外，作者构建了包含四个领域的多用户交互数据的MUMBench数据集，用于评估多用户环境下的多种记忆操作。

Result: 在MUMBench上进行的三次独立运行中，AIM实现了96.0%的可见性分类准确率、58.8%的严格操作准确率和70.5%的状态感知操作准确率。

Conclusion: AIM通过统一的、隐私感知的多用户记忆管理框架，在保护个体数据隐私的同时实现了跨用户的共享知识利用，显著提升了多智能体、多用户LLM系统的记忆管理能力。MUMBench作为首个多用户记忆基准，为未来该领域的研究提供了标准化的评估平台。

Abstract: Traditional large language models (LLMs) are scoped to individual user sessions, limiting their knowledge to a single conversation and preventing them from learning user preferences that evolve over time. Existing agentic memory systems address this limitation but generally operate at the individual-user level, restricting the public knowledge that could be shared across users to improve downstream responses. We introduce AIM (Agentic Interoperable Memory), a unified, privacy-aware memory framework that enables multi-agent, multi-user LLM systems to persistently manage private and shared memory. AIM dynamically classifies information as private, scoped to one user and inaccessible to others, or public, accessible to all users. It enforces index-level access controls so that private memories are retrievable only by their owner, protecting sensitive data while allowing beneficial shared knowledge to improve coordination and consistency. We also introduce MUMBench (Multi-User Memory Benchmark), a dataset of multi-user interactions containing private and shareable information across four domains. To our knowledge, MUMBench is the first public dataset designed to evaluate multiple memory operations, including retrieval, creation, update, and deletion, in a multi-user environment. Across three independent runs on MUMBench, AIM achieves 96.0% visibility classification accuracy, 58.8% strict operation accuracy, and 70.5% state-aware operation accuracy.

</details>


### [227] [Affective Agent: On-Device Personalized Intervention Reasoning for Wearable Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12322)
*Reina Mun,Zishen Wan,Vijay Janapa Reddi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Affective Agent，一种面向可穿戴级硬件的三层参考架构，用于在不确定性条件下进行个性化干预推理。它结合亚十亿参数语言模型与生理证据、情境和用户历史，在无云依赖、无需逐用户重训练的情况下决定是否、何时以及如何干预，并通过宿主管理的结构化记忆演化实现个性化，而无需权重更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 情感计算已推动可穿戴状态推断的进展，但设备端关于是否、何时以及如何干预的推理仍具挑战性，这限制了从状态推断走向闭环个性化干预的路径。

Method: 提出三层交互式架构（感知层、个性化层、推理层），使用紧凑的亚十亿参数语言模型结合生理证据、情境和用户历史进行干预决策；通过宿主管理的结构化记忆演化而非逐用户权重更新来适配个体；采用两遍结构化推理。

Result: 在室内环境质量控制场景中，基于模拟器生成的纵向场景（涵盖生理变化、情境、信号质量和干预历史）进行评估，结果显示记忆驱动的个性化和两遍结构化推理在合成评估中改善了干预决策。

Conclusion: 通过将决策层移至设备端，该工作展示了一条从可穿戴状态推断走向闭环、个性化干预的路径，证明了可在可穿戴级硬件上实现。

Abstract: Affective computing has advanced wearable state inference, but on-device reasoning about whether, when, and how to intervene remains challenging. We present Affective Agent, a three-layer reference architecture for personalized intervention reasoning under uncertainty on wearable-class hardware. It combines a compact sub-billion-parameter language model with physiological evidence, context, and user history to decide whether, when, and how to intervene, without cloud dependency or per-user retraining. The architecture is organized into three interacting layers (perception, personalization, and reasoning), adapting to individual users through host-managed structured memory evolution rather than per-user weight updates. We instantiate Affective Agent in indoor environmental quality control and evaluate it on held-out, simulator-generated longitudinal scenarios spanning physiological variation, context, signal quality, and intervention history. Results show that memory-driven personalization and two-pass structured reasoning improve intervention decisions within this synthetic evaluation. By moving the decision layer on-device, this work demonstrates a path from wearable state inference toward closed-loop, personalized intervention on wearable-class hardware.

</details>


### [228] [LoRA-RC: Reservoir Computing with Low-Rank Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12327)
*Wenbin Wan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LoRA-RC，一种针对储层计算（RC）的低秩在线自适应方法。它通过低秩修正矩阵在线调整循环权重矩阵，同时将修正矩阵投影到谱范数球上保证收缩性，并采用低通滤波保持稳定性。实验表明在Lorenz系统参数突变场景下，LoRA-RC相较固定储层和仅读出自适应分别降低了56%和51%的后漂移预测误差，且消融实验证明缺少投影会导致误差放大超过40倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 储层计算只训练线性读出层，虽快速且数据高效，但静态储层在系统漂移下性能退化。仅调整读出层不足以应对漂移，而无约束的储层自适应会破坏RC赖以可靠的回声状态特性和增量稳定性。因此需要一种既能在线自适应储层、又能保证稳定性的方法。

Method: LoRA-RC通过在线流式预测误差驱动的低秩修正来调整循环矩阵。基础储层和自适应基固定离线，仅在线更新一个小核心矩阵。每步将该核心矩阵投影到谱范数球上并低通滤波，保证每个应用的循环矩阵都处于经认证的收缩集内，并建立沿每条在线自适应路径的增量输入-状态稳定性界，其速率和增益与路径无关。

Result: 在具有突发参数漂移的Lorenz系统上，LoRA-RC相较固定RC将漂移后预测误差降低56%，相较仅读出自适应降低51%。20个种子的消融实验显示，移除投影会导致误差放大超过40倍。

Conclusion: 低秩、受约束的储层自适应能在保持回波状态与增量稳定性的前提下，显著提升系统漂移下的鲁棒预测性能。谱范数投影是维持稳定性的关键机制。

Abstract: Reservoir computing (RC) trains only a linear readout over a fixed recurrent layer, making it fast and data-efficient for online prediction. However, a static reservoir degrades under system drift, readout-only adaptation is then insufficient, and unconstrained reservoir adaptation can destroy the echo-state and incremental stability properties that make RC reliable. This paper proposes LoRA-RC, which adapts the recurrent matrix through a low-rank correction driven by streaming prediction errors. The base reservoir and adaptation bases are fixed offline; a small core matrix is adapted online, projected onto a spectral-norm ball, and low-pass filtered at each step. The projection guarantees that every applied recurrent matrix remains within a certified contraction set, and an incremental input-to-state stability bound is established for the reservoir along each online adaptation path, with path-independent rate and gain. On a Lorenz system with an abrupt parameter drift, LoRA-RC cuts post-drift prediction error by 56% versus a fixed RC and 51% versus readout-only adaptation; ablations over 20 seeds show that removing the projection inflates this error by more than a factor of 40.

</details>


### [229] [Toward Robust Personalized Alignment for LLMs: Mitigating Persona Drift in Multi-Turn Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12373)
*Youyuan Zhang,Siyuan Li,Fangming Liu,Jing Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CORE框架，将单轮局部证据与持久人设状态修订相分离，通过不确定性感知的信念修订选择性更新用户偏好，以解决个性化语言模型中人设漂移（Persona Drift）问题；同时引入PERSIST基准用于评估后锚点人设状态鲁棒性，实验证明该方法在ALOE、PersonaChat和PERSIST上提升了个性化对齐与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人设漂移是个性化语言模型的核心挑战：用户画像在长期交互中是动态演化的而非固定不变。模型需要在用户偏好真正改变时修正持久人设状态，同时避免被瞬时的、模糊的或未解决的观察所误导。现有方法缺乏对何时应更新持久状态的显式控制。

Method: CORE框架的核心思路：将"单轮局部证据"（turn-local evidence）与"持久人设状态修订"（persistent persona-state revision）分离；通过不确定性感知的信念修订机制，只对有根据的用户偏好（grounded user preferences）进行选择性更新，从而区分真实偏好变化与瞬时干扰。

Result: 在ALOE、PersonaChat及新增的PERSIST基准上，CORE显著提升了个性化对齐与鲁棒性，并在归一化闭槽状态保真度（normalized closed-slot state fidelity）上取得互补增益。人工评估与机制控制实验表明，相比单纯增强生成能力或依赖持久记忆，显式的更新控制带来了额外收益。

Conclusion: 显式的信念更新控制优于单纯更强的生成能力或持久记忆机制，为个性化语言模型在人设动态演化场景下的稳健对齐提供了有效方案，并可通过PERSIST基准系统评估此类鲁棒性。

Abstract: Persona drift remains a central challenge for personalized language models, as user profiles evolve over long interactions rather than remain permanently fixed. Models must therefore revise persistent persona states when preferences genuinely change, while avoiding updates driven by transient, ambiguous, or unresolved observations. We propose CORE, which separates turn-local evidence from persistent persona-state revision and selectively updates grounded user preferences through uncertainty-aware belief revision. We also introduce PERSIST, a held-out post-anchor benchmark for persona-state robustness under sequential interaction stress, covering ambiguity, conflict, and controlled social influence. Across ALOE, PersonaChat, and PERSIST, CORE improves personalized alignment and robustness, with complementary gains in normalized closed-slot state fidelity. Human evaluation and mechanistic controls further support explicit update control beyond stronger generation or persistent memory alone.

</details>


### [230] [BlueLM-GUI Technical Report: A Real-Device-Centric Flywheel for Self-Improving Mobile GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12394)
*Tong Ye,Kunyang Han,Guozhi Wang,Longqiang Luo,Zhifeng Ding,Yongxiang Zhang,Xiaolei Shen,Yuxuan Zhang,Zhuping Zhang,Tao Xu,Yue Pan,Yucheng Zhao,Yupei Hu,Yuanjiang Ouyang,Danfeng Shen,Runqi Lin,Hongda Cai,Zhaoxiong Wang,Mengjia Yan,Yingjie Zhong,Chen Zhou,Zeyu Zhang,Xuwen Zhu,Penggang Shi,Mingcheng Luo,Ziyang Wu,Min Jin,Mingfu Shen,Zairong Xu,Fan Zhang,Hao Wang,Liang Liu,Zhulin Xie,Lijun Yao,Xiao Liang,Liangmin Wen,Liqiang Feng,Feilong Wu,Min Hu,Min Chen,Guanjing Xiong,Xiaohu Ruan,Xiaoxin Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BlueLM-GUI，一个35B-A3B规模的移动端GUI智能体，构建以真实设备为中心的迭代飞轮，通过"Every Sample Matters（每样本有价值）、Every Rollout Is Real（每次真实rollout）、Every Query Evolves（每个查询都演化）"三大原则，填补沙盒训练与生产环境的分布不匹配、真实设备故障利用不足、固定基准饱和三大工业化部署缺口。在MobileGUI-VBench上达到87.4分（超过最佳闭源模型5.1分），在AndroidWorld上达到84.9分（开源最佳、媲美闭源）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动端GUI智能体正从多模块框架转向端到端原生模型，但工业化部署面临三大持久缺口：1) 沙盒训练导致与生产环境的分布不匹配；2) 昂贵真实设备上的失败样本未被充分利用；3) 固定基准饱和后丧失指导迭代的能力。这三方面阻碍了模型能力的稳定提升与迁移。

Method: 提出以真实设备为中心的飞轮架构，通过三大原则实现闭环迭代。1) Every Sample Matters：采用双轨数据管线，包含"异构三系统共识评估"与"错误纠正与推导模块"，将每条轨迹（含失败轨迹）转化为可用监督信号；2) Every Rollout Is Real：设计三阶段配方——持续预训练、监督微调、以及在数百台真实手机上进行智能体强化学习，使每次rollout都落地于真实生产环境，所学能力直接迁移至部署；3) Every Query Evolves：采用配额驱动的基准方法论，配合三个正交评测轴，实现能力归因的精确性，并随模型增强而系统性升级基准。

Result: BlueLM-GUI在MobileGUI-VBench上取得87.4分，超越最佳闭源模型5.1分；在AndroidWorld上取得84.9分，为开源模型最佳结果，并与闭源模型相当。实验证明将训练与迭代改进锚定在真实设备及三大Every原则上，能产出强健、稳健且可迁移的移动GUI能力。

Conclusion: 将模型训练和迭代改进同时扎根于真实设备与"Every Sample Matters / Every Rollout Is Real / Every Query Evolves"三大原则，可系统性弥合工业化部署的三大缺口，从而获得强健、稳定且可直接迁移到生产环境的移动端GUI能力，为移动GUI智能体的工业化落地提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Mobile GUI agents are shifting from multi-module frameworks to native models trained end-to-end, yet industrial deployment faces three persistent gaps. Sandbox training produces a distribution mismatch with production environments; expensive real-device failures remain underutilized; and fixed benchmarks saturate, losing the power to guide iteration. We present BlueLM-GUI, a 35B-A3B mobile GUI agent built as a real-device-centric flywheel that closes these gaps through three principles. Every Sample Matters: a dual-track pipeline with Heterogeneous Triple-System Consensus evaluation and an Error Correction \& Derivation Module salvages every trajectory into usable supervision. Every Rollout Is Real: a three-stage recipe---continual pre-training, supervised fine-tuning, and agentic reinforcement learning on hundreds of real phones---grounds every rollout in real production environments, so the capability the model learns transfers directly to deployment. Every Query Evolves: a quota-driven benchmark methodology with three orthogonal axes enables precise attribution and allows the benchmark to be systematically upgraded as the model improves. BlueLM-GUI achieves 87.4 on MobileGUI-VBench, surpassing the best closed-source model by 5.1 points, and 84.9 on AndroidWorld, the best result among open-source models and competitive with closed-source models. These results demonstrate that grounding model training and iterative improvement in both real devices and the three Every principles yields strong, robust, and transferable mobile GUI capability.

</details>


### [231] [Is Gaussian Splatting Becoming Neural Again? A Taxonomy and Controlled Study of Learned Parameterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12395)
*YuanHang Wang,Xin Cao,Yi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统刻画了3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）中“神经化”参数生成的趋势，沿五个轴分析了19种代表性方法，并通过受控实验证明：共享外观与不透明度的神经函数能提升重建质量，而解码几何结构无额外收益，支持“选择性神经化”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS使用显式基元与高效光栅化，但近期系统越来越多地用神经网络生成或共享高斯参数。作者希望厘清这一趋势的本质，避免简单贴“神经/非神经”二元标签，并为设计选择提供依据。

Method: 提出五轴分类框架：属性解码、空间共享、视图条件解码、拓扑生成、摊销推理；对19种代表性方法进行归纳分析；并在受控的mip-NeRF 360实验中隔离三种神经参数化形式（共享外观、共享不透明度、解码几何结构）逐一评估。

Result: 发现这些设计选择分别解决不同局限，无法简化为二元神经标签。受控实验表明：共享外观和不透明度可提升重建质量；解码几何结构没有带来进一步收益。

Conclusion: 证据支持“选择性神经化”：当共享函数能捕捉可复用相关性且不牺牲显式泼溅的局部几何自由度时有利；几何结构应保留显式表示。

Abstract: Three-dimensional Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) combines explicit primitives with efficient rasterization, yet recent systems increasingly use neural networks to generate or share Gaussian parameters. We characterize this trend along five axes: attribute decoding, spatial sharing, view-conditioned decoding, topology generation, and amortized inference. An analysis of 19 representative methods shows that these choices address different limitations and cannot be reduced to a binary neural label. We also isolate three forms of neural parameterization in a controlled mip-NeRF 360 study. Sharing appearance and opacity improves reconstruction quality, while decoding geometric structure offers no further gain. The evidence favors selective neuralization: shared functions help when they capture reusable correlations without sacrificing the local geometric freedom of explicit splats.

</details>


### [232] [Niching Agents in The Core](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12398)
*Gary B. Parker,Jim O'Connor,John Asaro*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种名为"The Core"的竞争性协同进化算法，该算法无需传统适应度函数即可让智能体自主进化控制策略。研究在Xpilot视频游戏环境中，通过将智能体限制（niching）到原始环境的特定子集来扩展先前工作。结果表明，针对特定子环境进行生态位划分的智能体，其成功率优于针对整个系统及其它子环境划分的智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究表明The Core能够进化出具备战斗和导航能力的智能体，但尚未探索环境细分（niching）对进化效果的影响。本研究旨在验证将智能体限制到特定环境子集是否能提升进化性能。

Method: 采用The Core这一独特的竞争性协同进化算法，通过锦标赛选择、交叉和变异等局部交互机制实现智能体进化，无需传统适应度函数。对智能体进行生态位限制（niching），使其分别面向整个系统、特定子环境及不同的子环境子集，并在Xpilot视频游戏环境中进行对比实验。

Result: 实验结果表明，针对特定子环境进行生态位划分的智能体，其成功能力优于针对整个系统划分的智能体，也优于针对不同子环境划分的智能体。

Conclusion: 环境细分策略能够有效提升The Core协同进化算法的性能，将智能体限制到特定子环境有助于进化出更优的控制器，为无适应度函数的进化算法提供了一种有效的性能提升途径。

Abstract: The Core is a unique competitive co-evolution algorithm that allows agents to evolve autonomous control without utilizing a traditional fitness function. The agents evolve via local interactions through tournament selection, crossover, and mutation, producing offspring by evolving better controllers. Previous works have shown The Core's ability to evolve agents capable of combat and navigation in the Xpilot video game. This research expands upon that premise by niching agents to specific subsets of the original environment The Core was tested in. Our results demonstrate the niched agents capacity for success over agents niched to the entire system and agents niched to different sub-environments.

</details>


### [233] [OneLA: Scaling Linear-Attention Decoding to Large Beams in Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12399)
*Xiangrui Yang,Cheng Peng,Yunfeng Zhao,Liang Zeng,Ao Hu,Jiawei Yang,Shengzhe Wang,Jingshan Lv,Xiao Liang,Chen Yang,Jiaqiang Liu,Yiming Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OneLA 是一个针对生成式推荐（GR）工作负载的线性注意力解码框架，通过利用共享的提示词（prompt）和较短的分叉后缀，用单个共享状态加紧凑的追加式记录（append-only records）来表示所有 beam 状态，仅计算每个解码步骤所需的状态信息，无需为每个 beam 重建完整循环状态。配合轻量级祖先索引和融合 GPU 内核复用共享状态，实现了 1.54-2.46 倍的端到端解码加速，同时大幅降低循环状态内存占用和数据移动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐依赖大 beam 解码来生成数百个候选物品，这给循环线性注意力带来了扩展性挑战。现有的线性注意力服务系统要么为每个 beam 物化完整的循环状态，要么反复重放共享历史，导致大量内存和流量开销。核心动机是解决 GR 场景下大 beam 解码导致的循环状态内存与数据移动瓶颈。

Method: OneLA 利用 GR 工作负载"共享提示词 + 短分叉后缀"的结构特点：(1) 将所有 beam 状态表示为单个共享 prompt 派生状态加紧凑的追加式分叉转换记录；(2) 每个解码步骤只计算所需的状态信息，避免为每个 beam 重建完整循环状态；(3) 引入轻量级祖先索引追踪构成各 beam 历史的转换记录，使 beam 更新无需移动或复制已有记录；(4) 设计融合 GPU 内核在多个 beam 间复用共享状态。

Result: 分析表明 OneLA 实现了 1.54-2.46 倍的端到端解码加速，同时显著减少了循环状态内存占用和数据移动。

Conclusion: OneLA 通过共享状态表示和追加式记录策略，有效解决了生成式推荐中大 beam 解码下线性注意力服务的内存与流量开销问题，在不重建完整循环状态的前提下提供了解码加速，为 GR 工作负载下的线性注意力服务提供了高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Generative recommendation (GR) relies on large-beam decoding to generate hundreds of candidate items, creating a new scaling challenge for recurrent linear attention. Existing linear attention serving systems either materialize a full recurrent state for every beam or repeatedly replay shared history, incurring substantial memory and traffic overhead. To address this, we present OneLA, a linear-attention decoding framework that exploits the shared prompt and short divergent suffixes of GR workloads. Specifically, OneLA represents all beam states using a single shared prompt-derived state and compact, append-only records of their divergent transitions. Using this representation, OneLA computes only the state information required at each decoding step, without reconstructing a full recurrent state for every beam. Furthermore, OneLA uses a lightweight ancestry index to track the transition records that make up each beam's history, allowing beams to be updated without moving or copying existing records. A fused GPU kernel further reuses the shared state across beams. Our analysis shows that OneLA achieves 1.54-2.46x end-to-end decode speedups while substantially reducing recurrent-state memory use and data movement.

</details>


### [234] [Decentralized Evolution of Hexapod Gaits with Independent Leg Controllers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12400)
*Gary B. Parker,John Asaro,Jim O'Connor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种六足机器人步态优化的新方法，通过分散式进化算法独立演化每条腿的步态控制器，无需集中协调。在Webots模拟器和Mantis六足机器人上进行了验证，并与合作协同进化方法对比，表明该方法在生成稳定且自适应的步态方面更有效，同时展现出有趣的涌现协调行为，降低了步态优化的复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统六足机器人步态优化通常依赖集中式控制或复杂的协同机制，这增加了优化复杂度并限制了系统的可扩展性与自适应性。作者旨在探索一种分散式策略，通过独立演化各腿控制器来降低复杂度，同时借助涌现行为实现高效的协调运动。

Method: 采用分散式进化算法，为每条腿独立优化其控制器（无需中央协调）。实验平台为Webots模拟器与Mantis六足机器人。将该分散式方法与合作协同进化（cooperative coevolution）进行基准对比，以评估其生成稳定、自适应步态的能力以及涌现协调特性。

Result: 分散式方法相较于合作协同进化在生成稳定和自适应步态方面展现出更高的效能，并表现出有趣的涌现协调行为（即协调并非显式设计，而是在独立演化中自发产生）。

Conclusion: 独立演化各腿控制器可有效降低步态优化复杂度，同时保持甚至提升运动性能；该研究凸显了分散式策略在构建可扩展、自适应机器人系统中的潜力。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel approach to hexapod locomotion by evolving each leg's gait independently through a decentralized evolutionary algorithm. Using the Webots simulator and the Mantis hexapod robot, we optimize individual leg controllers without centralized coordination, allowing emergent behaviors to drive the development of efficient, coordinated locomotion. Our decentralized method is benchmarked against cooperative coevolution, demonstrating improved efficacy in generating stable and adaptive gaits while showing interesting emergent coordination. By enabling independent evolution of leg controllers, this method reduces the complexity of gait optimization and highlights the potential of decentralized strategies for scalable and adaptive robotic systems.

</details>


### [235] [Beyond ID Embeddings: Process-Grounded Language Modeling for Cognitive Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12403)
*Minghang Liu,Yuanzhuo Wang,Qiang Qiu,Huawei Shen,Xueqi Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出过程感知语言认知诊断（PLCD）框架，利用大语言模型构建概念图式与认知过程图，以语言结构作为认知先验，并用响应记录校准学生潜在状态，实现新练习/新概念的语义化诊断和冷启动鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统认知诊断模型采用离散ID嵌入表示学生、练习和概念，与学习者认知的符号化本质不符，且在新练习或新概念出现时存在语义局限，缺乏认知可迁移性与冷启动能力。

Method: PLCD包含三个核心组件：1) 用LLM构建概念图式与认知过程图作为认知先验；2) 目标条件化语义记忆检索与目标练习相关的历史响应；3) 过程导向的语言到认知映射器，采用DA-MoE专家与过程级对比学习，将文本证据映射到统一认知空间。

Result: 实验表明PLCD在预测学生表现上优于传统基线，并展现出较强的认知迁移能力，尤其在冷启动场景下表现突出。

Conclusion: 结构化语言先验经响应记录校准可提升认知诊断的冷启动鲁棒性与认知基础性，为连接LLM计算能力与心理测量目标提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Cognitive Diagnosis Models (CDMs) play a pivotal role in personalized online learning. Traditional CDMs rely on discrete, ID-based embeddings to represent students, exercises, and concepts. This paradigm diverges from the nature of learner cognition, where knowledge is not stored and retrieved as isolated symbols. As a result, CDMs suffer from semantic limitations when new exercises or concepts appear. In this paper, we propose a Process-aware Language Cognitive Diagnosis (PLCD) framework that uses language-derived structures as cognitive priors and response records to calibrate student posterior states. PLCD leverages large language models (LLMs) to construct concept schemas and cognitive process graphs, and uses target-conditioned semantic memory to retrieve historical responses that are relevant to each target exercise. A process-grounded Language-to-Cognition Mapper with DA-MoE experts and process-level contrastive learning then maps the textual evidence into a unified cognitive space. Experimental results show that PLCD not only outperforms traditional baselines in predicting student performance but also exhibits strong cognitive transfer capabilities. These results connect the computational power of LLMs with the psychometric goal of measuring latent knowledge states, suggesting that structured language priors calibrated by response records can improve cold-start robustness and cognitive grounding.

</details>


### [236] [VRL-Bench: Benchmarking agents on computer control tasks under finite trial budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12404)
*Yu Bai,Yukai Miao,Dawei Wang,Li Chen,Yanyu Ren,Yuqian Shi,Dan Li,Ying Xiong,Chengqiu Tan,Run Zhou,Li Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出VRL-Bench基准和VEX²调度器，用于公平评估有限试验预算下语言代理的试错学习，发现反思式口头记忆更新在某些场景提升成功率但在其他场景降低，而VEX²在所有六个设置中均取得正增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言代理在复杂任务（如计算机控制）中可通过试错学习提升表现，但现有方法（如Reflexion）在不同设置下的收益不一致，缺乏公平的评估基准来比较口头记忆更新方法。

Method: 构建VRL-Bench测试平台，在MiniWoB和WebShop上使用三个模型评估多种口头记忆更新方法（含Reflexion及其后续工作）；提出VEX²，一种基于语言模型的言语探索-利用调度器，联合选择策略并分配剩余试验预算。

Result: 各口头记忆更新方法在部分设置中比无记忆重试提高成功率，但在其他设置中降低成功率；重放实验显示使用反思可能减少成功率，存在利用经验与持续探索之间的权衡；VEX²是唯一在所有六种设置中取得正成功率增益的更新方法。

Conclusion: 言语反射并非总是有益，需在探索与利用间权衡；VEX²通过自适应调度策略与预算分配，能在多种设置下稳定提升试错学习效果，为语言代理的强化学习提供可靠改进。

Abstract: Learning from trial and error is a promising way to improve language agents on complex tasks such as computer control. Reflexion introduced verbal reinforcement learning, which turns failed trials into text that guides later attempts without updating model parameters. We introduce VRL-Bench, a harness for fair evaluation of trial-and-error learning under finite trial budgets. Across three models on MiniWoB and WebShop, we evaluate updates from several prominent verbal-memory methods spanning Reflexion and later work: each improves observed success over memory-free retry in some settings but reduces it in others. Replay experiments show that using reflection can reduce success rates, revealing a trade-off between exploiting experience and continued exploration. We propose VEX$^2$, a verbal exploration--exploitation scheduler that uses a language model to jointly select policies and allocate the remaining trial budget. VEX$^2$ is the only evaluated update to achieve positive observed success-rate gains over retry in all six settings.

</details>


### [237] [SoK: Rethinking Jailbreaking in the Era of Agentic AI: Attacks, Defenses, and Practical Consideration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12413)
*Md Jueal Mia,Yanzhao Wu,Selcuk Uluagac,M. Hadi Amini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过系统化知识（SoK）方法，重新审视现代LLM与智能体（agentic）AI时代下的越狱（jailbreak）安全研究。作者建立了覆盖用户交互、规划推理、记忆、工具调用和智能体间通信的统一攻击与防御分类体系，并提出安全-效用-效率三维评估框架。通过对照实验发现三个关键差距：强原生对齐不等于对抗性越狱鲁棒性；防御效果高度依赖模型、攻击和组件，且可能以过度拒绝、效用和延迟的显著代价为代价；最终响应攻击成功率低可能掩盖规划、记忆、工具交互等中间环节的严重妥协。因此主张从响应中心防御转向跨层、执行感知的安全防护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM正从对话助手演变为具备推理、规划、工具调用、持久记忆、多智能体通信和多步任务执行的智能体系统，同时现代模型的原生安全对齐较早期世代显著增强。这一转变引发根本性问题：早期基于对话式越狱研究的防御结论是否仍然适用于现代LLM与智能体AI时代。现有研究缺乏针对完整智能体执行管道的系统性安全分析。

Method: 采用系统化知识（SoK）方法：1）围绕智能体完整执行管道（用户交互、规划与推理、记忆、工具使用、智能体间通信）建立统一的攻击与防御分类体系；2）提出安全-效用-效率三维评估框架，将原生有害提示安全、对抗性越狱鲁棒性和智能体级安全结果分离；3）在统一智能体框架内开展代表性攻击与防御的受控实证研究。

Result: 实证研究揭示三个关键差距：(1) 强原生对齐并不必然带来对抗性越狱鲁棒性；(2) 防御有效性高度依赖模型、攻击类型和组件，并可能以过度拒绝、效用下降和延迟增加为显著代价；(3) 低最终响应攻击成功率可能掩盖严重的中间环节妥协——即使最终响应被成功过滤，规划、记忆和工具交互仍可能不安全。

Conclusion: 现有发现表明需要从以响应为中心的越狱防御，转向跨层、执行感知的安全机制，以保护智能体状态、组件转换和外部动作，同时兼顾实用效用与效率。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are rapidly evolving from conversational assistants into agentic AI systems that reason, plan, invoke tools, maintain persistent memory, communicate with other agents, and execute multi-step tasks. At the same time, modern models exhibit substantially stronger native safety alignment than earlier generations on which many jailbreak attacks and defenses were originally studied. This shift raises a fundamental question: \textit{which established jailbreak-security findings remain valid in the era of modern LLMs and agentic AI?} We address this question through a Systematization of Knowledge (SoK) that reframes jailbreak security around the full agentic execution pipeline. We develop unified taxonomies of attacks and defenses spanning user interaction, planning and reasoning, memory, tool use, and inter-agent communication, and introduce a security--utility--efficiency evaluation framework that separates native harmful-prompt safety, adversarial jailbreak robustness, and agent-level security outcomes. We further conduct a controlled empirical study of representative attacks and defenses within a common agentic framework. Our results reveal three important gaps. First, strong native alignment does not imply robustness to adversarial jailbreaks. Second, defense effectiveness is highly model-, attack-, and component-dependent and can come at substantial cost in over-refusal, utility, and latency. Third, low final-response attack success can mask severe intermediate compromise: planning, memory, and tool interactions may remain unsafe even when the final response is successfully filtered. These findings motivate a shift from response-centric jailbreak defense toward cross-layer, execution-aware security that protects agent state, component transitions, and external actions while preserving practical utility and efficiency.

</details>


### [238] [Hierarchical Belief Modeling for Zero-Shot Opponent Adaptation in Partially Observable Multi-Agent Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12422)
*Kowei Shih,Lu Cheng,Zeyu Wang,Yeyun Xu,Kejian Tong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HORIZON，一个针对Lux AI Season 3设计的层次化智能体，通过对称感知、双记忆信念追踪、遗物中心图注意力、信息增益驱动探索及对手条件化策略混合等组件，在部分可观测与随机化环境下实现战术执行与快速适应，并在PPO训练后于多个评估指标上超越强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lux AI Season 3环境具有部分可观测性、随机化回合级动态以及五局三胜的匹配结构，要求智能体同时具备战术执行能力和快速适应能力。现有的循环与前馈基线难以同时兼顾短视距控制与跨匹配元推理，因此需要设计一个能够在隐藏游戏参数与对手风格条件下有效决策的层次化智能体。

Method: HORIZON采用层次化架构，将短视距控制与跨匹配元推理分离。其核心组件包括：对称感知空间感知、双记忆信念追踪（显式推断隐藏游戏参数）、遗物中心图注意力机制、信息增益驱动的探索策略，以及对手条件化的策略混合。训练中利用辅助信念目标与世界模型目标稳定学习，并在大规模JAX模拟器中使用PPO算法进行训练。

Result: 实验结果表明，HORIZON在匹配胜率、回合胜率、适应增益和联赛评分等多个指标上均取得了相对于强循环与前馈基线的持续提升，验证了各组件协同作用带来的综合性能优势。

Conclusion: HORIZON证明了层次化架构结合信念追踪与对手建模在部分可观测、动态随机化对抗环境中的有效性，为复杂竞技场景下的智能体设计提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Lux AI Season 3 requires agents to act under partial observability, randomized episode level dynamics, and a best of five match structure that rewards both tactical execution and fast adaptation. We present HORIZON, a hierarchical agent that combines symmetry aware spatial perception, dual memory belief tracking, relic centric graph attention, information gain driven exploration, and an opponent conditioned policy mixture. HORIZON separates short horizon control from cross match meta reasoning, while auxiliary belief and world model objectives stabilize learning. Trained with PPO in a large scale JAX simulator, the resulting agent explicitly infers hidden game parameters and opponent style. Experiments show consistent gains in match win rate, episode win rate, adaptation gain, and league rating over strong recurrent and feed forward baselines.

</details>


### [239] [LifeFuse-Mem: Lifecycle-Aware State Fusion Against Temporary Overwriting for Long-Term Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12436)
*Hanyu Zhao,Yuqian Feng,Zhenyu Song,Yuanchao Cheng,Yance Jiao,Tengfei Pan,Li Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 LifeFuse-Mem，一种生命周期感知的神经记忆框架，用于解决长时运行 LLM 智能体因临时信息覆盖持久知识而导致的行为漂移问题。该框架通过生命周期标注的记忆设定和专门的记忆组件，将临时上下文与持久知识分开存储和更新，在受控反覆盖基准上显著减少覆盖，在公共长记忆基准上保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时运行的 LLM 智能体需要记忆机制来维持跨交互的连贯内部状态。现有记忆机制无法区分应当长期保留的信息与仅影响当前上下文的信息，导致临时信息覆盖持久知识，从而引发持久智能体的行为漂移。作者引入生命周期标注的记忆设定，以诊断和缓解紧凑在线记忆中的覆盖问题。

Method: 采用生命周期标注的记忆设定：训练阶段写入片段附带生命周期元数据，评估阶段使用相位感知的读取。在此设定下提出 LifeFuse-Mem 框架：使用专门的记忆组件将信息按其时间承诺（稳定 vs 瞬态）分离，通过生命周期感知的更新机制使稳定知识和瞬态知识各自局部演化，避免将临时上下文转化为持久状态。

Result: 在受控反覆盖基准上，LifeFuse-Mem 提升了获取控制的保持率，降低了临时覆盖；在两个公开长记忆基准上保持整体竞争力。

Conclusion: 显式的生命周期信号有助于诊断和缓解紧凑在线记忆中的覆盖问题，为持久型 LLM 智能体的记忆设计提供了有价值的方向。

Abstract: Long-running LLM agents require memory mechanisms that maintain coherent internal states across interactions. We study a lifecycle-labeled memory setting in which write episodes provide lifecycle metadata during training, and phase-aware readout is used during evaluation. This setting reflects the need to distinguish information that should remain influential across future interactions from information that should affect only the current context. A mismatch between these lifecycles can cause temporary information to overwrite durable knowledge, leading to behavioral drift in persistent agents. Within this setting, we introduce \textbf{LifeFuse-Mem}, a lifecycle-aware neural memory framework that separates information according to its temporal commitment. LifeFuse-Mem uses dedicated memory components and lifecycle-aware updates to allow stable and transient knowledge to evolve locally without converting temporary context into durable state. On the controlled anti-overwrite benchmark, LifeFuse-Mem improves acquisition-controlled retention and reduces temporary overwrite; on two public long-memory benchmarks, it remains broadly competitive. These results suggest that explicit lifecycle signals can help diagnose and mitigate overwrite in compact online memory.

</details>


### [240] [Do LLMs Trust the Accuser or the Accusation? Measuring Belief Shifts in Werewolf](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12446)
*Yu-Yu Yang,Ti-Rong Wu,Hung Guei,Hsing-Yu Chen,I-Chen Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于狼人杀游戏的信念转移评测基准，通过观测村庄阵营模型在收到每条怀疑/指控消息后的信念变化，来评估LLM代理的沟通能力。作者在1,224条标注消息上评测了40种开放权重LLM配置，发现更大的模型能更好地区分真假狼人，但指控仍强烈影响其信念；模型会更怀疑被指控者而降低对指控者的怀疑，尤其当指控者受信任时，即使指控者是狼阵营。总体而言，当前最高120B参数的开放权重LLM在将指控内容与来源信任整合方面仍有困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有狼人杀等社交推理游戏的LLM评估往往只依赖最终游戏结果，忽略了沟通过程中的信念变化。研究者希望通过分析信念转移来更细致地评测LLM的沟通与说服能力，理解模型如何整合消息内容与消息来源的可信度。

Method: 构建狼人杀信念转移评测基准：使用LLM进行游戏对局，标注怀疑与指控消息，并测量一个观测方（村庄阵营模型）在每条消息之后的信念变化。在1,224条标注消息上系统评测40种开放权重LLM配置，考察模型在消息前后对真狼人与村民的信念转移情况。

Result: 研究发现：更大的模型能更好地根据游戏历史区分真狼人与村民；但指控消息仍强烈左右模型信念，使其更怀疑被指控对象且降低对指控者的怀疑，尤其当指控者受信任时（即使指控者实为狼阵营）。较大的模型更能抵抗来自自己已不信任的指控者的指控。

Conclusion: 当前开放权重LLM（最高120B参数）在战略性沟通中仍难以将指控内容与来源信任进行有效整合。这表明现有模型在信任推理与内容判断协同方面存在明显局限，为后续社交推理与沟通能力评估提供了方向。

Abstract: Social-deduction games such as Werewolf are increasingly used to evaluate LLM agents, but existing evaluations often rely on final game outcomes. We propose a belief-shift evaluation benchmark in Werewolf for analyzing communication skills through belief updating. Using LLM-played games, we annotate suspicion and accusation messages and measure how an observing village-side model's beliefs change after each message. We evaluate 40 open-weight LLM configurations on 1,224 annotated messages. Our results show that larger models better distinguish true wolves from villagers based on game history, but accusations still strongly influence their beliefs. Models become more suspicious of the accused target and less suspicious of the accuser, especially when the accuser is trusted, even if the accuser is wolf-aligned. Larger models better resist accusations from accusers they already distrust. Overall, our findings suggest that current open-weight LLMs up to 120B parameters still struggle to integrate accusation content with source trust in strategic communication. Our benchmark and code are available at https://rlg.iis.sinica.edu.tw/papers/werewolf-accusation-benchmark.

</details>


### [241] [EvoRS: On-Policy Self-Evolution of Reward Systems for Open-Ended Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12459)
*Weiyuan Li,Aili Chen,Xintao Wang,Yikai Zhang,Qingqing Dong,Jinghan Xu,Hongru Hou,Wenxuan Zhao,Chengkun Lang,Jun Gao,Yuanli Guo,Hongcheng Guo,Yanghua Xiao,Deqing Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EvoRS提出一个自进化的强化学习框架，将奖励系统表示为一个可执行的奖励有向无环图（Reward-DAG），并由一个智能体设计者根据在线策略经验（on-policy rollouts与reward traces）持续更新奖励系统，以维持训练期间的可靠性。在写作和角色扮演任务中，EvoRS在三种评测下均取得最佳质量，超越基线策略2.107和4.767分，同时减少奖励黑客（reward hacking）与覆盖失败，保持奖励信息量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放式强化学习通常依赖基于评分标准（rubric）的奖励来处理无直接可验证答案的任务。然而，策略与奖励系统构成动态反馈回路：随着策略优化当前奖励，原本有效的奖励系统可能因奖励黑客或响应可区分度下降而变得不可靠。现有动态评分标准方法仅调整评估准则，但奖励失败也可能源于评分机制或信号组成。因此奖励系统应当在训练过程中持续演化，而非固定不变。

Method: EvoRS将奖励系统建模为可执行的Reward-DAG（奖励有向无环图），由智能体设计者（agentic designer）基于在线策略经验（on-policy rollouts）和奖励追踪（reward traces）迭代更新这一系统，从而在训练期间动态维护奖励的可靠性。该设计同时覆盖评估准则、评分机制和信号组成等多维度奖励失败的来源。

Result: 在写作（writing）和角色扮演（roleplay）两个任务上，EvoRS在全部三种评测器下均取得最佳生成质量，分别以2.107分和4.767分的优势超越基线策略。同时，EvoRS减少了奖励黑客现象与覆盖失败（coverage failures），并保留了奖励的信息量（reward informativeness）。消融实验证实，一个全面但固定的奖励系统在开放式任务中无法保持可靠，必须在训练全程中进行演化。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，在开放式强化学习任务中，奖励系统不应固定不变，而须随策略优化过程动态演化。EvoRS通过将奖励系统表示为可执行的Reward-DAG并由智能体设计者依据在线经验持续更新，有效维持了训练期间的奖励可靠性，在质量、抗奖励黑客和奖励信息量方面均优于固定奖励系统。这一框架为开放式任务中奖励设计提供了自进化范式的有力证据。

Abstract: Open-ended reinforcement learning often relies on rubric-based rewards for tasks without directly verifiable answers. Yet the policy and reward system form a dynamic feedback loop: as the policy optimizes the current reward, an initially useful reward system may become unreliable due to reward hacking or reduced response discriminability. The reward system should therefore evolve rather than remain fixed during training. Existing dynamic-rubric methods adapt evaluation criteria, but reward failures can also arise from scoring mechanisms or signal composition. We introduce EvoRS, a self-evolving RL framework that evolves the reward system from on-policy experience, representing it as an executable Reward-DAG. Specifically, an agentic designer updates this system from on-policy rollouts and reward traces to maintain train-time reliability. Across writing and roleplay, EvoRS achieves the best quality under all three judges, outperforming the policy by \(2.107\) and \(4.767\) points, respectively, while reducing reward hacking and coverage failures and preserving reward informativeness. Ablations confirm that a comprehensive fixed reward system cannot remain reliable in open-ended tasks and must evolve throughout training.

</details>


### [242] [Beyond Vector Similarity: Hierarchical Context-Aware Graph RAG vs Standard RAG in Enterprise Code Migration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12464)
*Nilesh Jaiswal,Aniket Agrawal,Arjit Shukla,Divya Malhotra,Saurabh Garg,Suchit Puri,Suddhasatwa Bhaumik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分层上下文驻留图（HCRG）方法，用于企业将遗留单体系统迁移至微服务过程中的LLM自动化代码翻译。该方法通过tree-sitter提取抽象语法树，将架构边映射到Google Cloud Spanner属性图，并序列化至Gemini上下文缓存，实现拓扑优先、父节点优先的代码翻译。与传统基于向量的标准RAG相比，HCRG显著降低了API幻觉率（56.4%→16.2%），提升了依赖解析质量（34.8%→65.9%）和父子一致性（26.7%→45.5%），但以降低圈复杂度一致性（71.6%→46.7%）和文档字符串保留（67.0%→61.0%）为代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业在将遗留单体系统现代化为微服务时大量依赖LLM进行自动化代码翻译，但传统基于向量的RAG无法捕获拓扑关系，检索到的孤立代码块会切断继承链，导致高编译失败率。同时，传统评价指标如CodeBLEU（两种方法均得91%）掩盖了标准RAG的结构性缺陷，因其仅基于文本重叠，无法反映代码结构上的错误。

Method: 提出分层上下文驻留图（HCRG）流水线：1）使用tree-sitter进行抽象语法树（AST）提取；2）将架构边映射到Google Cloud Spanner属性图；3）将该结构序列化到Gemini上下文缓存中，实现拓扑优先、父节点优先的代码翻译。同时将评估从朴素的文本重叠指标转向自定义的7指标软件工程框架。

Result: 实证表明Graph RAG有效缓解依赖丢失：API幻觉率从56.4%降至16.2%，依赖解析质量从34.8%提升至65.9%，父子一致性从26.7%上升至45.5%。但Graph RAG也存在权衡：密集的全局上下文导致LLM防御性过度设计，圈复杂度一致性从71.6%降至46.7%，文档字符串保留从67.0%轻微降至61.0%。传统CodeBLEU（两种方法均91%）掩盖了标准RAG的结构性失败。

Conclusion: 虽然Graph RAG以牺牲代码复杂度为代价换取更低的幻觉率，但它为企业自动化代码库现代化提供了一条更可行、架构上更合理的路径。评估应转向能够反映结构正确性的软件工程指标，而非仅依赖文本重叠指标。

Abstract: As enterprises modernize legacy monolithic systems to microservices, Large Language Models (LLMs) are heavily utilized for automated code translation. However, traditional vector-based Retrieval-Augmented Generation (Standard RAG) struggles to capture topological relationships. It fetches isolated chunks that sever inheritance chains, leading to high compilation failure rates. This paper introduces a Hierarchical Context-Resident Graph (HCRG) methodology to resolve these limitations. Our pipeline uses tree-sitter for Abstract Syntax Tree (AST) extraction, maps architectural edges into a Google Cloud Spanner Property Graph, and serializes this structure into a Gemini Context Cache for topological, parent-first code translation. We shift evaluation from naive text-overlap to a custom 7-metric Software Engineering framework. Traditional metrics like CodeBLEU (which scored 91% for both methods) effectively masked Standard RAG's structural failures behind syntactically plausible but broken code. Empirically, Graph RAG decisively mitigates dependency loss: API hallucination rates dropped from 56.4% to 16.2%, Dependency Resolution Quality improved from 34.8% to 65.9%, and Parent-Child Consistency rose from 26.7% to 45.5%. However, Graph RAG introduces specific trade-offs. The dense global context causes defensive over-engineering by the LLM, reducing Cyclomatic Complexity Consistency from 71.6% to 46.7%, and slightly degrades Docstring Preservation (67.0% to 61.0%). Ultimately, while trading code complexity for reduced hallucinations, Graph RAG provides a substantially more viable, architecturally sound path for automated enterprise codebase modernization.

</details>


### [243] [TripPattern: A Pattern-based Text Watermarking Method for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12472)
*Sangjun Moon,Dasom Choi,Jingun Kwon,Hidetaka Kamigaito,Taro Watanabe,Manabu Okumura*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TripPattern是一种基于三段词汇分区的文本水印框架，通过将词汇分为一个中性组和两个模式组，让模型在两个模式组间交替选择token以嵌入可检测模式，同时中性token保持独立选择以维持自然度。理论分析和四个多语言数据集的实验表明，该方法在保持LLM生成质量的同时实现稳健的水印可检测性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本水印方法通常将LLM词汇划分为绿色和红色token，但鼓励生成向绿色token偏移会降低文本质量和自然度。作者希望在不牺牲生成质量的前提下实现可靠的水印嵌入与检测。

Method: TripPattern将词汇划分为一个中性组和两个模式组（三段分区），将水印问题转化为基于模式的匹配任务。生成时模型在两个模式组间交替选择token以嵌入可检测模式，中性组token独立选择以提升灵活性和自然度。检测时采用基于模式的统计检验，通过测量相邻token在模式组间的交替频率来提供可解释的p值。

Result: 在四个多语言数据集上的实验表明，TripPattern能在保持LLM生成质量的同时实现稳健的水印可检测性，并且具备理论分析支撑。

Conclusion: TripPattern通过三组词汇分区和模式交替机制，在生成质量与水印可检测性之间取得了良好平衡，为文本水印提供了具有可解释统计检验的新方案。

Abstract: Text watermarking techniques have gained significant attention for identifying machine-generated text and mitigating risks from large language models (LLMs). Existing methods typically divide an LLM's vocabulary into green and red tokens, but encouraging generation toward green tokens can reduce text quality and naturalness. To address this, we propose TripPattern, a watermarking framework that formulates text watermarking as a pattern-based matching task using three vocabulary partitions. TripPattern divides the vocabulary into one neutral group and two pattern groups. During generation, the model alternates token selection between the two pattern groups to embed detectable patterns, while neutral tokens are selected independently to improve flexibility and preserve naturalness. For detection, TripPattern uses pattern-based statistical tests that provide interpretable p-values by measuring how often adjacent tokens alternate between the pattern groups. Theoretical analysis and empirical evaluations on four multilingual datasets show that TripPattern maintains LLM generation quality while achieving robust watermark detectability.

</details>


### [244] [When Does AI Augment Work? A Workflow-Level Framework for Human-Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12482)
*CIVIC-AI Collaboration,:,Jiaying Wu,Caleb Ziems,Raymond Chan,Nancy F. Chen,Corlyss Chua,Gerard Chung,Jungpil Hahn,Wee Sun Lee,Zhengyuan Liu,Jamie Ng,Desmond C. Ong,Jeryl Ong,Da Ren Soon,Tianqi Song,Zhi-Xuan Tan,Sixing Tao,Emily Yang,Yajing Yang,Stella Xin Yin,Min-Yen Kan,Diyi Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文旨在重新界定人工智能在工作场所中的价值衡量标准。作者指出，现有研究主要基于当前自动化能力和公众采用率来评估AI价值，但这忽略了人机协作对工作本质转型的更大影响。因此，他们提出应将分析视野从当前的原子化任务扩展到未来的整体工作流程。为支撑这一分析，论文建立了包含六个条件的AI增强（augmentation）精确定义：持久净价值、有意义的人类控制、问责与恢复机制，以及通过学习、职业路径和工作目的实现的长期人类发展。文章详细阐述了这些条件，并通过对AI中介社会调查的案例研究加以应用，最后概述了组织、研究人员和政府领导者如何利用该框架理解工作的未来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究在衡量AI在工作场所的价值时，过度依赖当前的自动化能力与公众采用率这两类指标。这类指标忽略了人机协作在从根本上改变工作性质方面的巨大影响，无法全面反映AI的长期价值，因此有必要扩展分析范围。

Method: 论文采用概念框架构建与案例研究相结合的方法。首先建立包含六个条件的AI增强精确定义，涵盖持久净价值、有意义的人类控制、问责与恢复，以及长期人类发展（学习、职业路径、工作目的）；随后通过AI中介社会调查的案例来具体阐释和应用该框架。

Result: 论文提出了一个六条件的AI增强框架，并通过AI中介社会调查案例展示了该框架的实际应用方式，验证了其可操作性。

Conclusion: 作者概述了组织、研究人员和政府领导者如何利用这一框架来理解与把握工作的未来，从而为AI评估提供了一种超越当前自动化指标的更全面视角。

Abstract: We aim to characterise the value of artificial intelligence in the workplace. Current studies largely measure this value in terms of the current automation capabilities and public adoption of AI. However, such metrics ignore the greater impacts of human--agent collaboration in transforming the nature of work. To account for this, we must expand the scope of our analysis beyond atomised tasks of today, and instead focus on how AI can augment entire workflows of the future. To ground this analysis, we establish a precise definition of AI augmentation comprising six conditions, spanning durable net value, meaningful human control, accountability and recovery, and long-term human development through learning, career pathways, and job purpose. We elaborate on these conditions and apply the framework in a case study of AI-mediated social surveys. We conclude by outlining how organisations, researchers, and government leaders can use this framework to make sense of the future of work.

</details>


### [245] [Confidence-Gated Transductive Test Generation for Code Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12489)
*Sungjae Lee,Youngsik Yoon,Seockbean Song,Siwei Wang,Wei Chen,Jungseul Ok*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为CoTT（置信度门控的转导式测试生成）的自适应方法：先用高效归纳式测试生成流程处理，仅在归纳置信度低时才调用转导式生成，从而在提升输出可靠性（测试用例质量）的同时，把额外计算资源投入到真正需要的输入上。在代码重排序基准上，CoTT相比先前基线在所有报告指标上表现更好，且比对所有输入一律应用转导式生成更省成本。结论是：基于置信度分配测试时计算，能在单一高效LLM下实现效率与效果的较好权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试用例合成分解对评估和排序LLM生成的程序至关重要，但高质量测试用例难以构造，因为可靠的预期输出往往难以获得；现有方法中，一味对每个输入都使用转导式生成虽然准确但成本高，而纯归纳式生成效率高但可靠性不足。作者希望设计一种在效率与可靠性之间自适应的策略。

Method: CoTT采用两阶段自适应框架：首先运行高效的归纳式（inductive）测试生成流程；只有当归纳置信度较低时，才额外调用转导式（transductive）生成。通过"置信度门控"（confidence gating）来决定是否分配额外测试时计算，从而在保证可靠性的同时避免不必要的计算开销。

Result: 在代码重排序基准上，CoTT在全部报告指标上超越先前基线，同时相比于对所有输入都应用转导式生成，显著降低了计算成本。结果表明置信度驱动的计算分配策略能带来更优的效率-效果权衡。

Conclusion: 置信度门控的测试时计算分配是一种可行且有效的策略：单个高效LLM即可同时取得更好的性能与更低的成本，说明在测试生成场景中，按需（selective）地投入转导式计算是优于"一刀切"全量计算的做法。

Abstract: Test case synthesis is crucial for evaluating and ranking programs generated by large language models (LLMs). However, constructing high-quality test cases remains challenging because reliable expected outputs are often difficult to obtain. We propose Confidence-Gated Transductive Test Generation (CoTT), which first uses an efficient inductive procedure and invokes transductive generation only when inductive confidence is low. This adaptive design improves output reliability while allocating extra computation only when needed. On code reranking benchmarks, CoTT outperforms prior baselines across the reported metrics while reducing cost relative to applying transductive generation to every input. These results show that confidence-based allocation of test-time computation provides a favorable efficiency-effectiveness trade-off with a single efficient LLM.

</details>


### [246] [Information Specialization and Constrained Synthesis in Multi-Agent LLM Forecasting: A Prospective Live-Study of the 2026 FIFA World Cup](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12495)
*Julian Varghese,Lucas Bickmann,Sarah Sandmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对2026年世界杯最后56场比赛进行前瞻性实时评估，在保持基础大语言模型不变的前提下，分配两个具有不同专业角色的预测智能体（定量统计专家与新闻信息专家），其预测经独立评论者审阅后由元智能体整合，形成四智能体序列模型。结果显示新闻专家获得了最高的均值概率加权Top-3效用，且在Top-3精确比分命中上与博彩市场持平；但两个专家在50/56场比赛中至少有两项比分一致，元智能体从未产生超过一个专家预测集合之外的比分，表明添加评论者与元智能体阶段并未产生互补信息或超越最强专家。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型正被组织为具有专业角色的多智能体系统，但专业化是否产生不同预测、后续综合是否提升效用尚不明确。作者希望通过实时前瞻性研究来检验专业化分工与多阶段综合的实际价值。

Method: 在信息密集的2026年FIFA世界杯最后56场比赛中进行活体前瞻性评估：保持一个前沿基础模型不变，分配两名具有对比专业角色的主要预测智能体——专注于结构化表现统计的定量专家和专注于伤情、战术、新闻发布会信息的新闻专家；二者的预测由独立的评论者审阅，再由元智能体整合，形成顺序四智能体模型，并以博彩市场预测作为外部基准。

Result: 新闻专家获得最高的均值概率加权Top-3效用，并在Top-3精确比分命中上与博彩市场持平。然而两个专家预测员在50/56场比赛中至少有两项比分一致，元智能体从未产生超过一个专家预测集合之外的比分。

Conclusion: 快速变化、非结构化的信息能够与结构化统计一同提供有价值的预测信号；而增加评论者和元智能体阶段未必能创造互补信息或超越最强专业智能体的表现。

Abstract: Large language models are being organized into multi-agent systems with specialized roles, but whether such specialization produces distinct forecasts and whether subsequent synthesis improves utility remains unclear. In this study, we carried out a live, prospective evaluation over the final 56 matches of the information-dense 2026 FIFA World Cup, keeping a frontier foundation model constant while assigning two primary forecasting agents contrasting specialist roles: a quantitative specialist focusing on structured performance statistics and a news specialist focusing on current injuries, tactics and information from press conferences. Their forecasts were then reviewed by a separate critic before being combined by a meta-agent, resulting in a sequential four-agent model. Forecasts from the betting market served as an external benchmark. The news specialist obtained the highest mean probability-weighted Top-3 utility and matched the betting market in Top-3 exact-score hits. Nevertheless, the two specialist forecasters agreed on at least two of the three scorelines in 50 out of 56 matches, and the meta-agent never generated more than one scoreline outside the specialists' forecast set. These findings show that rapidly changing, unstructured information can provide a valuable forecasting signal alongside structured statistics, whereas adding critic and meta-agent stages does not necessarily create complementary information or improve on the strongest specialist.

</details>


### [247] [From Collaboration to Capability: Internalizing Routed LLM Experts into Compact Reasoners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12578)
*Frank Nie,Shuyao Wang,Ethan B. Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RIVET框架，通过"专家增强强化学习"和"验证轨迹内化"两种方法，将紧凑控制器与更强专家的协作过程（决策、推理、代码、交互结构）内化到部署模型中。即使移除外部专家，模型仍能保持高水准：RIVET-1.7B和RIVET-4B在七个竞赛数学基准上分别达到28.25%和44.16%的平均准确率，Stage II在移除专家后将RIVET-4B准确率提升6.49个百分点，并在GPQA-Diamond上展现出向科学推理的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧凑控制器通过与更强专家协作能取得更好表现，但部署时依赖外部专家成本高、不可靠。本文的核心动机是研究如何利用专家的推理和代码来训练控制器，使其在移除外部专家后仍能内化并重现协作能力，从而在部署时无需外部LLM即可达到接近专家协作的效果。

Method: RIVET包含两大训练方法：1) 专家增强强化学习（expert-augmented RL）：将共享的结果信号同时应用于控制器决策与专家返回的文本片段，使两者都获得监督；2) 验证轨迹内化（verified trajectory internalization）：通过格式感知的监督训练，将完整的成功交互（推理、代码、交互结构）整合进模型。部署时控制器无需外部LLM，仅依赖本地Python执行生成推理、代码和交互结构。

Result: RIVET-1.7B和RIVET-4B在七个竞赛数学基准上平均准确率分别为28.25%和44.16%。Stage II（验证轨迹内化）在移除专家后使RIVET-4B准确率提升6.49个百分点。GPQA-Diamond结果证明向科学推理的泛化。消融实验表明：普通轨迹监督和额外格式权重均带来增益，印证了训练验证协作内容与结构的有效性。

Conclusion: RIVET证明了通过同时训练控制器的决策和专家的推理/代码内容，可以有效将外部专家协作能力内化到部署模型中，使紧凑模型在无外部LLM的情况下保持高水平表现。对验证协作的内容（轨迹）与结构（格式）进行训练的消融增益，验证了该方法框架的有效性与可泛化性。

Abstract: A compact controller can coordinate stronger experts by selecting whom to consult, formulating requests, and integrating their responses. We study whether learning from both the controller's decisions and the experts' reasoning and code improves its generation after expert removal.
